
==================================================
FILE: app\lib\core\database\local_database.dart

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

class LocalDatabase {
  static const String databaseName = 'wholesale_pos.db';
  static const int schemaVersion = 8;
  static const String deviceIdFileName = 'device_id.txt';
  static const String themeSettingKey = 'theme_mode';

  LocalDatabase._();

  static void _configureFactory() {
    if (Platform.isWindows || Platform.isLinux || Platform.isMacOS) {
      sqfliteFfiInit();
      databaseFactory = databaseFactoryFfi;
    }
  }

  static Future<Database> open() async {
    _configureFactory();
    final dir = await getApplicationSupportDirectory();
    return _openAt(p.join(dir.path, databaseName));
  }

  /// Opens a database at an explicit path. Used by tests that need to exercise
  /// migrations against a real file.
  static Future<Database> openAt(String path) async {
    _configureFactory();
    return _openAt(path);
  }

  static Future<Database> openInMemory() async {
    _configureFactory();
    return databaseFactory.openDatabase(
      inMemoryDatabasePath,
      options: OpenDatabaseOptions(
        version: schemaVersion,
        onConfigure: (db) async => await db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON'),
        onCreate: _onCreate,
        onUpgrade: _onUpgrade,
      ),
    );
  }

  static Future<Database> _openAt(String path) {
    return databaseFactory.openDatabase(
      path,
      options: OpenDatabaseOptions(
        version: schemaVersion,
        onConfigure: (db) async => await db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON'),
        onCreate: _onCreate,
        onUpgrade: _onUpgrade,
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Returns the stable ID of this installation. The ID is persisted next to
  /// the database so a restored backup can safely add the row back instead of
  /// attaching new sales to another machine's device record.
  static Future<String> registerCurrentDevice(Database db) async {
    if (db.path == inMemoryDatabasePath) {
      final existing = await db.query('devices', orderBy: 'rowid', limit: 1);
      if (existing.isNotEmpty) return existing.first['id']! as String;
      final id = const Uuid().v4();
      await _insertDeviceIfMissing(db, id);
      return id;
    }

    final stateFile = File(p.join(p.dirname(db.path), deviceIdFileName));
    String? id;
    try {
      if (await stateFile.exists()) {
        final saved = (await stateFile.readAsString()).trim();
        if (saved.isNotEmpty) id = saved;
      }
    } catch (_) {
      id = null;
    }
    if (id == null) {
      id = const Uuid().v4();
      await stateFile.writeAsString(id, flush: true);
    }
    await _insertDeviceIfMissing(db, id);
    return id;
  }

  static Future<void> _insertDeviceIfMissing(Database db, String id) async {
    final existing = await db.query(
      'devices',
      columns: ['id'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (existing.isNotEmpty) return;
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': id,
      'name': '${Platform.localHostname} Register',
      'platform': Platform.operatingSystem,
    });
  }

  static Future<String?> getSetting(Database db, String key) async {
    final rows = await db.query(
      'settings',
      columns: ['value'],
      where: 'key = ?',
      whereArgs: [key],
      limit: 1,
    );
    return rows.isEmpty ? null : rows.first['value'] as String?;
  }

  static Future<void> setSetting(Database db, String key, String value) async {
    await db.insert('settings', {
      'key': key,
      'value': value,
    }, conflictAlgorithm: ConflictAlgorithm.replace);
  }

  static Future<void> _onUpgrade(
    Database db,
    int oldVersion,
    int newVersion,
  ) async {
    if (oldVersion < 2) {
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE shifts (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          cashier_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          opening_cash_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
          closing_cash_minor_units INTEGER,
          expected_cash_minor_units INTEGER,
          status TEXT NOT NULL,
          opened_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          closed_at TEXT
        )
      ''');
    }
    if (oldVersion < 3) {
      await db.execute(
        "ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN payment_method TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CASH'",
      );
    }
    if (oldVersion < 4) {
      await db.execute(
        "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN salt TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
      );
      await db.execute("ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN shift_id TEXT");
      await db.execute("ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN void_reason TEXT");
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE cash_movements (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          type TEXT NOT NULL,
          amount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
          reason TEXT,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        )
      ''');
    }
    if (oldVersion < 5) {
      await db.execute('ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN voided_at TEXT');
      await db.execute('ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN voided_by TEXT');
      await db.execute('ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN voided_shift_id TEXT');
      await db.execute('ALTER TABLE cash_movements ADD COLUMN user_id TEXT');
      final openShifts = await db.rawQuery(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM shifts WHERE status = 'OPEN'",
      );
      if ((openShifts.first['count'] as int) <= 1) {
        await db.execute(
          "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX one_open_shift ON shifts(status) WHERE status = 'OPEN'",
        );
      }
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TRIGGER prevent_second_open_shift_insert
        BEFORE INSERT ON shifts WHEN NEW.status = 'OPEN'
        AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shifts WHERE status = 'OPEN')
        BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'A shift is already open'); END
      ''');
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TRIGGER prevent_second_open_shift_update
        BEFORE UPDATE OF status ON shifts WHEN NEW.status = 'OPEN'
        AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shifts WHERE status = 'OPEN' AND id != NEW.id)
        BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'A shift is already open'); END
      ''');
      await db.execute('CREATE INDEX sales_shift_id ON sales(shift_id)');
    }
    if (oldVersion < 6) {
      await _rebuildCashMovementsWithUserForeignKey(db);
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS counters (
          name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          value INTEGER NOT NULL
        )
      ''');
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
          key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          value TEXT NOT NULL
        )
      ''');
      await _createIndexesAndTriggers(db);
    }
    if (oldVersion < 7) {
      await _rebuildSaleItemsWithFlexibleQuantities(db);
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS finance_categories (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
        )
      ''');
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS finance_entries (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          category_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          source TEXT NOT NULL,
          amount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
          description TEXT,
          status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
          entry_date TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
          completed_at TEXT,
          completed_by TEXT,
          updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          FOREIGN KEY(category_id) REFERENCES finance_categories(id),
          FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES users(id),
          FOREIGN KEY(completed_by) REFERENCES users(id)
        )
      ''');
      await _createIndexesAndTriggers(db);
    }
    if (oldVersion < 8) {
      await db.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS void_requests (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          sale_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          requested_by TEXT NOT NULL,
          reason TEXT NOT NULL,
          status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          decided_by TEXT,
          decided_at TEXT,
          FOREIGN KEY(sale_id) REFERENCES sales(id),
          FOREIGN KEY(requested_by) REFERENCES users(id),
          FOREIGN KEY(decided_by) REFERENCES users(id)
        )
      ''');
      await _createIndexesAndTriggers(db);
    }
  }

  /// Schema 6 stored quantities as plain integers. Schema 7 keeps the exact
  /// text the cashier typed ("2 boxes and 1 piece") plus a separate numeric
  /// billing quantity in milliunits, so descriptive entries are never forced
  /// into a number. Existing rows keep their values: the integer quantity is
  /// copied into both new columns.
  static Future<void> _rebuildSaleItemsWithFlexibleQuantities(
    Database db,
  ) async {
    final columns = await db.rawQuery('PRAGMA table_info(sale_items)');
    if (columns.any((column) => column['name'] == 'quantity_text')) return;

    await db.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE sale_items_v7 (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        sale_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        product_name_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
        unit_price_snapshot_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        quantity_text TEXT NOT NULL,
        billing_quantity_milliunits INTEGER,
        line_discount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        line_total_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        notes TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY(sale_id) REFERENCES sales(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
      )
    ''');
    await db.execute('''
      INSERT INTO sale_items_v7
        (id, sale_id, product_id, product_name_snapshot,
         unit_price_snapshot_minor_units, quantity_text,
         billing_quantity_milliunits, line_discount_minor_units,
         line_total_minor_units, notes)
      SELECT id, sale_id, product_id, product_name_snapshot,
         unit_price_snapshot_minor_units, CAST(quantity AS TEXT),
         quantity * 1000, line_discount_minor_units,
         line_total_minor_units, NULL
      FROM sale_items
    ''');
    await db.execute('DROP TABLE sale_items');
    await db.execute('ALTER TABLE sale_items_v7 RENAME TO sale_items');
  }

  /// Schema 5 added `user_id` with ALTER TABLE, which SQLite cannot attach a
  /// foreign key to. Rebuild the table once so upgraded databases match fresh
  /// installs.
  static Future<void> _rebuildCashMovementsWithUserForeignKey(
    Database db,
  ) async {
    final foreignKeys = await db.rawQuery(
      'PRAGMA foreign_key_list(cash_movements)',
    );
    if (foreignKeys.any((row) => row['table'] == 'users')) return;

    await db.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE cash_movements_v6 (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        type TEXT NOT NULL,
        amount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        reason TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        user_id TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY(shift_id) REFERENCES shifts(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
      )
    ''');
    await db.execute('''
      INSERT INTO cash_movements_v6
        (id, shift_id, type, amount_minor_units, reason, created_at, user_id)
      SELECT id, shift_id, type, amount_minor_units, reason, created_at, user_id
      FROM cash_movements
    ''');
    await db.execute('DROP TABLE cash_movements');
    await db.execute('ALTER TABLE cash_movements_v6 RENAME TO cash_movements');
  }

  static Future<void> _createIndexesAndTriggers(DatabaseExecutor db) async {
    final openShifts = await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM shifts WHERE status = 'OPEN'",
    );
    if ((openShifts.first['count'] as int) <= 1) {
      await db.execute(
        "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS one_open_shift ON shifts(status) WHERE status = 'OPEN'",
      );
    }
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS sales_shift_id ON sales(shift_id)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS sales_created_at ON sales(created_at)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS sale_items_sale_id ON sale_items(sale_id)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs_created_at ON audit_logs(created_at)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS cash_movements_shift_id ON cash_movements(shift_id)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS finance_entries_category_id '
      'ON finance_entries(category_id)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS finance_entries_status '
      'ON finance_entries(status)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS finance_entries_entry_date '
      'ON finance_entries(entry_date)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS void_requests_sale_id '
      'ON void_requests(sale_id)',
    );
    await db.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS void_requests_status '
      'ON void_requests(status)',
    );
    await db.execute('''
      CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS prevent_second_open_shift_insert
      BEFORE INSERT ON shifts WHEN NEW.status = 'OPEN'
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shifts WHERE status = 'OPEN')
      BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'A shift is already open'); END
    ''');
    await db.execute('''
      CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS prevent_second_open_shift_update
      BEFORE UPDATE OF status ON shifts WHEN NEW.status = 'OPEN'
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shifts WHERE status = 'OPEN' AND id != NEW.id)
      BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'A shift is already open'); END
    ''');
  }

  static Future<void> _onCreate(Database db, int version) async {
    final batch = db.batch();

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE users (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        salt TEXT NOT NULL,
        role TEXT NOT NULL,
        is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE products (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        code TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        category TEXT,
        unit TEXT NOT NULL,
        selling_price_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE devices (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        platform TEXT NOT NULL,
        license_key TEXT,
        last_sync_at TEXT
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE shifts (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        cashier_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        opening_cash_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        closing_cash_minor_units INTEGER,
        expected_cash_minor_units INTEGER,
        status TEXT NOT NULL,
        opened_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        closed_at TEXT
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE cash_movements (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        type TEXT NOT NULL,
        amount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        reason TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        user_id TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY(shift_id) REFERENCES shifts(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE sales (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        receipt_number TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        cashier_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        device_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        customer_name TEXT,
        payment_method TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CASH',
        subtotal_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        discount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        total_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        amount_paid_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        change_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        status TEXT NOT NULL,
        void_reason TEXT,
        voided_at TEXT,
        voided_by TEXT,
        voided_shift_id TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        synced_at TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY(cashier_id) REFERENCES users(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(device_id) REFERENCES devices(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(shift_id) REFERENCES shifts(id)
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE sale_items (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        sale_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        product_name_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
        unit_price_snapshot_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        quantity_text TEXT NOT NULL,
        billing_quantity_milliunits INTEGER,
        line_discount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        line_total_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        notes TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY(sale_id) REFERENCES sales(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE finance_categories (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE finance_entries (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        category_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        source TEXT NOT NULL,
        amount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        description TEXT,
        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
        entry_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
        completed_at TEXT,
        completed_by TEXT,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        FOREIGN KEY(category_id) REFERENCES finance_categories(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES users(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(completed_by) REFERENCES users(id)
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE void_requests (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        sale_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        requested_by TEXT NOT NULL,
        reason TEXT NOT NULL,
        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        decided_by TEXT,
        decided_at TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY(sale_id) REFERENCES sales(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(requested_by) REFERENCES users(id),
        FOREIGN KEY(decided_by) REFERENCES users(id)
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        user_id TEXT,
        action TEXT NOT NULL,
        details TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE counters (
        name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        value INTEGER NOT NULL
      )
    ''');

    batch.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE settings (
        key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        value TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    ''');

    await batch.commit(noResult: true);
    await _createIndexesAndTriggers(db);
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\core\licensing\license_validator.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:cryptography/cryptography.dart';

class LicenseValidator {
  /// Injected at build time, e.g.
  /// `flutter build windows --dart-define=WHOLESALE_POS_LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY=<hex>`.
  static const String _publicKeyHex = String.fromEnvironment(
    'WHOLESALE_POS_LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY',
  );

  /// Explicit, build-time escape hatch for development machines that do not
  /// have a signing key yet. Never enable this in a shipped build.
  static const bool _allowUnlicensedDev = bool.fromEnvironment(
    'WHOLESALE_POS_DEV_UNLICENSED',
  );

  static Future<String> getHardwareId() async {
    try {
      if (Platform.isWindows) {
        final result = await Process.run('powershell', [
          '-Command',
          '(Get-CimInstance Win32_ComputerSystemProduct).UUID',
        ]).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
        final uuid = result.stdout.toString().trim();
        if (result.exitCode == 0 && uuid.isNotEmpty) {
          return uuid;
        }
      }
      return Platform.localHostname;
    } catch (_) {
      return Platform.localHostname;
    }
  }

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> validateLicense({
    String? hardwareId,
    DateTime? now,
    String? verificationKeyHex,
  }) async {
    if (verificationKeyHex == null &&
        _allowUnlicensedDev &&
        _publicKeyHex.isEmpty) {
      return {
        'format': 'wholesale-pos-license',
        'version': 1,
        'product': 'wholesale_pos',
        'entitlements': const ['pos'],
        'issued_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        'expires_at': DateTime.now()
            .add(const Duration(days: 30))
            .toIso8601String(),
        'dev_license': true,
      };
    }

    try {
      var file = File('license.key');
      if (!await file.exists()) {
        final exeDir = File(Platform.resolvedExecutable).parent.path;
        file = File('$exeDir/license.key');
        if (!await file.exists()) {
          return {'error': 'license.key not found.'};
        }
      }

      final parts = (await file.readAsString()).trim().split('.');
      if (parts.length != 2) {
        return {'error': 'Invalid license format.'};
      }
      final payloadBytes = base64Url.decode(base64Url.normalize(parts[0]));
      final signatureBytes = base64Url.decode(base64Url.normalize(parts[1]));
      if (signatureBytes.length != 64) {
        return {'error': 'Invalid license format.'};
      }

      final decoded = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(payloadBytes));
      if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
        return {'error': 'Invalid license format.'};
      }
      final payload = decoded;

      final required = [
        'format',
        'version',
        'product',
        'entitlements',
        'issued_at',
        'expires_at',
      ];
      if (required.any((key) => !payload.containsKey(key)) ||
          payload['format'] != 'wholesale-pos-license' ||
          payload['version'] != 1 ||
          payload['product'] != 'wholesale_pos' ||
          payload['entitlements'] is! List ||
          (payload['entitlements'] as List).any((item) => item is! String) ||
          payload['issued_at'] is! String ||
          payload['expires_at'] is! String ||
          (payload.containsKey('hardware_id') &&
              payload['hardware_id'] is! String)) {
        return {'error': 'License is missing required fields.'};
      }

      final issuedAt = DateTime.tryParse(payload['issued_at'] as String);
      final expiresAt = DateTime.tryParse(payload['expires_at'] as String);
      if (issuedAt == null ||
          expiresAt == null ||
          !expiresAt.isAfter(issuedAt)) {
        return {'error': 'Invalid license dates.'};
      }

      final canonicalBytes = utf8.encode(jsonEncode(_canonicalize(payload)));
      if (!_sameBytes(payloadBytes, canonicalBytes)) {
        return {'error': 'Invalid license format.'};
      }

      final keyHex = verificationKeyHex ?? _publicKeyHex;
      if (keyHex.length != 64 ||
          !RegExp(r'^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$').hasMatch(keyHex)) {
        return {
          'error':
              'License verification key is not configured. Build with '
              '--dart-define=WHOLESALE_POS_LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY=<hex>.',
        };
      }

      final publicKey = SimplePublicKey(
        _hexToBytes(keyHex),
        type: KeyPairType.ed25519,
      );
      final validSignature = await Ed25519().verify(
        payloadBytes,
        signature: Signature(signatureBytes, publicKey: publicKey),
      );
      if (!validSignature) {
        return {'error': 'Invalid license signature.'};
      }

      final expectedHardwareId = hardwareId ?? await getHardwareId();
      if (payload['hardware_id'] != null &&
          payload['hardware_id'] != expectedHardwareId) {
        return {'error': 'License is for a different device.'};
      }
      final nowValue = now ?? DateTime.now();
      if (issuedAt.isAfter(nowValue.add(const Duration(days: 1)))) {
        return {'error': 'System clock is set before the license issue date.'};
      }
      if (!nowValue.isBefore(expiresAt)) {
        return {'error': 'License has expired.'};
      }
      return payload;
    } catch (_) {
      return {'error': 'Invalid license format.'};
    }
  }

  static Object? _canonicalize(Object? value) {
    if (value is Map) {
      final keys = value.keys.cast<String>().toList()..sort();
      return {for (final key in keys) key: _canonicalize(value[key])};
    }
    if (value is List) return value.map(_canonicalize).toList();
    return value;
  }

  static List<int> _hexToBytes(String hex) => [
    for (var i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2)
      int.parse(hex.substring(i, i + 2), radix: 16),
  ];

  static bool _sameBytes(List<int> left, List<int> right) {
    if (left.length != right.length) return false;
    var difference = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < left.length; i++) {
      difference |= left[i] ^ right[i];
    }
    return difference == 0;
  }
}

/// A person (or category) in the owner's private money-tracking area.
/// Renaming keeps the same row so historical entries stay attached.
class FinanceCategory {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  FinanceCategory({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory FinanceCategory.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return FinanceCategory(
      id: map['id'] as String,
      name: map['name'] as String,
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(map['created_at'] as String),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(map['updated_at'] as String),
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\core\models\finance_entry.dart
==================================================

/// One manually recorded money activity in the owner's private area, e.g.
/// "taken from Bank X". Completely separate from sales: creating or
/// completing an entry never touches sales or revenue totals.
class FinanceEntry {
  static const String statusPending = 'PENDING';
  static const String statusCompleted = 'COMPLETED';

  final String id;
  final String categoryId;
  final String source;
  final int amountMinorUnits;
  final String? description;
  final String status;
  final DateTime entryDate;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final String createdBy;
  final DateTime? completedAt;
  final String? completedBy;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  /// Person name hydrated from the category for display only.
  final String? categoryName;

  FinanceEntry({
    required this.id,
    required this.categoryId,
    required this.source,
    required this.amountMinorUnits,
    this.description,
    required this.status,
    required this.entryDate,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.createdBy,
    this.completedAt,
    this.completedBy,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.categoryName,
  });

  bool get isCompleted => status == statusCompleted;

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'category_id': categoryId,
      'source': source,
      'amount_minor_units': amountMinorUnits,
      'description': description,
      'status': status,
      'entry_date': entryDate.toIso8601String(),
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'created_by': createdBy,
      'completed_at': completedAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'completed_by': completedBy,
      'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory FinanceEntry.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return FinanceEntry(
      id: map['id'] as String,
      categoryId: map['category_id'] as String,
      source: map['source'] as String,
      amountMinorUnits: map['amount_minor_units'] as int,
      description: map['description'] as String?,
      status: map['status'] as String,
      entryDate: DateTime.parse(map['entry_date'] as String),
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(map['created_at'] as String),
      createdBy: map['created_by'] as String,
      completedAt: map['completed_at'] == null
          ? null
          : DateTime.parse(map['completed_at'] as String),
      completedBy: map['completed_by'] as String?,
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(map['updated_at'] as String),
      categoryName: map['category_name'] as String?,
    );
  }
}

class Product {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String code;
  final String? category;
  final String unit;
  final int sellingPriceMinorUnits;
  final bool isActive;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  Product({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.code,
    this.category,
    required this.unit,
    required this.sellingPriceMinorUnits,
    this.isActive = true,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'code': code,
      'category': category,
      'unit': unit,
      'selling_price_minor_units': sellingPriceMinorUnits,
      'is_active': isActive ? 1 : 0,
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory Product.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return Product(
      id: map['id'] as String,
      name: map['name'] as String,
      code: map['code'] as String,
      category: map['category'] as String?,
      unit: map['unit'] as String,
      sellingPriceMinorUnits: map['selling_price_minor_units'] as int,
      isActive: (map['is_active'] as int) == 1,
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(map['created_at'] as String),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(map['updated_at'] as String),
    );
  }
}

class User {
  final String id;
  final String username;
  final String passwordHash;
  final String salt;
  final String role;
  final bool isActive;

  User({
    required this.id,
    required this.username,
    required this.passwordHash,
    required this.salt,
    required this.role,
    this.isActive = true,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'username': username,
      'password_hash': passwordHash,
      'salt': salt,
      'role': role,
      'is_active': isActive ? 1 : 0,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory User.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return User(
      id: map['id'] as String,
      username: map['username'] as String,
      passwordHash: map['password_hash'] as String,
      salt: map['salt'] as String,
      role: map['role'] as String,
      isActive: (map['is_active'] as int) == 1,
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\core\repositories\finance_repository.dart
==================================================

import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../models/finance_category.dart';
import '../models/finance_entry.dart';
import '../utils/date_range.dart';
import '../utils/money_utils.dart';
import 'sale_repository.dart';

/// Data access for the owner's private money-tracking area. Every method —
/// reads included — verifies an active OWNER inside the query or transaction,
/// so cashiers are blocked at the data layer, not just by hidden navigation.
/// Financial entries are fully separate from sales and never affect revenue.
class FinanceRepository {
  final Database _db;

  FinanceRepository(this._db);

  static const int _maxNameLength = 80;
  static const int _maxSourceLength = 120;
  static const int _maxDescriptionLength = 500;

  Future<void> _requireActiveOwner(
    DatabaseExecutor executor,
    String userId,
  ) async {
    final rows = await executor.query(
      'users',
      columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [userId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (rows.isEmpty ||
        rows.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
        rows.first['is_active'] != 1) {
      throw Exception('Only an active owner can access financial records.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _audit(
    DatabaseExecutor executor,
    String userId,
    String action,
    String details,
  ) async {
    try {
      await executor.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': userId,
        'action': action,
        'details': details,
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    } catch (_) {
      // Auditing must never block financial record keeping.
    }
  }

  String _validatedName(String name) {
    final trimmed = name.trim();
    if (trimmed.isEmpty) {
      throw Exception('A name is required.');
    }
    if (trimmed.length > _maxNameLength) {
      throw Exception('Name must be $_maxNameLength characters or fewer.');
    }
    return trimmed;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Categories (people)
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<List<FinanceCategory>> getCategories(String ownerId) async {
    await _requireActiveOwner(_db, ownerId);
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'finance_categories',
      orderBy: 'name COLLATE NOCASE ASC',
    );
    return maps.map(FinanceCategory.fromMap).toList();
  }

  /// Categories with pending entry counts and pending totals, for the
  /// overview list.
  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getCategorySummaries(
    String ownerId,
  ) async {
    await _requireActiveOwner(_db, ownerId);
    return _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT finance_categories.id,
             finance_categories.name,
             COUNT(finance_entries.id) AS pending_count,
             COALESCE(SUM(finance_entries.amount_minor_units), 0) AS pending_total
      FROM finance_categories
      LEFT JOIN finance_entries
        ON finance_entries.category_id = finance_categories.id
       AND finance_entries.status = 'PENDING'
      GROUP BY finance_categories.id, finance_categories.name
      ORDER BY finance_categories.name COLLATE NOCASE ASC
    ''');
  }

  Future<FinanceCategory> createCategory({
    required String ownerId,
    required String name,
  }) async {
    final trimmed = _validatedName(name);
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final category = FinanceCategory(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      name: trimmed,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    );

    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      await _requireActiveOwner(txn, ownerId);
      final existing = await txn.query(
        'finance_categories',
        columns: ['id'],
        where: 'LOWER(name) = LOWER(?)',
        whereArgs: [trimmed],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (existing.isNotEmpty) {
        throw Exception('A person named "$trimmed" already exists.');
      }
      await txn.insert('finance_categories', category.toMap());
      await _audit(
        txn,
        ownerId,
        'FINANCE_CATEGORY_CREATED',
        'Person "$trimmed" added.',
      );
    });
    return category;
  }

  /// Renames a person. Entries stay attached to the same row, so history is
  /// preserved and remains searchable under the new name.
  Future<void> renameCategory({
    required String ownerId,
    required String categoryId,
    required String newName,
  }) async {
    final trimmed = _validatedName(newName);
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      await _requireActiveOwner(txn, ownerId);
      final existing = await txn.query(
        'finance_categories',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [categoryId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (existing.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Person not found.');
      }
      final duplicate = await txn.query(
        'finance_categories',
        columns: ['id'],
        where: 'LOWER(name) = LOWER(?) AND id != ?',
        whereArgs: [trimmed, categoryId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (duplicate.isNotEmpty) {
        throw Exception('A person named "$trimmed" already exists.');
      }
      final oldName = existing.first['name'] as String;
      await txn.update(
        'finance_categories',
        {'name': trimmed, 'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String()},
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [categoryId],
      );
      await _audit(
        txn,
        ownerId,
        'FINANCE_CATEGORY_RENAMED',
        'Person "$oldName" renamed to "$trimmed".',
      );
    });
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Entries
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  String _validatedSource(String source) {
    final trimmed = source.trim();
    if (trimmed.isEmpty) {
      throw Exception('A source is required (e.g., a bank name).');
    }
    if (trimmed.length > _maxSourceLength) {
      throw Exception('Source must be $_maxSourceLength characters or fewer.');
    }
    return trimmed;
  }

  Future<FinanceCategory> _requireCategory(
    DatabaseExecutor executor,
    String categoryId,
  ) async {
    final maps = await executor.query(
      'finance_categories',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [categoryId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (maps.isEmpty) throw Exception('Person not found.');
    return FinanceCategory.fromMap(maps.first);
  }

  Future<FinanceEntry> addEntry({
    required String ownerId,
    required String categoryId,
    required String source,
    required int amountMinorUnits,
    String? description,
    DateTime? entryDate,
  }) async {
    final validatedSource = _validatedSource(source);
    if (amountMinorUnits <= 0) {
      throw Exception('The amount must be greater than zero.');
    }
    final trimmedDescription = description?.trim();
    if (trimmedDescription != null &&
        trimmedDescription.length > _maxDescriptionLength) {
      throw Exception(
        'Description must be $_maxDescriptionLength characters or fewer.',
      );
    }

    final now = DateTime.now();
    final entry = FinanceEntry(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      categoryId: categoryId,
      source: validatedSource,
      amountMinorUnits: amountMinorUnits,
      description: trimmedDescription == null || trimmedDescription.isEmpty
          ? null
          : trimmedDescription,
      status: FinanceEntry.statusPending,
      entryDate: entryDate ?? now,
      createdAt: now,
      createdBy: ownerId,
      updatedAt: now,
    );

    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      await _requireActiveOwner(txn, ownerId);
      final category = await _requireCategory(txn, categoryId);
      await txn.insert('finance_entries', entry.toMap());
      await _audit(
        txn,
        ownerId,
        'FINANCE_ENTRY_ADDED',
        'Person "${category.name}": $validatedSource '
            '${MoneyUtils.formatBr(amountMinorUnits)} (pending).',
      );
    });
    return entry;
  }

  /// Edits a still-pending entry. Completed entries are immutable so the
  /// completion record stays a reliable audit trail.
  Future<void> updateEntry({
    required String ownerId,
    required String entryId,
    required String source,
    required int amountMinorUnits,
    String? description,
    DateTime? entryDate,
  }) async {
    final validatedSource = _validatedSource(source);
    if (amountMinorUnits <= 0) {
      throw Exception('The amount must be greater than zero.');
    }
    final trimmedDescription = description?.trim();

    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      await _requireActiveOwner(txn, ownerId);
      final maps = await txn.query(
        'finance_entries',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [entryId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (maps.isEmpty) throw Exception('Entry not found.');
      if (maps.first['status'] != FinanceEntry.statusPending) {
        throw Exception('Only pending entries can be edited.');
      }
      final entry = FinanceEntry.fromMap(maps.first);
      final category = await _requireCategory(txn, entry.categoryId);

      await txn.update(
        'finance_entries',
        {
          'source': validatedSource,
          'amount_minor_units': amountMinorUnits,
          'description':
              trimmedDescription == null || trimmedDescription.isEmpty
              ? null
              : trimmedDescription,
          'entry_date': (entryDate ?? entry.entryDate).toIso8601String(),
          'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        },
        where: 'id = ? AND status = ?',
        whereArgs: [entryId, FinanceEntry.statusPending],
      );
      await _audit(
        txn,
        ownerId,
        'FINANCE_ENTRY_UPDATED',
        'Pending entry for "${category.name}" edited: '
            '$validatedSource ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(amountMinorUnits)}.',
      );
    });
  }

  /// Marks an entry as taken/completed. The entry leaves the pending list but
  /// stays stored permanently, searchable, and viewable in history.
  Future<void> markCompleted({
    required String ownerId,
    required String entryId,
  }) async {
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      await _requireActiveOwner(txn, ownerId);
      final maps = await txn.query(
        'finance_entries',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [entryId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (maps.isEmpty) throw Exception('Entry not found.');
      if (maps.first['status'] != FinanceEntry.statusPending) {
        throw Exception('Entry is already completed.');
      }
      final entry = FinanceEntry.fromMap(maps.first);
      final category = await _requireCategory(txn, entry.categoryId);

      final now = DateTime.now();
      final updated = await txn.update(
        'finance_entries',
        {
          'status': FinanceEntry.statusCompleted,
          'completed_at': now.toIso8601String(),
          'completed_by': ownerId,
          'updated_at': now.toIso8601String(),
        },
        where: 'id = ? AND status = ?',
        whereArgs: [entryId, FinanceEntry.statusPending],
      );
      if (updated != 1) {
        throw Exception('Entry is no longer pending.');
      }
      await _audit(
        txn,
        ownerId,
        'FINANCE_ENTRY_COMPLETED',
        'Person "${category.name}": ${entry.source} '
            '${MoneyUtils.formatBr(entry.amountMinorUnits)} marked taken.',
      );
    });
  }

  /// Searches financial history. Matches person name, source, and
  /// description; filters by status and an optional entry-date range.
  Future<List<FinanceEntry>> searchEntries({
    required String ownerId,
    String? categoryId,
    String? searchQuery,
    String? status,
    DateRange? dateRange,
  }) async {
    await _requireActiveOwner(_db, ownerId);

    final where = <String>[];
    final args = <dynamic>[];

    if (categoryId != null && categoryId.isNotEmpty) {
      where.add('finance_entries.category_id = ?');
      args.add(categoryId);
    }
    if (status != null &&
        status.isNotEmpty &&
        status != 'ALL' &&
        status != FinanceEntry.statusPending &&
        status != FinanceEntry.statusCompleted) {
      throw Exception('Invalid status filter.');
    }
    if (status != null && status.isNotEmpty && status != 'ALL') {
      where.add('finance_entries.status = ?');
      args.add(status);
    }
    if (dateRange != null) {
      where.add(
        'finance_entries.entry_date >= ? AND finance_entries.entry_date < ?',
      );
      args.add(dateRange.start.toIso8601String());
      args.add(dateRange.end.toIso8601String());
    }

    final query = searchQuery?.trim();
    if (query != null && query.isNotEmpty) {
      final pattern = '%${SaleRepository.escapeLike(query.toLowerCase())}%';
      const escape = r" ESCAPE '\'";
      where.add('''
        (LOWER(finance_entries.source) LIKE ?$escape
         OR LOWER(COALESCE(finance_entries.description, '')) LIKE ?$escape
         OR LOWER(finance_categories.name) LIKE ?$escape)
      ''');
      args.addAll(List.filled(3, pattern));
    }

    final sql = StringBuffer('''
      SELECT finance_entries.*, finance_categories.name AS category_name
      FROM finance_entries
      JOIN finance_categories
        ON finance_entries.category_id = finance_categories.id
    ''');
    if (where.isNotEmpty) sql.write(' WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}');
    sql.write(
      ' ORDER BY finance_entries.entry_date DESC, finance_entries.created_at DESC',
    );

    final maps = await _db.rawQuery(sql.toString(), args);
    return maps.map(FinanceEntry.fromMap).toList();
  }

  /// Pending and completed totals for one person, used on the person page.
  Future<Map<String, int>> getPersonTotals(
    String ownerId,
    String categoryId,
  ) async {
    await _requireActiveOwner(_db, ownerId);
    final rows = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'PENDING' THEN amount_minor_units ELSE 0 END), 0) AS pending_total,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'COMPLETED' THEN amount_minor_units ELSE 0 END), 0) AS completed_total
      FROM finance_entries
      WHERE category_id = ?
    ''',
      [categoryId],
    );
    final row = rows.first;
    return {
      'pending_total': row['pending_total'] as int,
      'completed_total': row['completed_total'] as int,
    };
  }
}

import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

import '../models/product.dart';

class ProductRepository {
  final Database _db;

  ProductRepository(this._db);

  Future<void> save(Product product) async {
    await _db.insert('products', product.toMap());
  }

  /// Updates name, default price and active flag. Used by the Saved Names
  /// screen for rename, price edits and deactivate/reactivate.
  Future<void> update(Product product) async {
    await _db.update(
      'products',
      product.toMap(),
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [product.id],
    );
  }

  Future<void> deactivate(String id) async {
    await _db.update(
      'products',
      {'is_active': 0, 'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String()},
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );
  }

  Future<void> reactivate(String id) async {
    await _db.update(
      'products',
      {'is_active': 1, 'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String()},
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );
  }

  Future<Product?> getById(String id) async {
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'products',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
      limit: 1,
    );

    if (maps.isEmpty) return null;
    return Product.fromMap(maps.first);
  }

  Future<List<Product>> getAllActive() async {
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'products',
      where: 'is_active = ?',
      whereArgs: [1],
      orderBy: 'name ASC',
    );

    return maps.map((map) => Product.fromMap(map)).toList();
  }

  /// Deactivated names are kept for history; the Saved Names screen can
  /// bring them back instead of recreating them.
  Future<List<Product>> getAllInactive() async {
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'products',
      where: 'is_active = ?',
      whereArgs: [0],
      orderBy: 'name ASC',
    );

    return maps.map((map) => Product.fromMap(map)).toList();
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\core\repositories\sale_repository.dart
==================================================

import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../features/sales/sale.dart';
import '../../features/sales/sale_item.dart';
import '../../features/sales/shift.dart';
import '../../features/sales/void_request.dart';
import '../utils/date_range.dart';
import '../utils/money_utils.dart';
import 'shift_repository.dart';

class SaleRepository {
  final Database _db;

  SaleRepository(this._db);

  /// Escapes LIKE wildcards so user search text matches literally.
  static String escapeLike(String value) {
    return value
        .replaceAll('\\', '\\\\')
        .replaceAll('%', '\\%')
        .replaceAll('_', '\\_');
  }

  Future<void> saveSale(Sale sale) async {
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final shifts = await txn.query(
        'shifts',
        columns: ['id'],
        where: "id = ? AND status = 'OPEN'",
        whereArgs: [sale.shiftId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (shifts.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Sale requires an open register shift.');
      }
      if (sale.receiptNumber.trim().isEmpty ||
          sale.items.isEmpty ||
          sale.totalMinorUnits < 0 ||
          sale.subtotalMinorUnits < 0 ||
          sale.discountMinorUnits < 0 ||
          sale.totalMinorUnits !=
              sale.subtotalMinorUnits - sale.discountMinorUnits ||
          !{'CASH', 'BANK'}.contains(sale.paymentMethod)) {
        throw Exception('Sale contains invalid totals or payment method.');
      }
      var itemSubtotal = 0;
      for (final item in sale.items) {
        _validateLineItem(item);
        itemSubtotal += item.lineTotalMinorUnits;
      }
      if (itemSubtotal != sale.subtotalMinorUnits ||
          (sale.paymentMethod == 'CASH' &&
              (sale.amountPaidMinorUnits < sale.totalMinorUnits ||
                  sale.changeMinorUnits !=
                      sale.amountPaidMinorUnits - sale.totalMinorUnits)) ||
          (sale.paymentMethod == 'BANK' &&
              (sale.amountPaidMinorUnits != sale.totalMinorUnits ||
                  sale.changeMinorUnits != 0))) {
        throw Exception('Sale totals do not match the line items or payment.');
      }
      await txn.insert('sales', sale.toMap());

      for (final item in sale.items) {
        await txn.insert('sale_items', item.toMap());
      }

      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': sale.cashierId,
        'action': 'SALE_COMPLETED',
        'details':
            'Total: ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(sale.totalMinorUnits)}, '
            'Lines: ${sale.items.length}',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  /// A line item is valid when either
  /// (a) it has a numeric billing quantity and its total is exactly
  ///     price x quantity minus the line discount, or
  /// (b) it has no billing quantity and the total was entered manually,
  ///     in which case the quantity is only a description.
  /// Descriptive quantities are never interpreted as numbers.
  static void _validateLineItem(SaleItem item) {
    if (item.productNameSnapshot.trim().isEmpty ||
        item.quantityText.trim().isEmpty ||
        item.unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits < 0 ||
        item.lineDiscountMinorUnits < 0 ||
        item.lineTotalMinorUnits < 0) {
      throw Exception('Sale contains an invalid line item.');
    }

    final milliunits = item.billingQuantityMilliunits;
    if (milliunits != null) {
      if (milliunits < 0) {
        throw Exception('Sale contains an invalid line item.');
      }
      final base = MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(
        item.unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits,
        milliunits,
      );
      if (base == null) {
        throw Exception('A line item total is too large.');
      }
      if (item.lineDiscountMinorUnits > base ||
          item.lineTotalMinorUnits != base - item.lineDiscountMinorUnits) {
        throw Exception(
          'A line total does not match its unit price and quantity.',
        );
      }
    } else if (item.lineTotalMinorUnits == 0) {
      // Manual totals must be positive; a zero line adds nothing and is
      // almost certainly a mistake.
      throw Exception('A manually entered line amount is required.');
    }
  }

  /// Reserves the next sequential receipt number for today, e.g.
  /// `REC-20261021-0007`. Numbers are allocated in a transaction so a retail
  /// audit can follow them in order. They are internal identifiers only and
  /// are never shown in the sales-history interface.
  Future<String> nextReceiptNumber() async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final day =
        '${now.year}'
        '${now.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        '${now.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
    final key = 'receipt-$day';

    final next = await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final rows = await txn.query(
        'counters',
        columns: ['value'],
        where: 'name = ?',
        whereArgs: [key],
        limit: 1,
      );
      final value = rows.isEmpty ? 1 : (rows.first['value'] as int) + 1;
      await txn.insert('counters', {
        'name': key,
        'value': value,
      }, conflictAlgorithm: ConflictAlgorithm.replace);
      return value;
    });

    return 'REC-$day-${next.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}';
  }

  Future<void> voidSale(String saleId, String userId, String reason) async {
    if (reason.trim().isEmpty || reason.trim().length > 240) {
      throw Exception('A void reason of 1 to 240 characters is required.');
    }
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final saleMaps = await txn.query(
        'sales',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [saleId],
      );
      if (saleMaps.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Sale not found');
      }
      if (saleMaps.first['status'] == 'VOIDED') {
        throw Exception('Sale is already voided');
      }

      final users = await txn.query(
        'users',
        columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [userId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (users.isEmpty ||
          users.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
          users.first['is_active'] != 1) {
        throw Exception('Only an active owner can void a sale.');
      }
      final activeShifts = await txn.query(
        'shifts',
        columns: ['id'],
        where: "status = 'OPEN'",
        limit: 1,
      );

      final updated = await txn.update(
        'sales',
        {
          'status': 'VOIDED',
          'void_reason': reason.trim(),
          'voided_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
          'voided_by': userId,
          'voided_shift_id': activeShifts.isEmpty
              ? null
              : activeShifts.first['id'],
        },
        where: "id = ? AND status != 'VOIDED'",
        whereArgs: [saleId],
      );
      if (updated != 1) {
        throw Exception('Sale is no longer voidable');
      }

      // A direct owner void also settles any pending cashier request for the
      // same sale so it stops showing up in the approval queue.
      await txn.update(
        'void_requests',
        {
          'status': VoidRequest.statusApproved,
          'decided_by': userId,
          'decided_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        },
        where: "sale_id = ? AND status = ?",
        whereArgs: [saleId, VoidRequest.statusPending],
      );

      final voidedTotal = saleMaps.first['total_minor_units'] as int? ?? 0;
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': userId,
        'action': 'SALE_VOIDED',
        'details':
            'Sale of ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(voidedTotal)} voided. '
            'Reason: ${reason.trim()}',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  /// Queries historical sales. Search matches product names, quantity
  /// descriptions, notes, customer name, cashier username, and void reason —
  /// never receipt numbers. [period] and [status] are optional filters.
  Future<List<Sale>> getSales({
    String? searchQuery,
    DateRange? period,
    String? status,
    int? limit,
  }) async {
    final where = <String>[];
    final args = <dynamic>[];

    if (period != null) {
      where.add('sales.created_at >= ? AND sales.created_at < ?');
      args.add(period.start.toIso8601String());
      args.add(period.end.toIso8601String());
    }
    if (status != null && status.isNotEmpty && status != 'ALL') {
      where.add('sales.status = ?');
      args.add(status);
    }

    final query = searchQuery?.trim();
    if (query != null && query.isNotEmpty) {
      final pattern = '%${escapeLike(query.toLowerCase())}%';
      const escape = r" ESCAPE '\'";
      where.add('''
        (LOWER(COALESCE(sales.customer_name, '')) LIKE ?$escape
         OR LOWER(COALESCE(sales.void_reason, '')) LIKE ?$escape
         OR LOWER(users.username) LIKE ?$escape
         OR EXISTS (
              SELECT 1 FROM sale_items si
              WHERE si.sale_id = sales.id
                AND (LOWER(si.product_name_snapshot) LIKE ?$escape
                 OR LOWER(si.quantity_text) LIKE ?$escape
                 OR LOWER(COALESCE(si.notes, '')) LIKE ?$escape)))
      ''');
      args.addAll(List.filled(6, pattern));
    }

    final sql = StringBuffer('''
      SELECT sales.*, users.username AS cashier_name,
        EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM void_requests vr
          WHERE vr.sale_id = sales.id AND vr.status = 'PENDING'
        ) AS has_pending_void_request
      FROM sales
      LEFT JOIN users ON sales.cashier_id = users.id
    ''');
    if (where.isNotEmpty) sql.write(' WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}');
    sql.write(' ORDER BY sales.created_at DESC');
    if (limit != null) {
      sql.write(' LIMIT ?');
      args.add(limit);
    }

    final saleMaps = await _db.rawQuery(sql.toString(), args);
    return _hydrate(saleMaps);
  }

  Future<List<Sale>> getRecentSales({int limit = 5}) {
    return getSales(limit: limit);
  }

  /// Voids that must be reviewed at shift close. Mirrors the cash calculation
  /// in [ShiftRepository.getShiftSummary] exactly.
  Future<List<Sale>> getVoidedSalesForShift(String shiftId) async {
    final saleMaps = await _db.query(
      'sales',
      where:
          "status = 'VOIDED' AND "
          '(voided_shift_id = ? OR (voided_shift_id IS NULL AND shift_id = ?))',
      whereArgs: [shiftId, shiftId],
      orderBy: 'created_at DESC',
    );
    return _hydrate(saleMaps);
  }

  /// A cashier (or owner) flags a completed sale for voiding. The sale is left
  /// `COMPLETED` until an owner approves the request, so revenue and the drawer
  /// are unchanged in the meantime.
  Future<void> requestVoid(String saleId, String userId, String reason) async {
    if (reason.trim().isEmpty || reason.trim().length > 240) {
      throw Exception('A void reason of 1 to 240 characters is required.');
    }
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final saleMaps = await txn.query(
        'sales',
        columns: ['status'],
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [saleId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (saleMaps.isEmpty) throw Exception('Sale not found');
      if (saleMaps.first['status'] != 'COMPLETED') {
        throw Exception('Only a completed sale can be flagged for voiding.');
      }
      final users = await txn.query(
        'users',
        columns: ['is_active'],
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [userId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (users.isEmpty || users.first['is_active'] != 1) {
        throw Exception('An active user is required.');
      }
      final pending = await txn.query(
        'void_requests',
        columns: ['id'],
        where: "sale_id = ? AND status = 'PENDING'",
        whereArgs: [saleId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (pending.isNotEmpty) {
        throw Exception('A void request is already pending for this sale.');
      }
      await txn.insert('void_requests', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'sale_id': saleId,
        'requested_by': userId,
        'reason': reason.trim(),
        'status': VoidRequest.statusPending,
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': userId,
        'action': 'VOID_REQUESTED',
        'details': 'Void requested for sale $saleId. Reason: ${reason.trim()}',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  /// Pending void requests, newest first, each with the targeted sale hydrated
  /// for display. Owner-only surface, but readable by any caller.
  Future<List<VoidRequest>> getPendingVoidRequests() async {
    final rows = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT vr.*, users.username AS requester_name
      FROM void_requests vr
      LEFT JOIN users ON vr.requested_by = users.id
      WHERE vr.status = ?
      ORDER BY vr.created_at DESC
    ''',
      [VoidRequest.statusPending],
    );
    if (rows.isEmpty) return [];
    final sales = await _salesByIds(
      rows.map((row) => row['sale_id'] as String).toList(),
    );
    final byId = {for (final sale in sales) sale.id: sale};
    return rows
        .map(
          (row) => VoidRequest(
            id: row['id'] as String,
            saleId: row['sale_id'] as String,
            requestedBy: row['requested_by'] as String,
            requesterName: row['requester_name'] as String?,
            reason: row['reason'] as String,
            status: row['status'] as String,
            createdAt: DateTime.parse(row['created_at'] as String),
            decidedBy: row['decided_by'] as String?,
            decidedAt: row['decided_at'] == null
                ? null
                : DateTime.parse(row['decided_at'] as String),
            sale: byId[row['sale_id'] as String],
          ),
        )
        .toList();
  }

  /// Owner approves a pending request: the sale is voided exactly as a direct
  /// owner void would be, then the request is closed out.
  Future<void> approveVoidRequest(String requestId, String ownerId) async {
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final owner = await txn.query(
        'users',
        columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [ownerId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (owner.isEmpty ||
          owner.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
          owner.first['is_active'] != 1) {
        throw Exception('Only an active owner can approve a void request.');
      }
      final requests = await txn.query(
        'void_requests',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [requestId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (requests.isEmpty) throw Exception('Void request not found');
      if (requests.first['status'] != VoidRequest.statusPending) {
        throw Exception('This void request has already been decided.');
      }
      final saleId = requests.first['sale_id'] as String;
      final reason = requests.first['reason'] as String;

      final saleMaps = await txn.query(
        'sales',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [saleId],
      );
      if (saleMaps.isEmpty) throw Exception('Sale not found');
      if (saleMaps.first['status'] == 'VOIDED') {
        throw Exception('Sale is already voided');
      }
      final activeShifts = await txn.query(
        'shifts',
        columns: ['id'],
        where: "status = 'OPEN'",
        limit: 1,
      );
      final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
      final updated = await txn.update(
        'sales',
        {
          'status': 'VOIDED',
          'void_reason': reason,
          'voided_at': now,
          'voided_by': ownerId,
          'voided_shift_id': activeShifts.isEmpty
              ? null
              : activeShifts.first['id'],
        },
        where: "id = ? AND status != 'VOIDED'",
        whereArgs: [saleId],
      );
      if (updated != 1) throw Exception('Sale is no longer voidable');

      await txn.update(
        'void_requests',
        {
          'status': VoidRequest.statusApproved,
          'decided_by': ownerId,
          'decided_at': now,
        },
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [requestId],
      );

      final voidedTotal = saleMaps.first['total_minor_units'] as int? ?? 0;
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': ownerId,
        'action': 'SALE_VOIDED',
        'details':
            'Sale of ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(voidedTotal)} voided via request. '
            'Reason: $reason',
        'created_at': now,
      });
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': ownerId,
        'action': 'VOID_REQUEST_APPROVED',
        'details': 'Void request $requestId approved for sale $saleId.',
        'created_at': now,
      });
    });
  }

  /// Owner rejects a pending request; the sale is left untouched.
  Future<void> rejectVoidRequest(String requestId, String ownerId) async {
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final owner = await txn.query(
        'users',
        columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [ownerId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (owner.isEmpty ||
          owner.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
          owner.first['is_active'] != 1) {
        throw Exception('Only an active owner can reject a void request.');
      }
      final requests = await txn.query(
        'void_requests',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [requestId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (requests.isEmpty) throw Exception('Void request not found');
      if (requests.first['status'] != VoidRequest.statusPending) {
        throw Exception('This void request has already been decided.');
      }
      await txn.update(
        'void_requests',
        {
          'status': VoidRequest.statusRejected,
          'decided_by': ownerId,
          'decided_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        },
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [requestId],
      );
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': ownerId,
        'action': 'VOID_REQUEST_REJECTED',
        'details':
            'Void request $requestId rejected. '
            'Reason given: ${requests.first['reason']}',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  /// Loads sales by id (with cashier name and pending-request flag hydrated).
  Future<List<Sale>> _salesByIds(List<String> ids) async {
    if (ids.isEmpty) return [];
    final placeholders = List.filled(ids.length, '?').join(',');
    final maps = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT sales.*, users.username AS cashier_name,
        EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM void_requests vr
          WHERE vr.sale_id = sales.id AND vr.status = 'PENDING'
        ) AS has_pending_void_request
      FROM sales
      LEFT JOIN users ON sales.cashier_id = users.id
      WHERE sales.id IN ($placeholders)
    ''', ids);
    return _hydrate(maps);
  }

  /// Completed revenue and sale count per calendar day across [period],
  /// ordered oldest to newest, for the dashboard trend chart.
  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getRevenueTrend(DateRange period) async {
    return _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT substr(created_at, 1, 10) AS day,
        COUNT(*) AS count,
        COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total
      FROM sales
      WHERE status = 'COMPLETED' AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?
      GROUP BY day
      ORDER BY day ASC
    ''',
      [period.start.toIso8601String(), period.end.toIso8601String()],
    );
  }

  Future<List<Sale>> _hydrate(List<Map<String, Object?>> saleMaps) async {
    if (saleMaps.isEmpty) return [];

    final ids = saleMaps.map((map) => map['id'] as String).toList();
    final itemsBySale = <String, List<SaleItem>>{};
    const chunkSize = 400;
    for (var start = 0; start < ids.length; start += chunkSize) {
      final end = start + chunkSize > ids.length
          ? ids.length
          : start + chunkSize;
      final chunk = ids.sublist(start, end);
      final placeholders = List.filled(chunk.length, '?').join(',');
      final itemMaps = await _db.query(
        'sale_items',
        where: 'sale_id IN ($placeholders)',
        whereArgs: chunk,
        orderBy: 'rowid ASC',
      );
      for (final row in itemMaps) {
        final item = SaleItem.fromMap(row);
        itemsBySale.putIfAbsent(item.saleId, () => []).add(item);
      }
    }

    return saleMaps.map((map) {
      final saleId = map['id'] as String;
      return Sale(
        id: saleId,
        receiptNumber: map['receipt_number'] as String,
        cashierId: map['cashier_id'] as String,
        deviceId: map['device_id'] as String,
        shiftId: map['shift_id'] as String? ?? '',
        customerName: map['customer_name'] as String?,
        paymentMethod: map['payment_method'] as String,
        subtotalMinorUnits: map['subtotal_minor_units'] as int,
        discountMinorUnits: map['discount_minor_units'] as int,
        totalMinorUnits: map['total_minor_units'] as int,
        amountPaidMinorUnits: map['amount_paid_minor_units'] as int,
        changeMinorUnits: map['change_minor_units'] as int,
        status: map['status'] as String,
        voidReason: map['void_reason'] as String?,
        voidedAt: map['voided_at'] == null
            ? null
            : DateTime.parse(map['voided_at'] as String),
        voidedBy: map['voided_by'] as String?,
        voidedShiftId: map['voided_shift_id'] as String?,
        createdAt: DateTime.parse(map['created_at'] as String),
        syncedAt: map['synced_at'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(map['synced_at'] as String)
            : null,
        cashierName: map['cashier_name'] as String?,
        hasPendingVoidRequest:
            (map['has_pending_void_request'] as int? ?? 0) == 1,
        items: itemsBySale[saleId] ?? const [],
      );
    }).toList();
  }

  /// Revenue figures for the dashboard. Voided sales are always excluded.
  /// Defaults to today so the dashboard resets at local midnight purely by
  /// date filtering — records are never deleted.
  Future<Map<String, int>> getDashboardStats({DateRange? period}) async {
    final range = period ?? DateRange.today();
    final rangeArgs = [
      range.start.toIso8601String(),
      range.end.toIso8601String(),
    ];
    const completed =
        "created_at >= ? AND created_at < ? AND status = 'COMPLETED'";

    final revenueRow = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT COUNT(*) AS count, COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total
      FROM sales WHERE $completed
    ''', rangeArgs);

    final cashRow = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total
      FROM sales WHERE $completed AND payment_method = 'CASH'
    ''', rangeArgs);

    final bankRow = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total
      FROM sales WHERE $completed AND payment_method = 'BANK'
    ''', rangeArgs);

    // Sales rung up in the period that were later voided.
    final voidRow = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM sales
      WHERE status = 'VOIDED' AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?
    ''', rangeArgs);

    // Cashier void requests still awaiting an owner's decision.
    final pendingRow = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
      FROM void_requests vr
      JOIN sales s ON vr.sale_id = s.id
      WHERE vr.status = 'PENDING'
        AND s.created_at >= ? AND s.created_at < ?
    ''', rangeArgs);

    final shiftResult = await _db.query(
      'shifts',
      where: 'status = ?',
      whereArgs: ['OPEN'],
      limit: 1,
    );
    int expectedCash = 0;
    if (shiftResult.isNotEmpty) {
      final shift = Shift.fromMap(shiftResult.first);
      final summary = await ShiftRepository(_db).getShiftSummary(shift);
      expectedCash = summary['expected_cash']!;
    }

    final salesCount = revenueRow.first['count'] as int;
    final revenue = revenueRow.first['total'] as int;

    return {
      'revenue': revenue,
      'sales_count': salesCount,
      'cash_revenue': cashRow.first['total'] as int,
      'bank_revenue': bankRow.first['total'] as int,
      'average_sale': salesCount == 0 ? 0 : revenue ~/ salesCount,
      'expected_cash': expectedCash,
      'is_shift_open': shiftResult.isEmpty ? 0 : 1,
      'void_count': voidRow.first['count'] as int,
      'pending_void_requests': pendingRow.first['count'] as int,
    };
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getAuditLogs({int limit = 500}) async {
    return _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT audit_logs.action, audit_logs.details, audit_logs.created_at, users.username
      FROM audit_logs
      LEFT JOIN users ON audit_logs.user_id = users.id
      ORDER BY audit_logs.created_at DESC
      LIMIT ?
      ''',
      [limit],
    );
  }

  Future<int> getTodayRevenue() async {
    final stats = await getDashboardStats();
    return stats['revenue']!;
  }

  /// Revenue by entered product name for the reports screen. Quantities are
  /// summed in milliunits; lines with manual totals contribute revenue but no
  /// numeric quantity.
  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getProductSalesSummary({
    DateRange? period,
  }) async {
    final rangeArgs = period == null
        ? <dynamic>[]
        : [period.start.toIso8601String(), period.end.toIso8601String()];
    final rangeClause = period == null
        ? ''
        : ' AND sales.created_at >= ? AND sales.created_at < ?';
    return _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT si.product_name_snapshot,
        COALESCE(SUM(si.billing_quantity_milliunits), 0) AS total_qty_milliunits,
        COUNT(*) AS line_count,
        SUM(si.line_total_minor_units) AS total_revenue
      FROM sale_items si
      JOIN sales ON si.sale_id = sales.id
      WHERE sales.status = 'COMPLETED'$rangeClause
      GROUP BY si.product_name_snapshot
      ORDER BY total_revenue DESC
    ''', rangeArgs);
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getCashierPerformance({
    DateRange? period,
  }) async {
    final rangeArgs = period == null
        ? <dynamic>[]
        : [period.start.toIso8601String(), period.end.toIso8601String()];
    final rangeClause = period == null
        ? ''
        : ' AND sales.created_at >= ? AND sales.created_at < ?';
    return _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT users.username, COUNT(sales.id) AS total_sales, COALESCE(SUM(sales.total_minor_units), 0) AS total_revenue
      FROM sales
      JOIN users ON sales.cashier_id = users.id
      WHERE sales.status = 'COMPLETED'$rangeClause
      GROUP BY users.id, users.username
      ORDER BY total_revenue DESC
    ''', rangeArgs);
  }
}
