import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../features/sales/shift.dart';
import '../utils/date_range.dart';

class ShiftRepository {
  final Database _db;

  ShiftRepository(this._db);

  Future<Shift?> getActiveShift() async {
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'shifts',
      where: 'status = ?',
      whereArgs: ['OPEN'],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (maps.isEmpty) return null;
    return Shift.fromMap(maps.first);
  }

  /// Past and present register sessions, newest first, each carrying the
  /// cashier username for display. Filter by the day the shift was *opened*
  /// ([period], a half-open local range) and/or [status] (`OPEN`/`CLOSED`).
  /// Closed shifts keep their stored expected/counted figures even if sales are
  /// later voided, so the historical reconciliation is never rewritten.
  Future<List<Shift>> getShiftHistory({
    DateRange? period,
    String? status,
  }) async {
    final clauses = <String>[];
    final args = <Object?>[];
    if (period != null) {
      clauses.add('shifts.opened_at >= ? AND shifts.opened_at < ?');
      args
        ..add(period.start.toIso8601String())
        ..add(period.end.toIso8601String());
    }
    if (status != null) {
      clauses.add('shifts.status = ?');
      args.add(status);
    }
    final where = clauses.isEmpty ? '' : ' WHERE ${clauses.join(' AND ')}';
    final maps = await _db.rawQuery('''
      SELECT shifts.*, users.username AS cashier_name
      FROM shifts
      LEFT JOIN users ON shifts.cashier_id = users.id
      $where
      ORDER BY shifts.opened_at DESC
    ''', args);
    return maps.map(Shift.fromMap).toList();
  }

  Future<void> openShift(String cashierId, int openingCash) async {
    if (openingCash < 0) throw Exception('Opening cash cannot be negative.');
    final shift = Shift(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      cashierId: cashierId,
      openingCashMinorUnits: openingCash,
      status: 'OPEN',
      openedAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final active = await txn.query(
        'shifts',
        where: "status = 'OPEN'",
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (active.isNotEmpty) throw Exception('A shift is already open');
      await txn.insert('shifts', shift.toMap());
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': cashierId,
        'action': 'SHIFT_OPENED',
        'details': 'Shift $shift.id opened with cash $openingCash minor units.',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  Future<void> addCashMovement(
    String shiftId,
    String type,
    int amount,
    String reason, {
    String? userId,
  }) async {
    if (amount <= 0) throw Exception('Movement amount must be positive.');
    if (!{'WITHDRAWAL', 'DEPOSIT', 'EXPENSE'}.contains(type)) {
      throw Exception('Invalid cash movement type.');
    }
    if (reason.trim().isEmpty) throw Exception('A reason is required.');
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final shifts = await txn.query(
        'shifts',
        where: "id = ? AND status = 'OPEN'",
        whereArgs: [shiftId],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (shifts.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Cash movement requires an open shift.');
      }
      if (userId != null) {
        final users = await txn.query(
          'users',
          columns: ['is_active'],
          where: 'id = ?',
          whereArgs: [userId],
          limit: 1,
        );
        if (users.isEmpty || users.first['is_active'] != 1) {
          throw Exception('An active user is required.');
        }
      }
      await txn.insert('cash_movements', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'shift_id': shiftId,
        'user_id': userId,
        'type': type,
        'amount_minor_units': amount,
        'reason': reason.trim(),
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': userId,
        'action': 'CASH_MOVEMENT_ADDED',
        'details': '$type $amount for shift $shiftId: ${reason.trim()}',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  Future<Map<String, int>> getShiftSummary(Shift shift) async {
    final cashSales = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT SUM(total_minor_units) as total 
      FROM sales 
      WHERE status = 'COMPLETED' AND payment_method = 'CASH' AND shift_id = ?
    ''',
      [shift.id],
    );

    final bankSales = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT SUM(total_minor_units) as total 
      FROM sales 
      WHERE status = 'COMPLETED' AND payment_method = 'BANK' AND shift_id = ?
    ''',
      [shift.id],
    );

    final voids = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT SUM(total_minor_units) as total 
      FROM sales 
      WHERE status = 'VOIDED' AND (voided_shift_id = ? OR (voided_shift_id IS NULL AND shift_id = ?))
    ''',
      [shift.id, shift.id],
    );

    final cashMovements = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT 
        SUM(CASE WHEN type = 'DEPOSIT' THEN amount_minor_units ELSE 0 END) as deposits,
        SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('WITHDRAWAL', 'EXPENSE') THEN amount_minor_units ELSE 0 END) as withdrawals
      FROM cash_movements 
      WHERE shift_id = ?
    ''',
      [shift.id],
    );

    final cashTotal = (cashSales.first['total'] as int?) ?? 0;
    final bankTotal = (bankSales.first['total'] as int?) ?? 0;
    final voidTotal = (voids.first['total'] as int?) ?? 0;

    final deposits = (cashMovements.first['deposits'] as int?) ?? 0;
    final withdrawals = (cashMovements.first['withdrawals'] as int?) ?? 0;

    final cashRefunds = await _cashRefundsForShift(shift.id);
    final expectedCash =
        shift.openingCashMinorUnits +
        cashTotal +
        deposits -
        withdrawals -
        cashRefunds;

    return {
      'cash_sales': cashTotal,
      'bank_sales': bankTotal,
      'voids': voidTotal,
      'deposits': deposits,
      'withdrawals': withdrawals,
      'cash_refunds': cashRefunds,
      'expected_cash': expectedCash,
    };
  }

  /// Cash that genuinely leaves *this* drawer because of a void: a sale that
  /// was rung up in a different shift but refunded while this one was open.
  /// A sale rung up and voided in the same shift nets to zero (its incoming
  /// cash was never counted in `cash_sales`, so it must not be subtracted
  /// again), which is why `shift_id <> voided_shift_id` is required here.
  Future<int> _cashRefundsForShift(String shiftId) async {
    final rows = await _db.rawQuery(
      '''
      SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total FROM sales
      WHERE status = 'VOIDED' AND payment_method = 'CASH'
        AND voided_shift_id = ? AND shift_id <> ?
    ''',
      [shiftId, shiftId],
    );
    return rows.first['total'] as int;
  }

  Future<void> closeShift(
    Shift shift,
    int closingCash, {
    String? userId,
  }) async {
    if (closingCash < 0) throw Exception('Closing cash cannot be negative.');
    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final rows = await txn.query(
        'shifts',
        where: "id = ? AND status = 'OPEN'",
        whereArgs: [shift.id],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (rows.isEmpty) throw Exception('Shift is no longer open.');
      final currentShift = Shift.fromMap(rows.first);
      final cashSales = await txn.rawQuery(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total FROM sales WHERE status = 'COMPLETED' AND payment_method = 'CASH' AND shift_id = ?",
        [shift.id],
      );
      final movements = await txn.rawQuery(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN type = 'DEPOSIT' THEN amount_minor_units ELSE -amount_minor_units END), 0) AS total FROM cash_movements WHERE shift_id = ?",
        [shift.id],
      );
      // Only cross-shift cash refunds reduce this drawer; see
      // [_cashRefundsForShift] for the same-shift netting rule.
      final cashVoids = await txn.rawQuery(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_minor_units), 0) AS total FROM sales WHERE status = 'VOIDED' AND payment_method = 'CASH' AND voided_shift_id = ? AND shift_id <> ?",
        [shift.id, shift.id],
      );
      final expected =
          currentShift.openingCashMinorUnits +
          (cashSales.first['total'] as int) +
          (movements.first['total'] as int) -
          (cashVoids.first['total'] as int);
      await txn.update(
        'shifts',
        {
          'closing_cash_minor_units': closingCash,
          'expected_cash_minor_units': expected,
          'status': 'CLOSED',
          'closed_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        },
        where: "id = ? AND status = 'OPEN'",
        whereArgs: [shift.id],
      );
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': userId ?? shift.cashierId,
        'action': 'SHIFT_CLOSED',
        'details':
            'Shift ${shift.id} closed. Expected: $expected, '
            'Counted: $closingCash, Difference: ${closingCash - expected}.',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\core\repositories\user_repository.dart

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:isolate';
import 'dart:math';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:cryptography/cryptography.dart' as cryptography;
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../models/user.dart';

class UserRepository {
  final Database _db;
  UserRepository(this._db);

  static const int defaultIterations = 210000;
  static const int minimumPasswordLength = 6;
  static const Set<String> roles = {'OWNER', 'CASHIER'};

  static const String _pbkdf2Prefix = 'pbkdf2-sha256\$v=1\$';
  static const int _legacyIterations = 10000;
  static const int _minimumIterations = 1000;
  static const int _maximumIterations = 2000000;

  String _generateSalt() {
    final random = Random.secure();
    return base64Url.encode(List<int>.generate(16, (i) => random.nextInt(256)));
  }

  /// Derives a key with standard PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 on a background isolate so
  /// the UI stays responsive during the 210k rounds.
  static Future<String> _derive(String password, String salt, int iterations) {
    return Isolate.run(() async {
      final algorithm = cryptography.Pbkdf2(
        macAlgorithm: cryptography.Hmac(cryptography.Sha256()),
        iterations: iterations,
        bits: 256,
      );
      final key = await algorithm.deriveKey(
        secretKey: cryptography.SecretKey(utf8.encode(password)),
        nonce: utf8.encode(salt),
      );
      return base64.encode(await key.extractBytes());
    });
  }

  String _formatHash(int iterations, String derived) {
    return 'pbkdf2-sha256\$v=1\$i=$iterations\$$derived';
  }

  Future<String> _hashPassword(String password, String salt) async {
    final derived = await _derive(password, salt, defaultIterations);
    return _formatHash(defaultIterations, derived);
  }

  /// Password format used by releases before schema 5: 10,000 rounds of
  /// HMAC-SHA256 over the salt, stored as base64. Kept only to migrate
  /// existing installations; new passwords always use [_hashPassword].
  String _hashPasswordLegacy(String password, String salt) {
    final key = utf8.encode(password);
    final saltBytes = utf8.encode(salt);
    var bytes = Hmac(sha256, key).convert(saltBytes).bytes;
    for (var i = 0; i < _legacyIterations; i++) {
      bytes = Hmac(sha256, key).convert(bytes).bytes;
    }
    return base64.encode(bytes);
  }

  bool _constantTimeEquals(String left, String right) {
    final leftBytes = utf8.encode(left);
    final rightBytes = utf8.encode(right);
    if (leftBytes.length != rightBytes.length) return false;
    var difference = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < leftBytes.length; i++) {
      difference |= leftBytes[i] ^ rightBytes[i];
    }
    return difference == 0;
  }

  Future<bool> _verifyPassword(
    String password,
    String salt,
    String storedHash,
  ) async {
    if (!storedHash.startsWith(_pbkdf2Prefix)) return false;
    final parts = storedHash.split(r'$');
    if (parts.length != 4 || parts[1] != 'v=1') return false;
    if (!parts[2].startsWith('i=')) return false;
    final iterations = int.tryParse(parts[2].substring(2));
    if (iterations == null ||
        iterations < _minimumIterations ||
        iterations > _maximumIterations) {
      return false;
    }
    final derived = await _derive(password, salt, iterations);
    return _constantTimeEquals(_formatHash(iterations, derived), storedHash);
  }

  Future<bool> _verifyUserPassword(User user, String password) async {
    if (user.passwordHash.startsWith(_pbkdf2Prefix)) {
      return _verifyPassword(password, user.salt, user.passwordHash);
    }
    return _constantTimeEquals(
      _hashPasswordLegacy(password, user.salt),
      user.passwordHash,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _upgradePasswordHash(User user, String password) async {
    final salt = _generateSalt();
    final hash = await _hashPassword(password, salt);
    await _db.update(
      'users',
      {'password_hash': hash, 'salt': salt},
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [user.id],
    );
  }

  Future<void> _audit({
    String? userId,
    required String action,
    required String details,
  }) async {
    try {
      await _db.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': userId,
        'action': action,
        'details': details,
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    } catch (_) {
      // Auditing must never block authentication or account management.
    }
  }

  static void _validatePassword(String password) {
    if (password.length < minimumPasswordLength) {
      throw Exception(
        'Password must be at least $minimumPasswordLength characters.',
      );
    }
  }

  Future<bool> hasUsers() async {
    final result = await _db.rawQuery('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM users');
    return (result.first['count'] as int) > 0;
  }

  Future<User> createFirstOwner(String username, String password) async {
    final name = username.trim();
    if (name.isEmpty) {
      throw Exception('Username cannot be empty.');
    }
    _validatePassword(password);

    final salt = _generateSalt();
    final hash = await _hashPassword(password, salt);
    final user = User(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      username: name,
      passwordHash: hash,
      salt: salt,
      role: 'OWNER',
    );

    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final existing = await txn.rawQuery(
        'SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM users',
      );
      if ((existing.first['count'] as int) > 0) {
        throw Exception('An owner account already exists.');
      }
      await txn.insert('users', user.toMap());
    });

    await _audit(
      userId: user.id,
      action: 'OWNER_CREATED',
      details: 'First owner account "$name" created.',
    );
    return user;
  }

  Future<User?> authenticate(String username, String password) async {
    final name = username.trim();
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'users',
      where: 'LOWER(username) = LOWER(?) AND is_active = 1',
      whereArgs: [name],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (maps.isEmpty) {
      await _audit(action: 'LOGIN_FAILED', details: 'Unknown username: $name');
      return null;
    }

    final user = User.fromMap(maps.first);
    if (await _verifyUserPassword(user, password)) {
      if (!user.passwordHash.startsWith(_pbkdf2Prefix)) {
        await _upgradePasswordHash(user, password);
        await _audit(
          userId: user.id,
          action: 'PASSWORD_UPGRADED',
          details: 'Legacy password hash upgraded for "${user.username}".',
        );
      }
      await _audit(
        userId: user.id,
        action: 'LOGIN_SUCCEEDED',
        details: 'User "${user.username}" signed in.',
      );
      return user;
    }

    await _audit(
      action: 'LOGIN_FAILED',
      details: 'Wrong password for username: $name',
    );
    return null;
  }

  Future<void> createUser(String username, String password, String role) async {
    final name = username.trim();
    if (name.isEmpty) {
      throw Exception('Username cannot be empty.');
    }
    _validatePassword(password);
    if (!roles.contains(role)) {
      throw Exception('Invalid role.');
    }

    final salt = _generateSalt();
    final hash = await _hashPassword(password, salt);
    final user = User(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      username: name,
      passwordHash: hash,
      salt: salt,
      role: role,
    );

    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      final existing = await txn.query(
        'users',
        where: 'LOWER(username) = LOWER(?)',
        whereArgs: [name],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (existing.isNotEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Username is already taken.');
      }
      await txn.insert('users', user.toMap());
    });

    await _audit(
      userId: user.id,
      action: 'USER_CREATED',
      details: 'User "$name" created with role $role.',
    );
  }

  Future<void> changePassword({
    required String userId,
    required String currentPassword,
    required String newPassword,
  }) async {
    _validatePassword(newPassword);
    final maps = await _db.query(
      'users',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [userId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (maps.isEmpty) throw Exception('User not found.');
    final user = User.fromMap(maps.first);
    if (!await _verifyUserPassword(user, currentPassword)) {
      throw Exception('Current password is incorrect.');
    }
    await _upgradePasswordHash(user, newPassword);
    await _audit(
      userId: user.id,
      action: 'PASSWORD_CHANGED',
      details: 'User "${user.username}" changed their password.',
    );
  }

  Future<void> resetPassword({
    required String targetUserId,
    required String newPassword,
    required String actingUserId,
  }) async {
    _validatePassword(newPassword);
    final actors = await _db.query(
      'users',
      columns: ['role', 'is_active', 'username'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [actingUserId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (actors.isEmpty ||
        actors.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
        actors.first['is_active'] != 1) {
      throw Exception('Only an active owner can reset a password.');
    }
    final targets = await _db.query(
      'users',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [targetUserId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (targets.isEmpty) throw Exception('User not found.');
    final target = User.fromMap(targets.first);
    await _upgradePasswordHash(target, newPassword);
    await _audit(
      userId: actingUserId,
      action: 'PASSWORD_RESET',
      details: 'Password reset for "${target.username}".',
    );
  }

  Future<void> setUserActive({
    required String targetUserId,
    required bool active,
    required String actingUserId,
  }) async {
    if (targetUserId == actingUserId && !active) {
      throw Exception('You cannot deactivate your own account.');
    }
    final actors = await _db.query(
      'users',
      columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [actingUserId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (actors.isEmpty ||
        actors.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
        actors.first['is_active'] != 1) {
      throw Exception('Only an active owner can change user status.');
    }
    final targets = await _db.query(
      'users',
      columns: ['username', 'role', 'is_active'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [targetUserId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (targets.isEmpty) throw Exception('User not found.');
    final target = targets.first;

    await _db.transaction((txn) async {
      if (!active && target['role'] == 'OWNER') {
        final owners = await txn.rawQuery(
          "SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM users WHERE role = 'OWNER' AND is_active = 1",
        );
        if ((owners.first['count'] as int) <= 1) {
          throw Exception('At least one active owner is required.');
        }
      }
      await txn.update(
        'users',
        {'is_active': active ? 1 : 0},
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [targetUserId],
      );
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': actingUserId,
        'action': active ? 'USER_ACTIVATED' : 'USER_DEACTIVATED',
        'details':
            'Account "${target['username']}" was '
            '${active ? 'activated' : 'deactivated'}.',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  Future<List<User>> getAllUsers() async {
    final maps = await _db.query('users', orderBy: 'username ASC');
    return maps.map((m) => User.fromMap(m)).toList();
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppTheme {
  static const Color background = Color(0xFFFAFAFA);
  static const Color foreground = Color(0xFF09090B);
  static const Color surface = Color(0xFFFFFFFF);
  static const Color border = Color(0xFFE4E4E7);
  static const Color muted = Color(0xFF71717A);
  static const Color primary = Color(0xFF18181B);
  static const Color primaryForeground = Color(0xFFFAFAFA);
  static const Color danger = Color(0xFFEF4444);
  static const Color success = Color(0xFF22C55E);
  static const Color warning = Color(0xFFF59E0B);

  static ThemeData light() {
    const fontFamily = 'Segoe UI';

    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: background,
      fontFamily: fontFamily,
      colorScheme: const ColorScheme.light(
        primary: primary,
        surface: surface,
        onSurface: foreground,
        error: danger,
      ),
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: surface,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          color: foreground,
          fontSize: 16,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: foreground),
        shape: const Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: border, width: 1)),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: surface,
        elevation: 0,
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          side: const BorderSide(color: border, width: 1),
        ),
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: primary,
          foregroundColor: primaryForeground,
          elevation: 0,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
          textStyle: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: foreground,
          side: const BorderSide(color: border),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
          textStyle: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: surface,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: 12,
          vertical: 14,
        ),
        hintStyle: TextStyle(
          color: muted,
          fontSize: 14,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: border),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: border),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: primary, width: 1),
        ),
      ),
      dividerTheme: const DividerThemeData(color: border, thickness: 1),
    );
  }

  static ThemeData dark() {
    const fontFamily = 'Segoe UI';
    const darkBackground = Color(0xFF09090B);
    const darkSurface = Color(0xFF18181B);
    const darkBorder = Color(0xFF27272A);
    const darkMuted = Color(0xFFA1A1AA);
    const darkForeground = Color(0xFFFAFAFA);

    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: darkBackground,
      fontFamily: fontFamily,
      colorScheme: const ColorScheme.dark(
        primary: darkForeground,
        surface: darkSurface,
        onSurface: darkForeground,
        error: danger,
      ),
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: darkSurface,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          color: darkForeground,
          fontSize: 16,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: darkForeground),
        shape: const Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: darkBorder, width: 1)),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: darkSurface,
        elevation: 0,
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          side: const BorderSide(color: darkBorder, width: 1),
        ),
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: darkForeground,
          foregroundColor: darkBackground,
          elevation: 0,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
          textStyle: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: darkForeground,
          side: const BorderSide(color: darkBorder),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
          textStyle: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: darkSurface,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: 12,
          vertical: 14,
        ),
        hintStyle: TextStyle(
          color: darkMuted,
          fontSize: 14,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: darkBorder),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: darkBorder),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: darkForeground, width: 1),
        ),
      ),
      dividerTheme: const DividerThemeData(color: darkBorder, thickness: 1),
    );
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\core\utils\data_exporter.dart

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../database/local_database.dart';

class DataExporter {
  static Future<String> exportToJson(
    Database db, {
    required String requestingUserId,
  }) async {
    final user = await db.query(
      'users',
      columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [requestingUserId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (user.isEmpty ||
        user.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
        user.first['is_active'] != 1) {
      throw Exception('Only an active owner can export data.');
    }

    final users = await db.query('users');
    final products = await db.query('products');
    final sales = await db.query('sales');
    final saleItems = await db.query('sale_items');
    final voidRequests = await db.query('void_requests');
    final shifts = await db.query('shifts');
    final cashMovements = await db.query('cash_movements');
    final auditLogs = await db.query('audit_logs');
    final devices = await db.query('devices');
    final counters = await db.query('counters');
    final settings = await db.query('settings');
    final financeCategories = await db.query('finance_categories');
    final financeEntries = await db.query('finance_entries');

    final exportData = {
      'exported_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      'version': LocalDatabase.schemaVersion,
      'users': users,
      'products': products,
      'sales': sales,
      'sale_items': saleItems,
      'void_requests': voidRequests,
      'shifts': shifts,
      'cash_movements': cashMovements,
      'audit_logs': auditLogs,
      'devices': devices,
      'counters': counters,
      'settings': settings,
      'finance_categories': financeCategories,
      'finance_entries': financeEntries,
    };

    final jsonString = const JsonEncoder.withIndent('  ').convert(exportData);

    final directory = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
    final timestamp = DateTime.now()
        .toIso8601String()
        .replaceAll(':', '-')
        .split('.')[0];
    final filePath = p.join(
      directory.path,
      'wholesale_pos_backup_$timestamp.json',
    );

    final file = File(filePath);
    await file.writeAsString(jsonString);

    await db.insert('audit_logs', {
      'id': const Uuid().v4(),
      'user_id': requestingUserId,
      'action': 'BACKUP_EXPORTED',
      'details': 'Backup written to $filePath',
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });

    return filePath;
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\core\utils\data_importer.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../database/local_database.dart';
import 'data_exporter.dart';

class DataImporter {
  static Future<void> importFromJson(
    Database db,
    String filePath, {
    required String requestingUserId,
  }) async {
    final user = await db.query(
      'users',
      columns: ['role', 'is_active'],
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [requestingUserId],
      limit: 1,
    );
    if (user.isEmpty ||
        user.first['role'] != 'OWNER' ||
        user.first['is_active'] != 1) {
      throw Exception('Only an active owner can restore a backup.');
    }
    final file = File(filePath);
    if (!await file.exists()) throw Exception('Backup file not found.');

    final jsonString = await file.readAsString();
    final Map<String, dynamic> data = jsonDecode(jsonString);

    // Schema 8 backups restore directly. Schema 6 backups are accepted and
    // normalized on import (sale-item quantities are converted to the new
    // flexible columns); anything older is rejected rather than guessed at.
    final backupVersion = data['version'];
    if (backupVersion is! int ||
        backupVersion < 6 ||
        backupVersion > LocalDatabase.schemaVersion) {
      throw Exception(
        'Only schema version 6 through ${LocalDatabase.schemaVersion} '
        'backups are supported. Older backups are not guessed at.',
      );
    }
    for (final table in [
      'users',
      'products',
      'sales',
      'sale_items',
      'shifts',
      'cash_movements',
      'audit_logs',
      'devices',
      'counters',
      'settings',
    ]) {
      if (data[table] is! List) {
        throw Exception('Backup is missing the $table table.');
      }
    }
    // The finance tables were introduced in schema 7 and void_requests in
    // schema 8; older backups simply start with none.
    final financeCategories = data['finance_categories'] is List
        ? data['finance_categories'] as List
        : <dynamic>[];
    final financeEntries = data['finance_entries'] is List
        ? data['finance_entries'] as List
        : <dynamic>[];
    final voidRequests = data['void_requests'] is List
        ? data['void_requests'] as List
        : <dynamic>[];

    final requiredFields = <String, List<String>>{
      'users': [
        'id',
        'username',
        'password_hash',
        'salt',
        'role',
        'is_active',
        'created_at',
      ],
      'products': [
        'id',
        'name',
        'code',
        'unit',
        'selling_price_minor_units',
        'is_active',
        'created_at',
        'updated_at',
      ],
      'sales': [
        'id',
        'receipt_number',
        'cashier_id',
        'device_id',
        'subtotal_minor_units',
        'discount_minor_units',
        'total_minor_units',
        'amount_paid_minor_units',
        'change_minor_units',
        'status',
        'created_at',
      ],
      'sale_items': [
        'id',
        'sale_id',
        'product_name_snapshot',
        'unit_price_snapshot_minor_units',
        'line_total_minor_units',
        if (backupVersion >= 7) 'quantity_text',
        if (backupVersion < 7) 'quantity',
      ],
      'shifts': [
        'id',
        'cashier_id',
        'opening_cash_minor_units',
        'status',
        'opened_at',
      ],
      'cash_movements': [
        'id',
        'shift_id',
        'type',
        'amount_minor_units',
        'created_at',
      ],
      'audit_logs': ['id', 'action', 'created_at'],
      'devices': ['id', 'name', 'platform'],
      'counters': ['name', 'value'],
      'settings': ['key', 'value'],
    };
    for (final entry in requiredFields.entries) {
      for (final row in data[entry.key] as List) {
        if (row is! Map ||
            entry.value.any((field) => !row.containsKey(field))) {
          throw Exception('Backup has an invalid ${entry.key} record.');
        }
      }
    }
    const financeRequiredFields = <String, List<String>>{
      'finance_categories': ['id', 'name', 'created_at', 'updated_at'],
      'finance_entries': [
        'id',
        'category_id',
        'source',
        'amount_minor_units',
        'status',
        'entry_date',
        'created_at',
        'created_by',
        'updated_at',
      ],
    };
    for (final entry in financeRequiredFields.entries) {
      final rows = entry.key == 'finance_categories'
          ? financeCategories
          : financeEntries;
      for (final row in rows) {
        if (row is! Map ||
            entry.value.any((field) => !row.containsKey(field))) {
          throw Exception('Backup has an invalid ${entry.key} record.');
        }
      }
    }
    // void_requests is optional (introduced in schema 8). When present its
    // rows reference sales and users, so they are validated here and restored
    // after those parents.
    const voidRequestRequired = [
      'id',
      'sale_id',
      'requested_by',
      'reason',
      'status',
      'created_at',
    ];
    for (final row in voidRequests) {
      if (row is! Map ||
          voidRequestRequired.any((field) => !row.containsKey(field))) {
        throw Exception('Backup has an invalid void_requests record.');
      }
    }

    // Normalize sale items to the schema 7 shape. Rows from schema 6 backups
    // carried an integer quantity; schema 7 rows pass through unchanged.
    final saleItems = (data['sale_items'] as List)
        .map((row) => _normalizeSaleItem(row as Map))
        .toList();

    final users = data['users'] as List;
    if (!users.any(
      (row) =>
          row['id'] == requestingUserId &&
          row['role'] == 'OWNER' &&
          row['is_active'] == 1,
    )) {
      throw Exception(
        'Backup must retain the active owner performing the restore.',
      );
    }

    // Persist a recoverable snapshot before the replacement transaction starts.
    final recoveryPath = await DataExporter.exportToJson(
      db,
      requestingUserId: requestingUserId,
    );

    await db.transaction((txn) async {
      // Clear existing data
      await txn.delete('audit_logs');
      await txn.delete('cash_movements');
      await txn.delete('void_requests');
      await txn.delete('sale_items');
      await txn.delete('sales');
      await txn.delete('shifts');
      await txn.delete('finance_entries');
      await txn.delete('finance_categories');
      await txn.delete('devices');
      await txn.delete('products');
      await txn.delete('settings');
      await txn.delete('counters');
      await txn.delete('users');

      // Insert new data
      for (var row in (data['users'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('users', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['devices'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('devices', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['products'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('products', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['shifts'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('shifts', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['sales'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('sales', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in saleItems) {
        await txn.insert('sale_items', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in voidRequests) {
        await txn.insert('void_requests', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['cash_movements'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('cash_movements', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in financeCategories) {
        await txn.insert('finance_categories', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in financeEntries) {
        await txn.insert('finance_entries', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['audit_logs'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('audit_logs', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['counters'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('counters', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      for (var row in (data['settings'] as List)) {
        await txn.insert('settings', Map<String, dynamic>.from(row));
      }
      final fkErrors = await txn.rawQuery('PRAGMA foreign_key_check');
      if (fkErrors.isNotEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Backup contains broken foreign-key relationships.');
      }
      await txn.insert('audit_logs', {
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'user_id': requestingUserId,
        'action': 'BACKUP_RESTORED',
        'details':
            'Restored validated schema $backupVersion backup; '
            'recovery copy saved at $recoveryPath',
        'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      });
    });
  }

  /// Converts a backed-up sale-item row to the schema 7 shape. Schema 6 rows
  /// stored an integer `quantity`; it becomes both the exact quantity text
  /// and the numeric billing quantity in milliunits. Schema 7 rows already
  /// carry `quantity_text` and pass through unchanged — including a null
  /// billing quantity, which marks a manually entered line amount.
  static Map<String, dynamic> _normalizeSaleItem(Map row) {
    final isLegacy = row['quantity_text'] == null;
    final legacyQuantity = row['quantity'] is int ? row['quantity'] as int : 1;
    return {
      'id': row['id'],
      'sale_id': row['sale_id'],
      'product_id': row['product_id'] ?? 'MANUAL',
      'product_name_snapshot': row['product_name_snapshot'],
      'unit_price_snapshot_minor_units': row['unit_price_snapshot_minor_units'],
      'quantity_text': isLegacy ? '$legacyQuantity' : row['quantity_text'],
      'billing_quantity_milliunits': isLegacy
          ? legacyQuantity * 1000
          : row['billing_quantity_milliunits'],
      'line_discount_minor_units': row['line_discount_minor_units'] ?? 0,
      'line_total_minor_units': row['line_total_minor_units'],
      'notes': row['notes'],
    };
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\core\utils\date_range.dart
==================================================

/// A half-open local-time range `[start, end)` used for reporting periods.
/// All boundaries are computed in the machine's local timezone, matching how
/// `created_at` timestamps are stored (local, no UTC suffix), so string
/// comparisons and `date()` filtering stay consistent at midnight.
class DateRange {
  final DateTime start;
  final DateTime end;

  const DateRange({required this.start, required this.end});

  factory DateRange.today() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final start = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    return DateRange(start: start, end: start.add(const Duration(days: 1)));
  }

  factory DateRange.yesterday() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final start = DateTime(
      now.year,
      now.month,
      now.day,
    ).subtract(const Duration(days: 1));
    return DateRange(start: start, end: start.add(const Duration(days: 1)));
  }

  /// Monday-based week: from Monday 00:00 until next Monday 00:00.
  factory DateRange.thisWeek() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final today = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final start = today.subtract(
      Duration(days: today.weekday - DateTime.monday),
    );
    return DateRange(start: start, end: start.add(const Duration(days: 7)));
  }

  factory DateRange.thisMonth() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final start = DateTime(now.year, now.month, 1);
    return DateRange(
      start: start,
      end: now.month == 12
          ? DateTime(now.year + 1, 1, 1)
          : DateTime(now.year, now.month + 1, 1),
    );
  }

  factory DateRange.thisYear() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    return DateRange(
      start: DateTime(now.year, 1, 1),
      end: DateTime(now.year + 1, 1, 1),
    );
  }

  /// Everything the app has ever recorded. Used for "All time" views.
  factory DateRange.allTime() {
    return DateRange(start: DateTime(2000), end: DateTime(2100));
  }

  /// A custom range where both dates are inclusive calendar days.
  factory DateRange.custom(DateTime startDay, DateTime endDay) {
    final start = DateTime(startDay.year, startDay.month, startDay.day);
    var end = DateTime(endDay.year, endDay.month, endDay.day);
    if (end.isBefore(start)) end = start;
    return DateRange(start: start, end: end.add(const Duration(days: 1)));
  }

  bool contains(DateTime moment) =>
      !moment.isBefore(start) && moment.isBefore(end);
}

/// The period presets offered on the dashboard, history, and report screens.
enum ReportPeriod {
  today('Today'),
  yesterday('Yesterday'),
  thisWeek('This Week'),
  thisMonth('This Month'),
  thisYear('This Year'),
  allTime('All Time'),
  custom('Custom');

  const ReportPeriod(this.label);
  final String label;

  /// Resolves the preset to a concrete local-time range. `custom` returns
  /// null; callers combine it with their own picked dates.
  DateRange? toDateRange() {
    switch (this) {
      case ReportPeriod.today:
        return DateRange.today();
      case ReportPeriod.yesterday:
        return DateRange.yesterday();
      case ReportPeriod.thisWeek:
        return DateRange.thisWeek();
      case ReportPeriod.thisMonth:
        return DateRange.thisMonth();
      case ReportPeriod.thisYear:
        return DateRange.thisYear();
      case ReportPeriod.allTime:
        return DateRange.allTime();
      case ReportPeriod.custom:
        return null;
    }
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\core\utils\money_utils.dart

class MoneyUtils {
  static final RegExp _amountPattern = RegExp(r'^(\d+)(?:\.(\d{0,2}))?$');
  static final RegExp _quantityPattern = RegExp(r'^(\d{1,9})(?:\.(\d{1,3}))?$');

  /// Converts a user input string (e.g., "4500.50") to integer minor units
  /// (e.g., 450050). Returns null when the value is not a plain non-negative
  /// amount with at most two decimal places.
  static int? stringToMinorUnits(String value) {
    final cleaned = value.trim().replaceAll(',', '');
    if (cleaned.isEmpty) return null;
    final match = _amountPattern.firstMatch(cleaned);
    if (match == null) return null;

    final wholeUnits = int.tryParse(match.group(1)!);
    if (wholeUnits == null) return null;
    final fraction = (match.group(2) ?? '').padRight(2, '0');
    final minorUnits = int.tryParse(fraction);
    if (minorUnits == null) return null;

    // Guard against values that would overflow a 64-bit integer.
    if (wholeUnits > 90000000000000) return null;
    return wholeUnits * 100 + minorUnits;
  }

  /// Converts integer minor units (e.g., 450050) to a display string
  /// (e.g., "4500.50") without floating-point arithmetic.
  static String minorUnitsToString(int minorUnits) {
    final isNegative = minorUnits < 0;
    final absValue = minorUnits.abs();
    final wholeUnits = absValue ~/ 100;
    final fraction = (absValue % 100).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    final formatted = '$wholeUnits.$fraction';
    return isNegative ? '-$formatted' : formatted;
  }

  /// Formats minor units as an Ethiopian-birr amount, e.g. `1,250.50 Br`.
  /// This is the app-wide currency format; use it everywhere money is shown.
  static String formatBr(int minorUnits) {
    final plain = minorUnitsToString(minorUnits);
    final isNegative = plain.startsWith('-');
    final unsigned = isNegative ? plain.substring(1) : plain;
    final parts = unsigned.split('.');
    final grouped = _groupThousands(parts[0]);
    final formatted = '$grouped.${parts[1]} Br';
    return isNegative ? '-$formatted' : formatted;
  }

  static String _groupThousands(String digits) {
    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    for (var i = 0; i < digits.length; i++) {
      buffer.write(digits[i]);
      final remaining = digits.length - i - 1;
      if (remaining > 0 && remaining % 3 == 0) buffer.write(',');
    }
    return buffer.toString();
  }

  /// Parses a plain decimal quantity such as "2", "1.5", or "0.25" into
  /// milliunits (1500 means 1.5). Returns null when the text is not a plain
  /// number, which lets callers keep descriptive quantities like
  /// "2 boxes and 1 piece" as text instead of guessing a value.
  static int? stringToMilliunits(String value) {
    final cleaned = value.trim().replaceAll(',', '');
    if (cleaned.isEmpty) return null;
    final match = _quantityPattern.firstMatch(cleaned);
    if (match == null) return null;

    final wholeUnits = int.tryParse(match.group(1)!);
    if (wholeUnits == null) return null;
    final fraction = (match.group(2) ?? '').padRight(3, '0');
    final fractionMilliunits = int.tryParse(fraction);
    if (fractionMilliunits == null) return null;

    // Guard against values that would overflow a 64-bit integer once scaled.
    if (wholeUnits > 9000000) return null;
    return wholeUnits * 1000 + fractionMilliunits;
  }

  /// Converts milliunits (1500 = 1.5) to a short decimal string ("1.5").
  /// Trailing zeros are dropped so "2" stays "2" and "0.25" stays "0.25".
  static String milliunitsToString(int milliunits) {
    final isNegative = milliunits < 0;
    final absValue = milliunits.abs();
    final whole = absValue ~/ 1000;
    var fraction = (absValue % 1000).toString().padLeft(3, '0');
    while (fraction.length > 1 && fraction.endsWith('0')) {
      fraction = fraction.substring(0, fraction.length - 1);
    }
    final formatted = fraction == '0' ? '$whole' : '$whole.$fraction';
    return isNegative ? '-$formatted' : formatted;
  }

  /// Multiplies a unit price by a billing quantity (milliunits) and rounds
  /// half-up to minor units using exact integer math — no floating point.
  /// Example: 500 minor units x 1500 milliunits (1.5) = 750 minor units.
  /// Returns null when inputs are negative or the result would overflow.
  static int? tryLineTotalMinorUnits(
    int unitPriceMinorUnits,
    int billingQuantityMilliunits,
  ) {
    if (unitPriceMinorUnits < 0 || billingQuantityMilliunits < 0) return null;
    if (billingQuantityMilliunits == 0) return 0;

    final product = unitPriceMinorUnits * billingQuantityMilliunits;
    // Detect 64-bit overflow: the division must give back the original price.
    if (product < 0 ||
        product ~/ billingQuantityMilliunits != unitPriceMinorUnits) {
      return null;
    }
    if (product > 9223372036854775807 - 500) return null;
    return (product + 500) ~/ 1000;
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\core\utils\receipt_pdf_generator.dart

import 'package:pdf/pdf.dart';
import 'package:pdf/widgets.dart' as pw;
import 'package:printing/printing.dart';

import '../../features/sales/sale.dart';
import 'money_utils.dart';

class ReceiptPdfGenerator {
  static Future<void> printReceipt(Sale sale) async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();

    pdf.addPage(
      pw.Page(
        pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.roll80,
        build: (pw.Context context) {
          return pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Center(
                child: pw.Text(
                  'WHOLESALE POS',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              pw.Center(child: pw.Text('Sales Receipt')),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
              pw.Text('Date: ${sale.createdAt.toString().split('.')[0]}'),
              if (sale.customerName != null &&
                  sale.customerName!.trim().isNotEmpty)
                pw.Text('Customer: ${sale.customerName}'),
              pw.Divider(),
              ...sale.items.map(
                (item) => pw.Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    pw.Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        pw.Expanded(child: pw.Text(item.productNameSnapshot)),
                        pw.Text(MoneyUtils.formatBr(item.lineTotalMinorUnits)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    pw.Text(
                      '${item.quantityText} @ '
                      '${MoneyUtils.formatBr(item.unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits)}'
                      ' each',
                      style: const pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 8),
                    ),
                    if (item.notes != null && item.notes!.trim().isNotEmpty)
                      pw.Text(
                        'Note: ${item.notes}',
                        style: const pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 8),
                      ),
                    pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              pw.Divider(),
              pw.Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  pw.Text('Subtotal:'),
                  pw.Text(MoneyUtils.formatBr(sale.subtotalMinorUnits)),
                ],
              ),
              pw.Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  pw.Text(
                    'TOTAL:',
                    style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                  pw.Text(
                    MoneyUtils.formatBr(sale.totalMinorUnits),
                    style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 8),
              pw.Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  pw.Text(
                    '${sale.paymentMethod == 'CASH' ? 'Cash' : 'Bank'} Paid:',
                  ),
                  pw.Text(MoneyUtils.formatBr(sale.amountPaidMinorUnits)),
                ],
              ),
              pw.Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  pw.Text('Change:'),
                  pw.Text(MoneyUtils.formatBr(sale.changeMinorUnits)),
                ],
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 20),
              pw.Center(child: pw.Text('Thank you for your business!')),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );

    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (PdfPageFormat format) async => pdf.save(),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';

class AuditLogScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;

  const AuditLogScreen({super.key, required this.saleRepository});

  @override
  State<AuditLogScreen> createState() => _AuditLogScreenState();
}

class _AuditLogScreenState extends State<AuditLogScreen> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _logs = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadLogs();
  }

  Future<void> _loadLogs() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    final logs = await widget.saleRepository.getAuditLogs();
    setState(() {
      _logs = logs;
      _isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Audit Logs'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _loadLogs),
        ],
      ),
      body: ListView.builder(
        itemCount: _logs.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final log = _logs[index];
          final action = log['action'] as String;
          final username = log['username'] as String? ?? 'System';
          final details = log['details'] as String? ?? '';
          final date = DateTime.parse(log['created_at'] as String)
              .toString()
              .split('.')[0];

          Color iconColor = Colors.blue;
          IconData icon = Icons.info;

          if (action == 'SALE_VOIDED') {
            iconColor = Colors.red;
            icon = Icons.cancel;
          } else if (action == 'SALE_COMPLETED') {
            iconColor = Colors.green;
            icon = Icons.check_circle;
          }

          return Card(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
            child: ListTile(
              leading: Icon(icon, color: iconColor),
              title: Text('$action by $username'),
              subtitle: Text('$details\n$date'),
              isThreeLine: true,
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\features\auth\login_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/user.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/user_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

class LoginScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final Function(User) onLoginSuccess;

  const LoginScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.onLoginSuccess,
  });

  @override
  State<LoginScreen> createState() => _LoginScreenState();
}

class _LoginScreenState extends State<LoginScreen> {
  final _usernameController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmController = TextEditingController();
  String? _errorMessage;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _checkingFirstRun = true;
  bool _isFirstRun = false;
  bool _obscurePassword = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _checkFirstRun();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _usernameController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _confirmController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _checkFirstRun() async {
    try {
      final hasUsers = await widget.userRepository.hasUsers();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isFirstRun = !hasUsers;
        _checkingFirstRun = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _checkingFirstRun = false;
        _errorMessage = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final username = _usernameController.text.trim();
    final password = _passwordController.text;

    if (username.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _errorMessage = 'Please enter a username.');
      return;
    }
    if (password.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _errorMessage = 'Please enter a password.');
      return;
    }
    if (_isFirstRun) {
      if (password.length < UserRepository.minimumPasswordLength) {
        setState(
          () => _errorMessage =
              'Password must be at least '
              '${UserRepository.minimumPasswordLength} characters.',
        );
        return;
      }
      if (password != _confirmController.text) {
        setState(() => _errorMessage = 'Passwords do not match.');
        return;
      }
    }

    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _errorMessage = null;
    });

    try {
      final User? user;
      if (_isFirstRun) {
        user = await widget.userRepository.createFirstOwner(username, password);
      } else {
        user = await widget.userRepository.authenticate(username, password);
      }

      if (!mounted) return;
      if (user == null) {
        setState(() => _errorMessage = 'Invalid username or password.');
        return;
      }
      widget.onLoginSuccess(user);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(
        () => _errorMessage = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_checkingFirstRun) {
      return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }

    final title = _isFirstRun
        ? 'Create Owner Account'
        : 'Sign in to your account';
    final buttonLabel = _isFirstRun ? 'Create Account' : 'Sign In';

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: Center(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Container(
            width: 400,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(40),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppTheme.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.storefront, size: 48, color: AppTheme.primary),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 20,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
                if (_isFirstRun) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  const Text(
                    'Set up the first owner account for this business.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 32),
                TextField(
                  controller: _usernameController,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Username'),
                  textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                TextField(
                  controller: _passwordController,
                  obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Password',
                    suffixIcon: IconButton(
                      tooltip: _obscurePassword
                          ? 'Show password'
                          : 'Hide password',
                      icon: Icon(
                        _obscurePassword
                            ? Icons.visibility
                            : Icons.visibility_off,
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                      onPressed: () =>
                          setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
                    ),
                  ),
                  onSubmitted: (_) {
                    if (!_isFirstRun) _submit();
                  },
                ),
                if (_isFirstRun) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextField(
                    controller: _confirmController,
                    obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'Confirm Password',
                    ),
                    onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
                  ),
                ],
                if (_errorMessage != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    _errorMessage!,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppTheme.danger,
                      fontSize: 13,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  height: 48,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _submit,
                    child: _isLoading
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 20,
                            height: 20,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                              strokeWidth: 2,
                              color: Colors.white,
                            ),
                          )
                        : Text(buttonLabel),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: app\lib\features\dashboard\widgets\dashboard_charts.dart
