import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';

/// One calendar-day bucket for the revenue trend chart.
class TrendPoint {
  final String day; // YYYY-MM-DD
  final int count;
  final int totalMinorUnits;

  const TrendPoint({
    required this.day,
    required this.count,
    required this.totalMinorUnits,
  });

  factory TrendPoint.fromRow(Map<String, dynamic> row) {
    return TrendPoint(
      day: row['day'] as String,
      count: (row['count'] as int?) ?? 0,
      totalMinorUnits: (row['total'] as int?) ?? 0,
    );
  }
}

/// Lightweight dependency-free charts (custom widgets only) so the offline
/// Windows build stays reproducible without pulling in fl_chart.
class DashboardPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;
  final Widget child;

  const DashboardPanel({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.child,
    this.subtitle,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              title,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
            if (subtitle != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 2),
              Text(
                subtitle!,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                ),
              ),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            child,
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Vertical bars, one per day, scaled to the busiest day in the range.
class RevenueTrendChart extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<TrendPoint> points;

  const RevenueTrendChart({super.key, required this.points});

  static const int _maxBars = 14;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (points.isEmpty) {
      return _Empty(label: 'No completed sales in this period.');
    }
    // Keep the chart readable: show the most recent days only.
    final shown = points.length > _maxBars
        ? points.sublist(points.length - _maxBars)
        : points;
    final maxTotal = shown.fold<int>(
      0,
      (m, p) => p.totalMinorUnits > m ? p.totalMinorUnits : m,
    );
    final effectiveMax = maxTotal == 0 ? 1 : maxTotal;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          height: 150,
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: shown.map((p) {
              final ratio = (p.totalMinorUnits / effectiveMax).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
              final barHeight = (ratio * 108).clamp(4.0, 108.0);
              return Expanded(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 3),
                  child: Tooltip(
                    message:
                        '${p.day}  •  ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(p.totalMinorUnits)}'
                        '  (${p.count} sale${p.count == 1 ? '' : 's'})',
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          height: barHeight,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                            borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                              top: Radius.circular(3),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),
                        Text(
                          _shortDay(p.day),
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10,
                            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }).toList(),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  String _shortDay(String day) {
    final parts = day.split('-');
    if (parts.length != 3) return day;
    return '${parts[1]}/${parts[2]}';
  }
}

/// A single horizontal bar contrasting cash vs bank collections.
class PaymentSplitBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final int cashMinorUnits;
  final int bankMinorUnits;

  const PaymentSplitBar({
    super.key,
    required this.cashMinorUnits,
    required this.bankMinorUnits,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final total = cashMinorUnits + bankMinorUnits;
    if (total == 0) {
      return const _Empty(label: 'No collections in this period.');
    }
    final cashRatio = cashMinorUnits / total;
    final bankRatio = bankMinorUnits / total;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          child: SizedBox(
            height: 22,
            child: Row(
              children: [
                if (cashMinorUnits > 0)
                  Expanded(
                    flex: (cashRatio * 1000).round().clamp(1, 1000),
                    child: Container(color: AppTheme.success),
                  ),
                if (bankMinorUnits > 0)
                  Expanded(
                    flex: (bankRatio * 1000).round().clamp(1, 1000),
                    child: Container(color: AppTheme.primary),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Row(
          children: [
            _Legend(
              color: AppTheme.success,
              label: 'Cash',
              value: MoneyUtils.formatBr(cashMinorUnits),
              pct: '${(cashRatio * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 24),
            _Legend(
              color: AppTheme.primary,
              label: 'Bank',
              value: MoneyUtils.formatBr(bankMinorUnits),
              pct: '${(bankRatio * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Horizontal bars for the top revenue-earning product names.
class TopProductsBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>>
  rows; // product_name_snapshot + total_revenue

  const TopProductsBar({super.key, required this.rows});

  static const int _maxRows = 6;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final data = rows
        .where((r) => ((r['total_revenue'] as int?) ?? 0) > 0)
        .take(_maxRows)
        .toList();
    if (data.isEmpty) {
      return const _Empty(label: 'No product sales in this period.');
    }
    final maxRevenue = data.fold<int>(0, (m, r) {
      final v = (r['total_revenue'] as int?) ?? 0;
      return v > m ? v : m;
    });
    final effectiveMax = maxRevenue == 0 ? 1 : maxRevenue;

    return Column(
      children: data.map((r) {
        final name = r['product_name_snapshot'] as String? ?? '—';
        final revenue = (r['total_revenue'] as int?) ?? 0;
        final ratio = (revenue / effectiveMax).clamp(0.05, 1.0);
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              SizedBox(
                width: 120,
                child: Text(
                  name,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                  child: SizedBox(
                    height: 16,
                    child: Stack(
                      children: [
                        Positioned.fill(
                          child: Container(
                            color: Theme.of(context)
                                .colorScheme
                                .surfaceContainerHighest,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Align(
                          alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                          child: FractionallySizedBox(
                            widthFactor: ratio,
                            child: Container(
                              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              SizedBox(
                width: 96,
                child: Text(
                  MoneyUtils.formatBr(revenue),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.right,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }
}

class _Legend extends StatelessWidget {
  final Color color;
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final String pct;

  const _Legend({
    required this.color,
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.pct,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 12,
          height: 12,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 6),
        Text('$label  ', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
        Text(
          value,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
        Text(
          '  ($pct)',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Empty extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;

  const _Empty({required this.label});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 80,
      child: Center(
        child: Text(
          label,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 13,
            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\dashboard\dashboard_screen.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/date_range.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';
import '../sales/sale.dart';
import 'widgets/dashboard_charts.dart';

class DashboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;

  const DashboardScreen({super.key, required this.saleRepository});

  @override
  State<DashboardScreen> createState() => DashboardScreenState();
}

class DashboardScreenState extends State<DashboardScreen> {
  Map<String, int> _stats = {};
  List<Sale> _recentSales = [];
  List<TrendPoint> _trend = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _topProducts = [];
  int _prevRevenue = 0;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  ReportPeriod _period = ReportPeriod.today;
  DateTimeRange? _customRange;

  /// Watches for the local calendar date changing so "Today" rolls over at
  /// midnight without deleting or resetting anything — totals are recomputed
  /// by date filtering.
  Timer? _midnightWatcher;
  DateTime _watchedDate = DateTime.now();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    loadDashboard();
    _midnightWatcher = Timer.periodic(const Duration(minutes: 1), (_) {
      final now = DateTime.now();
      if (now.year != _watchedDate.year ||
          now.month != _watchedDate.month ||
          now.day != _watchedDate.day) {
        _watchedDate = now;
        if (_period == ReportPeriod.today ||
            _period == ReportPeriod.yesterday) {
          loadDashboard();
        }
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _midnightWatcher?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  DateRange? get _activeRange {
    if (_period == ReportPeriod.custom) {
      if (_customRange == null) return null;
      return DateRange.custom(_customRange!.start, _customRange!.end);
    }
    return _period.toDateRange();
  }

  Future<void> loadDashboard() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });

    try {
      final range = _activeRange ?? DateRange.today();
      final stats = await widget.saleRepository.getDashboardStats(
        period: _activeRange,
      );
      final recent = await widget.saleRepository.getRecentSales(limit: 6);
      // Comparison against the immediately preceding window of equal length
      // (for "Today" that is yesterday; for "This Week" last week, etc.).
      final duration = range.end.difference(range.start);
      final prevStats = await widget.saleRepository.getDashboardStats(
        period: DateRange(
          start: range.start.subtract(duration),
          end: range.start,
        ),
      );
      final trendRows = await widget.saleRepository.getRevenueTrend(range);
      final topProducts = await widget.saleRepository.getProductSalesSummary(
        period: range,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _stats = stats;
        _recentSales = recent;
        _prevRevenue = prevStats['revenue'] ?? 0;
        _trend = trendRows.map(TrendPoint.fromRow).toList();
        _topProducts = topProducts;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickCustomRange() async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 1, 12, 31),
      initialDateRange:
          _customRange ??
          DateTimeRange(
            start: DateTime(
              now.year,
              now.month,
              now.day,
            ).subtract(const Duration(days: 6)),
            end: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day),
          ),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      setState(() {
        _period = ReportPeriod.custom;
        _customRange = picked;
      });
      loadDashboard();
    }
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading && _stats.isEmpty) {
      return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    }
    if (_error != null && _stats.isEmpty) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(_error!),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            TextButton(onPressed: loadDashboard, child: const Text('Retry')),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Dashboard'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: loadDashboard),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: loadDashboard,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          children: [
            _buildPeriodBar(),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            Wrap(
              spacing: 16,
              runSpacing: 16,
              children: [
                _buildStatCard(
                  'Sales Revenue',
                  MoneyUtils.formatBr(_stats['revenue'] ?? 0),
                  isPrimary: true,
                ),
                _buildStatCard(
                  'Completed Sales',
                  '${_stats['sales_count'] ?? 0}',
                ),
                _buildStatCard(
                  'Cash Collected',
                  MoneyUtils.formatBr(_stats['cash_revenue'] ?? 0),
                ),
                _buildStatCard(
                  'Bank Collected',
                  MoneyUtils.formatBr(_stats['bank_revenue'] ?? 0),
                ),
                _buildStatCard(
                  'Average per Sale',
                  MoneyUtils.formatBr(_stats['average_sale'] ?? 0),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'Revenue from completed sales only. This app does not track '
              'costs, so revenue is not profit.',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                fontSize: 12,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _buildComparisonStrip(),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            DashboardPanel(
              title: 'Revenue Trend',
              subtitle: _periodLabel,
              child: RevenueTrendChart(points: _trend),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraints) {
                final split = DashboardPanel(
                  title: 'Payment Split',
                  subtitle: 'Cash vs bank collections',
                  child: PaymentSplitBar(
                    cashMinorUnits: _stats['cash_revenue'] ?? 0,
                    bankMinorUnits: _stats['bank_revenue'] ?? 0,
                  ),
                );
                final top = DashboardPanel(
                  title: 'Top Products',
                  subtitle: 'By revenue',
                  child: TopProductsBar(rows: _topProducts),
                );
                if (constraints.maxWidth < 800) {
                  return Column(
                    children: [split, const SizedBox(height: 16), top],
                  );
                }
                return Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Expanded(child: split),
                    const SizedBox(width: 16),
                    Expanded(child: top),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraints) {
                final recentCard = _buildRecentSalesCard();
                final statusCard = _buildStatusCard();
                if (constraints.maxWidth < 900) {
                  return Column(
                    children: [
                      recentCard,
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      statusCard,
                    ],
                  );
                }
                return Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Expanded(flex: 2, child: recentCard),
                    const SizedBox(width: 24),
                    Expanded(child: statusCard),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String get _periodLabel {
    if (_period == ReportPeriod.custom) {
      if (_customRange == null) return 'No custom range selected';
      return '${_formatDate(_customRange!.start)} - '
          '${_formatDate(_customRange!.end)}';
    }
    return _period.label;
  }

  /// A compact strip of comparison metrics: revenue vs the previous period,
  /// voids, pending void requests, and average per sale.
  Widget _buildComparisonStrip() {
    final revenue = _stats['revenue'] ?? 0;
    final delta = revenue - _prevRevenue;
    final String deltaText;
    final Color deltaColor;
    if (_prevRevenue == 0 && revenue == 0) {
      deltaText = 'No sales yet';
      deltaColor =
          Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color ?? AppTheme.muted;
    } else if (_prevRevenue == 0) {
      deltaText = 'New (prev: ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(0)})';
      deltaColor = AppTheme.success;
    } else {
      final pct = (delta / _prevRevenue * 100).abs().toStringAsFixed(0);
      final up = delta >= 0;
      deltaText =
          '${up ? '▲' : '▼'} ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(delta.abs())} '
          '($pct% ${up ? 'up' : 'down'})';
      deltaColor = up ? AppTheme.success : AppTheme.danger;
    }

    final voidCount = _stats['void_count'] ?? 0;
    final pendingCount = _stats['pending_void_requests'] ?? 0;

    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
        child: Wrap(
          spacing: 24,
          runSpacing: 12,
          children: [
            _ComparisonItem(
              label: 'vs $_previousPeriodLabel',
              value: deltaText,
              valueColor: deltaColor,
            ),
            _ComparisonItem(
              label: 'Voided Sales',
              value: '$voidCount',
              valueColor: voidCount > 0 ? AppTheme.danger : null,
            ),
            _ComparisonItem(
              label: 'Pending Void Requests',
              value: '$pendingCount',
              valueColor: pendingCount > 0 ? AppTheme.warning : null,
            ),
            _ComparisonItem(
              label: 'Average per Sale',
              value: MoneyUtils.formatBr(_stats['average_sale'] ?? 0),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String get _previousPeriodLabel {
    switch (_period) {
      case ReportPeriod.today:
        return 'yesterday';
      case ReportPeriod.yesterday:
        return '2 days ago';
      case ReportPeriod.thisWeek:
        return 'last week';
      case ReportPeriod.thisMonth:
        return 'last month';
      case ReportPeriod.thisYear:
        return 'last year';
      case ReportPeriod.allTime:
        return 'previous';
      case ReportPeriod.custom:
        return 'previous period';
    }
  }

  Widget _buildPeriodBar() {
    return Wrap(
      spacing: 8,
      runSpacing: 8,
      crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
      children: [
        ...ReportPeriod.values
            .where((p) => p != ReportPeriod.custom)
            .map(
              (period) => ChoiceChip(
                label: Text(period.label),
                selected: _period == period,
                onSelected: (_) {
                  setState(() => _period = period);
                  loadDashboard();
                },
              ),
            ),
        ActionChip(
          avatar: Icon(
            Icons.date_range,
            size: 16,
            color: _period == ReportPeriod.custom
                ? Colors.white
                : AppTheme.foreground,
          ),
          label: Text(
            _period == ReportPeriod.custom && _customRange != null
                ? '${_formatDate(_customRange!.start)} - '
                      '${_formatDate(_customRange!.end)}'
                : 'Custom',
          ),
          onPressed: _pickCustomRange,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  String _saleHeadline(Sale sale) {
    if (sale.items.isEmpty) return 'Sale';
    final names = sale.items.map((item) => item.productNameSnapshot).toList();
    if (names.length == 1) return names.first;
    if (names.length == 2) return '${names[0]}, ${names[1]}';
    return '${names.first} +${names.length - 1} more';
  }

  Widget _buildRecentSalesCard() {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Text(
              'Recent Sales',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          if (_recentSales.isEmpty)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Text(
                'No sales recorded yet.',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                ),
              ),
            )
          else
            ..._recentSales.map(
              (sale) => ListTile(
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 24,
                  vertical: 8,
                ),
                dense: true,
                leading: CircleAvatar(
                  backgroundColor: sale.status == 'VOIDED'
                      ? Colors.grey
                      : Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
                  child: Icon(
                    Icons.shopping_basket_outlined,
                    color: sale.status == 'VOIDED'
                        ? Colors.white
                        : Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
                    size: 20,
                  ),
                ),
                title: Text(
                  _saleHeadline(sale),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
                subtitle: Text(
                  '${sale.createdAt.toString().split('.')[0]}'
                  ' • ${sale.cashierName ?? 'Unknown'}',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                ),
                trailing: Text(
                  MoneyUtils.formatBr(sale.totalMinorUnits),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    decoration: sale.status == 'VOIDED'
                        ? TextDecoration.lineThrough
                        : null,
                    color: sale.status == 'VOIDED'
                        ? Colors.grey
                        : Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusCard() {
    final isOpen = (_stats['is_shift_open'] ?? 0) == 1;
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Register Status',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.circle,
                  color: isOpen ? AppTheme.success : AppTheme.danger,
                  size: 12,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(
                  isOpen ? 'Register Open' : 'Register Closed',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              isOpen
                  ? 'Expected Cash: '
                        '${MoneyUtils.formatBr(_stats['expected_cash'] ?? 0)}'
                  : 'Open the register to start recording cash sales.',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                fontSize: 13,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, {bool isPrimary = false}) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: 240,
      child: Card(
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                value,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 24,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: isPrimary
                      ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary
                      : Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ComparisonItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final Color? valueColor;

  const _ComparisonItem({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    this.valueColor,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Text(
          label,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          value,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 15,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            color: valueColor ?? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/user.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/finance_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';
import 'finance_person_screen.dart';

/// Owner-only overview of people tracked in the private money area. Cashier
/// access is blocked in [FinanceRepository] at the data layer, not just here.
class FinanceOverviewScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final FinanceRepository financeRepository;
  final User currentUser;

  const FinanceOverviewScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.financeRepository,
    required this.currentUser,
  });

  @override
  State<FinanceOverviewScreen> createState() => _FinanceOverviewScreenState();
}

class _FinanceOverviewScreenState extends State<FinanceOverviewScreen> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _summaries = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final summaries = await widget.financeRepository.getCategorySummaries(
        widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _summaries = summaries;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showAddPersonDialog() async {
    final name = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => const _NameDialog(title: 'Add Person'),
    );
    if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty) return;
    try {
      await widget.financeRepository.createCategory(
        ownerId: widget.currentUser.id,
        name: name,
      );
      await _load();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showRenameDialog(Map<String, dynamic> summary) async {
    final name = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _NameDialog(
        title: 'Rename Person',
        initial: summary['name'] as String,
      ),
    );
    if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty) return;
    try {
      await widget.financeRepository.renameCategory(
        ownerId: widget.currentUser.id,
        categoryId: summary['id'] as String,
        newName: name,
      );
      await _load();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  void _showError(Object e) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''))),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _openPerson(Map<String, dynamic> summary) async {
    await Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (context) => FinancePersonScreen(
          financeRepository: widget.financeRepository,
          currentUser: widget.currentUser,
          categoryId: summary['id'] as String,
          personName: summary['name'] as String,
        ),
      ),
    );
    _load();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final totalPending = _summaries.fold<int>(
      0,
      (sum, row) => sum + (row['pending_total'] as int? ?? 0),
    );

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Money Tracking'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _load),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
          ? Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text(_error!),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  TextButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Retry')),
                ],
              ),
            )
          : ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              children: [
                Card(
                  margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            const Icon(Icons.lock_outline, size: 16),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(
                                'Private area — visible only to you. Manual '
                                'records of money taken from banks or other '
                                'sources, organized by person.',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Theme.of(context)
                                      .textTheme
                                      .bodySmall
                                      ?.color,
                                  fontSize: 12,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        Text(
                          'Pending Across Everyone',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                            fontSize: 13,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Text(
                          MoneyUtils.formatBr(totalPending),
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 28,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                if (_summaries.isEmpty)
                  Center(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                      child: Text(
                        'No people yet. Add the names you want to track '
                        'money for.',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                else
                  ..._summaries.map(_buildPersonTile),
              ],
            ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _showAddPersonDialog,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.person_add),
        label: const Text('Add Person'),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPersonTile(Map<String, dynamic> summary) {
    final name = summary['name'] as String;
    final pendingCount = summary['pending_count'] as int? ?? 0;
    final pendingTotal = summary['pending_total'] as int? ?? 0;

    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: ListTile(
        leading: CircleAvatar(
          backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
          child: Text(
            name.isEmpty ? '?' : name[0].toUpperCase(),
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 14),
          ),
        ),
        title: Text(name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
        subtitle: Text(
          pendingCount == 0
              ? 'No pending entries'
              : '$pendingCount pending • ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(pendingTotal)}',
        ),
        trailing: PopupMenuButton<String>(
          onSelected: (value) {
            if (value == 'rename') _showRenameDialog(summary);
          },
          itemBuilder: (context) => const [
            PopupMenuItem(value: 'rename', child: Text('Rename')),
          ],
        ),
        onTap: () => _openPerson(summary),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _NameDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final String title;
  final String? initial;

  const _NameDialog({required this.title, this.initial});

  @override
  State<_NameDialog> createState() => _NameDialogState();
}

class _NameDialogState extends State<_NameDialog> {
  late final TextEditingController _controller;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = TextEditingController(text: widget.initial ?? '');
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submit() {
    final name = _controller.text.trim();
    if (name.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _error = 'A name is required.');
      return;
    }
    Navigator.of(context).pop(name);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: Text(widget.title),
      content: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          TextField(
            controller: _controller,
            autofocus: true,
            decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Name', errorText: _error),
            onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
          ),
          if (widget.title == 'Rename Person')
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
              child: Text(
                'Renaming keeps all existing entries and history.',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        FilledButton(onPressed: _submit, child: const Text('Save')),
      ],
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\finance\finance_person_screen.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/finance_entry.dart';
import '../../core/models/user.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/finance_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';

/// The dedicated page for one person in the owner's private money area.
/// Pending entries disappear from the active list once marked taken, but
/// stay permanently in history and remain searchable.
class FinancePersonScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final FinanceRepository financeRepository;
  final User currentUser;
  final String categoryId;
  final String personName;

  const FinancePersonScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.financeRepository,
    required this.currentUser,
    required this.categoryId,
    required this.personName,
  });

  @override
  State<FinancePersonScreen> createState() => _FinancePersonScreenState();
}

class _FinancePersonScreenState extends State<FinancePersonScreen> {
  List<FinanceEntry> _entries = [];
  Map<String, int> _totals = {'pending_total': 0, 'completed_total': 0};
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  final _searchController = TextEditingController();
  Timer? _searchDebounce;

  String _statusFilter = 'PENDING';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchDebounce?.cancel();
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final entries = await widget.financeRepository.searchEntries(
        ownerId: widget.currentUser.id,
        categoryId: widget.categoryId,
        searchQuery: _searchController.text.trim().isEmpty
            ? null
            : _searchController.text.trim(),
        status: _statusFilter,
      );
      final totals = await widget.financeRepository.getPersonTotals(
        widget.currentUser.id,
        widget.categoryId,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _entries = entries;
        _totals = totals;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  void _onSearchChanged(String value) {
    _searchDebounce?.cancel();
    _searchDebounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 350), _load);
  }

  void _showError(Object e) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''))),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _showAddEntryDialog({FinanceEntry? editing}) async {
    final result = await showDialog<Map<String, dynamic>>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _EntryDialog(initial: editing),
    );
    if (result == null) return;

    try {
      if (editing == null) {
        await widget.financeRepository.addEntry(
          ownerId: widget.currentUser.id,
          categoryId: widget.categoryId,
          source: result['source'] as String,
          amountMinorUnits: result['amount'] as int,
          description: result['description'] as String?,
          entryDate: result['date'] as DateTime,
        );
      } else {
        await widget.financeRepository.updateEntry(
          ownerId: widget.currentUser.id,
          entryId: editing.id,
          source: result['source'] as String,
          amountMinorUnits: result['amount'] as int,
          description: result['description'] as String?,
          entryDate: result['date'] as DateTime,
        );
      }
      await _load();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _markCompleted(FinanceEntry entry) async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Mark as Taken?'),
        content: Text(
          'Mark ${entry.source} (${MoneyUtils.formatBr(entry.amountMinorUnits)}) as taken? It will leave the pending list but stay in history.',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('Mark Taken'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirmed != true) return;

    try {
      await widget.financeRepository.markCompleted(
        ownerId: widget.currentUser.id,
        entryId: entry.id,
      );
      await _load();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(widget.personName)),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          _buildSummary(),
          _buildTabsAndSearch(),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          Expanded(
            child: _isLoading
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : _error != null
                ? Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(_error!),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        TextButton(
                          onPressed: _load,
                          child: const Text('Retry'),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  )
                : _entries.isEmpty
                ? Center(
                    child: Text(
                      _statusFilter == 'PENDING'
                          ? 'No pending entries.'
                          : _statusFilter == 'COMPLETED'
                          ? 'No completed entries yet.'
                          : 'No entries yet.',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                : ListView.builder(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    itemCount: _entries.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) =>
                        _buildEntryCard(_entries[index]),
                  ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () => _showAddEntryDialog(),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
        label: const Text('Add Entry'),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSummary() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Card(
              margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Pending',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                        fontSize: 13,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      MoneyUtils.formatBr(_totals['pending_total'] ?? 0),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 20,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Card(
              margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Taken (Completed)',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                        fontSize: 13,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      MoneyUtils.formatBr(_totals['completed_total'] ?? 0),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 20,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTabsAndSearch() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 0, 24, 16),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          SegmentedButton<String>(
            segments: const [
              ButtonSegment(value: 'PENDING', label: Text('Pending')),
              ButtonSegment(value: 'COMPLETED', label: Text('Completed')),
              ButtonSegment(value: 'ALL', label: Text('All History')),
            ],
            selected: {_statusFilter},
            onSelectionChanged: (selection) {
              setState(() => _statusFilter = selection.first);
              _load();
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          TextField(
            controller: _searchController,
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'Search source or description...',
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
              isDense: true,
              suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isEmpty
                  ? null
                  : IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
                      onPressed: () {
                        _searchController.clear();
                        _load();
                      },
                    ),
            ),
            onChanged: _onSearchChanged,
            onSubmitted: (_) => _load(),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEntryCard(FinanceEntry entry) {
    final isPending = !entry.isCompleted;
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: ListTile(
        leading: Icon(
          isPending ? Icons.schedule : Icons.check_circle_outline,
          color: isPending ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.success,
        ),
        title: Text(
          entry.source,
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
        ),
        subtitle: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('${_formatDate(entry.entryDate)} • ${entry.status}'),
            if (entry.description != null &&
                entry.description!.trim().isNotEmpty)
              Text(
                entry.description!,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              ),
            if (entry.isCompleted && entry.completedAt != null)
              Text(
                'Taken on ${_formatDate(entry.completedAt!)}',
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        trailing: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(
              MoneyUtils.formatBr(entry.amountMinorUnits),
              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            if (isPending) ...[
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              PopupMenuButton<String>(
                onSelected: (value) {
                  if (value == 'complete') _markCompleted(entry);
                  if (value == 'edit') _showAddEntryDialog(editing: entry);
                },
                itemBuilder: (context) => const [
                  PopupMenuItem(
                    value: 'complete',
                    child: Text('Mark as Taken'),
                  ),
                  PopupMenuItem(value: 'edit', child: Text('Edit')),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _EntryDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final FinanceEntry? initial;

  const _EntryDialog({this.initial});

  @override
  State<_EntryDialog> createState() => _EntryDialogState();
}

class _EntryDialogState extends State<_EntryDialog> {
  late final TextEditingController _sourceController;
  late final TextEditingController _amountController;
  late final TextEditingController _descriptionController;
  late DateTime _selectedDate;
  String? _error;
  bool _isSaving = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final initial = widget.initial;
    _sourceController = TextEditingController(text: initial?.source ?? '');
    _amountController = TextEditingController(
      text: initial == null
          ? ''
          : MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(initial.amountMinorUnits),
    );
    _descriptionController = TextEditingController(
      text: initial?.description ?? '',
    );
    _selectedDate = initial?.entryDate ?? DateTime.now();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _sourceController.dispose();
    _amountController.dispose();
    _descriptionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate() async {
    final picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: _selectedDate,
      firstDate: DateTime(2000),
      lastDate: DateTime(2100),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      setState(() => _selectedDate = picked);
    }
  }

  void _submit() {
    final source = _sourceController.text.trim();
    if (source.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _error = 'A source is required (e.g., a bank name).');
      return;
    }
    final amount = MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(_amountController.text);
    if (amount == null || amount <= 0) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Enter a valid amount in Br.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isSaving = true);
    Navigator.of(context).pop({
      'source': source,
      'amount': amount,
      'description': _descriptionController.text.trim(),
      'date': _selectedDate,
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: Text(widget.initial == null ? 'Add Entry' : 'Edit Entry'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 420,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: _sourceController,
              autofocus: widget.initial == null,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Source (e.g., CBE Bank)',
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _amountController,
              keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                decimal: true,
              ),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Amount (Br)'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            InkWell(
              onTap: _pickDate,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
              child: InputDecorator(
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Date',
                  suffixIcon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size: 18),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  '${_selectedDate.year}-'
                  '${_selectedDate.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
                  '${_selectedDate.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}',
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _descriptionController,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Description / Notes (optional)',
              ),
            ),
            if (_error != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                _error!,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.danger, fontSize: 13),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: _isSaving ? null : () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        FilledButton(onPressed: _submit, child: const Text('Save')),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/licensing/license_validator.dart';

class ActivationScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback onActivated;
  final String? errorMessage;
  final Future<String> Function() hardwareIdProvider;

  const ActivationScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.onActivated,
    this.errorMessage,
    this.hardwareIdProvider = LicenseValidator.getHardwareId,
  });

  @override
  State<ActivationScreen> createState() => _ActivationScreenState();
}

class _ActivationScreenState extends State<ActivationScreen> {
  String _hardwareId = 'Loading...';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    widget.hardwareIdProvider().then((id) => setState(() => _hardwareId = id));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Center(

FILE: app\lib\features\products\add_product_dialog.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../core/models/product.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';

/// Dialog for adding or editing a saved name. Saved names are purely
/// optional shortcuts on the POS screen — only the name matters; the
/// default price simply pre-fills the unit price field when tapped.
class SavedNameDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final Product? initial;

  const SavedNameDialog({super.key, this.initial});

  @override
  State<SavedNameDialog> createState() => _SavedNameDialogState();
}

class _SavedNameDialogState extends State<SavedNameDialog> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  late final TextEditingController _nameController;
  late final TextEditingController _priceController;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _nameController = TextEditingController(text: widget.initial?.name ?? '');
    final initialPrice = widget.initial?.sellingPriceMinorUnits ?? 0;
    _priceController = TextEditingController(
      text: initialPrice > 0 ? MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(initialPrice) : '',
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _priceController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submit() {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    final name = _nameController.text.trim();
    final priceText = _priceController.text.trim();
    final priceMinorUnits = priceText.isEmpty
        ? 0
        : MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(priceText);
    if (priceText.isNotEmpty && priceMinorUnits == null) return;

    final now = DateTime.now();
    final existing = widget.initial;
    final product = existing == null
        ? Product(
            // code/unit are legacy required columns; they are auto-generated
            // because saved names never use codes or units.
            id: const Uuid().v4(),
            name: name,
            code: 'SN-${const Uuid().v4().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase()}',
            unit: 'Item',
            sellingPriceMinorUnits: priceMinorUnits ?? 0,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          )
        : Product(
            id: existing.id,
            name: name,
            code: existing.code,
            category: existing.category,
            unit: existing.unit,
            sellingPriceMinorUnits: priceMinorUnits ?? 0,
            isActive: existing.isActive,
            createdAt: existing.createdAt,
            updatedAt: now,
          );

    Navigator.of(context).pop(product);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: Text(widget.initial == null ? 'Add Saved Name' : 'Edit Name'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 400,
        child: Form(
          key: _formKey,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextFormField(
                controller: _nameController,
                autofocus: true,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Name',
                  hintText: 'e.g., Berbere Shiro, Pasta 500g',
                ),
                validator: (v) =>
                    (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) ? 'Required' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _priceController,
                keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                  decimal: true,
                ),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Default Price (Br, optional)',
                  hintText: 'Pre-fills the price when the name is tapped',
                ),
                validator: (v) {
                  if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) return null;
                  if (MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(v) == null) {
                    return 'Enter a valid amount (max 2 decimals)';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                'Saved names are optional suggestions. The cashier can always '
                'type any name directly on the sale screen.',
                style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 12),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        FilledButton(onPressed: _submit, child: const Text('Save')),
      ],
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\products\product_list_screen.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/product.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/product_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';
import 'add_product_dialog.dart';

/// Owner screen for managing saved names. Saved names are optional
/// suggestions on the POS screen — the catalog is never required to make a
/// sale. Deactivated names are kept so they can be brought back later.
class ProductListScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final ProductRepository repository;

  const ProductListScreen({super.key, required this.repository});

  @override
  State<ProductListScreen> createState() => _ProductListScreenState();
}

class _ProductListScreenState extends State<ProductListScreen> {
  List<Product> _activeNames = [];
  List<Product> _inactiveNames = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadNames();
  }

  Future<void> _loadNames() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final active = await widget.repository.getAllActive();
      final inactive = await widget.repository.getAllInactive();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _activeNames = active;
        _inactiveNames = inactive;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = 'Could not load saved names.';
      });
    }
  }

  void _showError(Object e) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''))),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _showAddDialog() async {
    final product = await showDialog<Product>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => const SavedNameDialog(),
    );
    if (product == null) return;
    try {
      await widget.repository.save(product);
      await _loadNames();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showEditDialog(Product product) async {
    final updated = await showDialog<Product>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => SavedNameDialog(initial: product),
    );
    if (updated == null) return;
    try {
      await widget.repository.update(updated);
      await _loadNames();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deactivate(Product product) async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Deactivate Name?'),
        content: Text(
          '"${product.name}" will stop appearing as a suggestion on the POS '
          'screen. Existing sales keep their recorded names.',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('Deactivate'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirmed != true) return;
    try {
      await widget.repository.deactivate(product.id);
      await _loadNames();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _reactivate(Product product) async {
    try {
      await widget.repository.reactivate(product.id);
      await _loadNames();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Saved Names'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _loadNames),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
          ? Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text(_error!),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  TextButton(onPressed: _loadNames, child: const Text('Retry')),
                ],
              ),
            )
          : ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              children: [
                Card(
                  margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        const Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 16),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            'Saved names are optional shortcuts for the POS '
                            'screen — tapping one pre-fills the name and '
                            'default price. Cashiers can always type any '
                            'product name without saving it here.',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: Theme.of(context)
                                  .textTheme
                                  .bodySmall
                                  ?.color,
                              fontSize: 12,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                Text(
                  'ACTIVE NAMES',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                if (_activeNames.isEmpty)
                  Card(
                    margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                      child: Center(
                        child: Text(
                          'No saved names yet. Sales never require them — '
                          'add one only if you want a shortcut.',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                            fontSize: 13,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                else
                  ..._activeNames.map(_buildActiveTile),
                if (_inactiveNames.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Text(
                    'DEACTIVATED',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 11,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ..._inactiveNames.map(_buildInactiveTile),
                ],
              ],
            ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _showAddDialog,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
        label: const Text('Add Name'),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActiveTile(Product product) {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: ListTile(
        leading: const Icon(Icons.label_outline),
        title: Text(
          product.name,
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
        ),
        subtitle: Text(
          product.sellingPriceMinorUnits > 0
              ? 'Default: ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(product.sellingPriceMinorUnits)}'
              : 'No default price',
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
        ),
        trailing: PopupMenuButton<String>(
          onSelected: (value) {
            if (value == 'edit') _showEditDialog(product);
            if (value == 'deactivate') _deactivate(product);
          },
          itemBuilder: (context) => const [
            PopupMenuItem(value: 'edit', child: Text('Edit')),
            PopupMenuItem(value: 'deactivate', child: Text('Deactivate')),
          ],
        ),
        onTap: () => _showEditDialog(product),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInactiveTile(Product product) {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: ListTile(
        dense: true,
        leading: Icon(Icons.label_outline, color: AppTheme.muted),
        title: Text(
          product.name,
          style: TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color),
        ),
        trailing: TextButton.icon(
          onPressed: () => _reactivate(product),
          icon: const Icon(Icons.restore, size: 16),
          label: const Text('Restore'),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\register\register_screen.dart
