import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/user.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/shift_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';
import '../sales/sale.dart';
import '../sales/shift.dart';
import '../sales/void_request.dart';
import 'shift_history_screen.dart';

class RegisterScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final User currentUser;
  final ShiftRepository shiftRepository;
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;
  final VoidCallback onShiftUpdated;

  const RegisterScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.currentUser,
    required this.shiftRepository,
    required this.saleRepository,
    required this.onShiftUpdated,
  });

  @override
  State<RegisterScreen> createState() => RegisterScreenState();
}

class RegisterScreenState extends State<RegisterScreen> {
  Shift? _activeShift;
  Map<String, int>? _summary;
  List<Sale> _voids = [];
  List<VoidRequest> _pendingRequests = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  bool _isProcessing = false;
  bool _voidsVerified = false;
  String? _loadError;

  final _openingCashController = TextEditingController();
  final _actualCashController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    loadData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _openingCashController.dispose();
    _actualCashController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> loadData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _loadError = null;
    });

    try {
      final shift = await widget.shiftRepository.getActiveShift();
      if (shift == null) {
        if (!mounted) return;
        setState(() {
          _activeShift = null;
          _summary = null;
          _voids = [];
          _voidsVerified = false;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
        return;
      }

      final summary = await widget.shiftRepository.getShiftSummary(shift);
      final voids = await widget.saleRepository.getVoidedSalesForShift(
        shift.id,
      );
      final isOwner = widget.currentUser.role == 'OWNER';
      final pending = isOwner
          ? await widget.saleRepository.getPendingVoidRequests()
          : <VoidRequest>[];

      final oldVoidIds = _voids.map((sale) => sale.id).toSet();
      final newVoidIds = voids.map((sale) => sale.id).toSet();
      if (!oldVoidIds.containsAll(newVoidIds) ||
          !newVoidIds.containsAll(oldVoidIds)) {
        _voidsVerified = false;
      }

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _activeShift = shift;
        _summary = summary;
        _voids = voids;
        _pendingRequests = pending;
        if (voids.isEmpty) _voidsVerified = true;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _loadError = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  void _showMessage(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
  }

  Future<void> _openRegister() async {
    if (_isProcessing) return;
    final cash = MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(_openingCashController.text);
    if (cash == null) {
      _showMessage('Please enter a valid starting cash amount.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isProcessing = true);
    try {
      await widget.shiftRepository.openShift(widget.currentUser.id, cash);
      widget.onShiftUpdated();
      _openingCashController.clear();
      await loadData();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        _showMessage(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _closeRegister() async {
    if (_activeShift == null || _isProcessing) return;
    if (!_voidsVerified) {
      _showMessage('You must verify the voided payments before closing.');
      return;
    }

    final actualCash = MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(
      _actualCashController.text,
    );
    if (actualCash == null) {
      _showMessage('Please enter the actual cash counted.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isProcessing = true);
    try {
      await widget.shiftRepository.closeShift(
        _activeShift!,
        actualCash,
        userId: widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      widget.onShiftUpdated();
      _actualCashController.clear();
      await loadData();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        _showMessage(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showCashMovementDialog() async {
    if (_activeShift == null) return;
    final result = await showDialog<Map<String, dynamic>>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => const _CashMovementDialog(),
    );
    if (result == null || !mounted) return;

    try {
      await widget.shiftRepository.addCashMovement(
        _activeShift!.id,
        result['type'] as String,
        result['amount'] as int,
        result['reason'] as String,
        userId: widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      await loadData();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        _showMessage(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _approveVoidRequest(VoidRequest request) async {
    final amount = request.sale == null
        ? ''
        : ' of ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(request.sale!.totalMinorUnits)}';
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Approve void?'),
        content: Text(
          'This will void the sale$amount and remove it from revenue. '
          'The cashier${request.requesterName == null ? '' : ' (${request.requesterName})'} '
          'asked because: ${request.reason}',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
            child: const Text('Approve void'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirmed != true || !mounted) return;
    try {
      await widget.saleRepository.approveVoidRequest(
        request.id,
        widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      widget.onShiftUpdated();
      await loadData();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showMessage(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _rejectVoidRequest(VoidRequest request) async {
    try {
      await widget.saleRepository.rejectVoidRequest(
        request.id,
        widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      await loadData();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showMessage(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    if (_loadError != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(_loadError!),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            TextButton(onPressed: loadData, child: const Text('Retry')),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Cash Register'),
        actions: [
          if (widget.currentUser.role == 'OWNER')
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.receipt_long),
              tooltip: 'Register History',
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (context) => ShiftHistoryScreen(
                    shiftRepository: widget.shiftRepository,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          if (_activeShift != null)
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.add_card),
              tooltip: 'Add Cash Movement',
              onPressed: _showCashMovementDialog,
            ),
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: loadData),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: _activeShift == null
            ? _buildOpenRegisterCard()
            : _buildActiveRegisterCard(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildOpenRegisterCard() {
    return Center(
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 500),
        child: Card(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const Text(
                  'Open Register',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                const Text(
                  'Enter the starting cash in the drawer to begin your shift.',
                  style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                TextField(
                  controller: _openingCashController,
                  keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                    decimal: true,
                  ),
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Starting Cash (Br)',
                  ),
                  onSubmitted: (_) => _openRegister(),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _isProcessing ? null : _openRegister,
                    child: _isProcessing
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 20,
                            height: 20,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                              strokeWidth: 2,
                              color: Colors.white,
                            ),
                          )
                        : const Text('Open Register'),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActiveRegisterCard() {
    final expectedCash = _summary!['expected_cash']!;
    final cashSales = _summary!['cash_sales']!;
    final bankSales = _summary!['bank_sales']!;
    final deposits = _summary!['deposits']!;
    final withdrawals = _summary!['withdrawals']!;
    final cashRefunds = _summary!['cash_refunds'] ?? 0;

    final actualCash =
        MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(_actualCashController.text) ?? 0;
    final discrepancy = actualCash - expectedCash;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Wrap(
          spacing: 16,
          runSpacing: 16,
          children: [
            _buildStatCard('Opening Cash', _activeShift!.openingCashMinorUnits),
            _buildStatCard('Cash Sales', cashSales),
            _buildStatCard('Bank Sales', bankSales),
            _buildStatCard('Withdrawals', withdrawals),
            _buildStatCard('Deposits', deposits),
            _buildStatCard('Expected Cash', expectedCash, isPrimary: true),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        _buildExpectedCashBreakdown(
          opening: _activeShift!.openingCashMinorUnits,
          cashSales: cashSales,
          deposits: deposits,
          withdrawals: withdrawals,
          cashRefunds: cashRefunds,
          expectedCash: expectedCash,
        ),
        if (_pendingRequests.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          _buildPendingRequestsCard(),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, constraints) {
            final closeCard = _buildCloseCard(discrepancy);
            final voidsCard = _buildVoidsCard();
            if (constraints.maxWidth < 900) {
              return Column(
                children: [closeCard, const SizedBox(height: 24), voidsCard],
              );
            }
            return Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Expanded(flex: 2, child: closeCard),
                const SizedBox(width: 24),
                Expanded(child: voidsCard),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildExpectedCashBreakdown({
    required int opening,
    required int cashSales,
    required int deposits,
    required int withdrawals,
    required int cashRefunds,
    required int expectedCash,
  }) {
    Widget line(String label, int amount, {bool emphasize = false}) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13,
                color: emphasize ? null : AppTheme.muted,
                fontWeight: emphasize ? FontWeight.bold : null,
              ),
            ),
            Text(
              MoneyUtils.formatBr(amount),
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: emphasize ? FontWeight.bold : null,
                color: emphasize ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary : null,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'How Expected Cash is calculated',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            line('Opening Cash', opening),
            line('+ Cash Sales', cashSales),
            line('+ Deposits', deposits),
            line('- Withdrawals', -withdrawals),
            line('- Cash refunds for other-shift sales', -cashRefunds),
            const Divider(height: 20),
            line('= Expected Cash', expectedCash, emphasize: true),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text(
              'A sale voided within this same shift nets to zero and is not '
              'subtracted again; only refunds for sales from other shifts '
              'reduce the drawer.',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPendingRequestsCard() {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                const Icon(
                  Icons.pending_actions,
                  color: AppTheme.warning,
                  size: 20,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),

                  'Pending Void Requests',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                ),
                const Spacer(),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 8,
                    vertical: 2,
                  ),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppTheme.warning,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    '${_pendingRequests.length}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      fontSize: 12,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text(
              'Cashier void requests awaiting your decision.',
              style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
            ),
            const Divider(height: 24),
            ..._pendingRequests.map(
              (request) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                child: Container(

                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        request.sale == null
                            ? 'Sale ${request.saleId}'
                            : '${MoneyUtils.formatBr(request.sale!.totalMinorUnits)}'
                                  ' • ${request.sale!.paymentMethod}',
                        style: const TextStyle(

                          fontSize: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        'Requested by ${request.requesterName ?? 'unknown'}'
                        ' • ${_formatTime(request.createdAt)}',

                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        'Reason: ${request.reason}',
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                        children: [
                          TextButton(
                            onPressed: () => _rejectVoidRequest(request),
                            child: const Text('Reject'),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          FilledButton(
                            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                              backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
                            ),
                            onPressed: () => _approveVoidRequest(request),
                            child: const Text('Approve void'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCloseCard(int discrepancy) {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Close Register',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text(
              'Count the physical cash in the drawer and enter the total below.',
              style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            TextField(
              controller: _actualCashController,
              keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                decimal: true,
              ),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Actual Cash in Drawer (Br)',
              ),
              onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
              onSubmitted: (_) => _closeRegister(),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    'Discrepancy',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    MoneyUtils.formatBr(discrepancy),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: discrepancy == 0
                          ? AppTheme.success
                          : AppTheme.danger,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: _voidsVerified ? AppTheme.danger : null,
                ),
                onPressed: _voidsVerified && !_isProcessing
                    ? _closeRegister
                    : null,
                child: _isProcessing
                    ? const SizedBox(
                        width: 20,
                        height: 20,
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                          strokeWidth: 2,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      )
                    : const Text('Close Register'),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVoidsCard() {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Row(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
                  color: AppTheme.danger,
                  size: 20,
                ),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(
                  'Voided Payments',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text(
              'Verify these voids before closing.',
              style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
            ),
            const Divider(height: 24),
            if (_voids.isEmpty)
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                child: Text(
                  'No voids during this shift.',
                  style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
                ),
              )
            else ...[
              ..._voids.map(
                (voidedSale) => Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                      border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        const Icon(
                          Icons.cancel,
                          color: AppTheme.danger,
                          size: 16,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                '${MoneyUtils.formatBr(voidedSale.totalMinorUnits)}'
                                ' • ${voidedSale.paymentMethod}',
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 13,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (voidedSale.voidReason != null &&
                                  voidedSale.voidReason!.trim().isNotEmpty)
                                Text(
                                  'Reason: ${voidedSale.voidReason}',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 11,
                                    color: AppTheme.muted,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              if (voidedSale.voidedAt != null)
                                Text(
                                  'Voided at ${_formatTime(voidedSale.voidedAt!)}',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 11,
                                    color: AppTheme.muted,
                                  ),
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const Divider(),
              CheckboxListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                title: const Text(
                  'I have reviewed these voids',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                ),
                value: _voidsVerified,
                onChanged: (value) =>
                    setState(() => _voidsVerified = value ?? false),
                controlAffinity: ListTileControlAffinity.leading,
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _formatTime(DateTime time) {
    return '${time.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:'
        '${time.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(
    String title,
    int amountMinorUnits, {
    bool isPrimary = false,
  }) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: 180,
      child: Card(
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                MoneyUtils.formatBr(amountMinorUnits),
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: isPrimary
                      ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary
                      : Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _CashMovementDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const _CashMovementDialog();

  @override
  State<_CashMovementDialog> createState() => _CashMovementDialogState();
}

class _CashMovementDialogState extends State<_CashMovementDialog> {
  final _amountController = TextEditingController();
  final _reasonController = TextEditingController();
  String _type = 'WITHDRAWAL';
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _amountController.dispose();
    _reasonController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submit() {
    final amount = MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(_amountController.text);
    final reason = _reasonController.text.trim();
    if (amount == null || amount <= 0) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Enter a valid amount.');
      return;
    }
    if (reason.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _error = 'A reason or reference is required.');
      return;
    }
    Navigator.of(context)
        .pop({'type': _type, 'amount': amount, 'reason': reason});
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Add Cash Movement'),
      content: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          SegmentedButton<String>(
            segments: const [
              ButtonSegment(
                value: 'WITHDRAWAL',
                label: Text('Withdrawal'),
                icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_upward),
              ),
              ButtonSegment(
                value: 'DEPOSIT',
                label: Text('Deposit'),
                icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_downward),
              ),
            ],
            selected: {_type},
            onSelectionChanged: (selection) =>
                setState(() => _type = selection.first),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          TextField(
            controller: _amountController,
            autofocus: true,
            keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
            decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Amount (Br)'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          TextField(
            controller: _reasonController,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Reason / Reference'),
            onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
          ),
          if (_error != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              _error!,
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.danger, fontSize: 13),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        FilledButton(onPressed: _submit, child: const Text('Save')),
      ],
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\register\shift_history_screen.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/repositories/shift_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/date_range.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';
import '../sales/shift.dart';

/// A searchable history of cash-register sessions (shifts). Once a shift is
/// closed the live register no longer shows it, so this screen lets the owner
/// look back at any day: opening cash, expected vs counted, the drawer
/// discrepancy, who ran it, and the full breakdown of how the cash moved.
class ShiftHistoryScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final ShiftRepository shiftRepository;

  const ShiftHistoryScreen({super.key, required this.shiftRepository});

  @override
  State<ShiftHistoryScreen> createState() => _ShiftHistoryScreenState();
}

class _ShiftHistoryScreenState extends State<ShiftHistoryScreen> {
  List<Shift> _shifts = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  ReportPeriod _period = ReportPeriod.allTime;
  DateTimeRange? _customRange;
  String _status = 'ALL';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  DateRange? get _activeRange {
    if (_period == ReportPeriod.custom) {
      if (_customRange == null) return null;
      return DateRange.custom(_customRange!.start, _customRange!.end);
    }
    return _period.toDateRange();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final shifts = await widget.shiftRepository.getShiftHistory(
        period: _activeRange,
        status: _status == 'ALL' ? null : _status,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _shifts = shifts;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickCustomRange() async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 1, 12, 31),
      initialDateRange:
          _customRange ??
          DateTimeRange(
            start: DateTime(
              now.year,
              now.month,
              now.day,
            ).subtract(const Duration(days: 30)),
            end: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day),
          ),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      setState(() {
        _period = ReportPeriod.custom;
        _customRange = picked;
      });
      _load();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Register History'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _load),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 16, 24, 8),
            child: _buildFilters(),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          Expanded(child: _buildBody()),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFilters() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Wrap(
          spacing: 8,
          runSpacing: 8,
          children: [
            ...ReportPeriod.values
                .where((p) => p != ReportPeriod.custom)
                .map(
                  (period) => ChoiceChip(
                    label: Text(period.label),
                    selected: _period == period,
                    onSelected: (_) {
                      setState(() => _period = period);
                      _load();
                    },
                  ),
                ),
            ActionChip(
              avatar: Icon(
                Icons.date_range,
                size: 16,
                color: _period == ReportPeriod.custom
                    ? Colors.white
                    : AppTheme.foreground,
              ),
              label: Text(
                _period == ReportPeriod.custom && _customRange != null
                    ? '${_formatDate(_customRange!.start)} - '
                          '${_formatDate(_customRange!.end)}'
                    : 'Custom',
              ),
              onPressed: _pickCustomRange,
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Row(
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Status:  ',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.muted),
            ),
            _statusChip('All', 'ALL'),
            _statusChip('Open', 'OPEN'),
            _statusChip('Closed', 'CLOSED'),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _statusChip(String label, String value) {
    final selected = _status == value;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
      child: ChoiceChip(
        label: Text(label),
        selected: selected,
        onSelected: (_) {
          setState(() => _status = value);
          _load();
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBody() {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    }
    if (_error != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(_error!),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            TextButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Retry')),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }
    if (_shifts.isEmpty) {
      return Center(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Text(
            _period == ReportPeriod.allTime && _status == 'ALL'
                ? 'No register sessions recorded yet.'
                : 'No register sessions match these filters.',
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style: TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    return ListView.separated(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
      itemCount: _shifts.length,
      separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(height: 1),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) => _buildRow(_shifts[index]),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRow(Shift shift) {
    final isOpen = shift.status == 'OPEN';
    final discrepancy = shift.discrepancy;
    return ListTile(
      onTap: () => _showDetail(shift),
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 8),
      leading: CircleAvatar(
        backgroundColor: (isOpen ? AppTheme.success : AppTheme.muted)
            .withValues(alpha: 0.15),
        child: Icon(
          isOpen ? Icons.play_circle_outline : Icons.check_circle_outline,
          color: isOpen ? AppTheme.success : AppTheme.muted,
        ),
      ),
      title: Row(
        children: [
          Text(
            _dayLabel(shift.openedAt),
            style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, fontSize: 14),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(
            '${_formatTime(shift.openedAt)}'
            '${shift.closedAt != null ? ' - ${_formatTime(shift.closedAt!)}' : ' - open'}',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      subtitle: Text(
        'Cashier: ${shift.cashierName ?? 'Unknown'}',
        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
      ),
      trailing: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          Text(
            isOpen
                ? 'Expected ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(_liveExpected(shift))}'
                : 'Counted ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(shift.closingCashMinorUnits ?? 0)}',
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
          ),
          if (!isOpen && discrepancy != null)
            Text(
              discrepancy == 0
                  ? 'Balanced'
                  : 'Diff ${MoneyUtils.formatBr(discrepancy)}',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: discrepancy == 0 ? AppTheme.success : AppTheme.danger,
              ),
            )
          else if (isOpen)
            const Text(
              'Open',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.success),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Expected cash is only stored when a shift closes. For a still-open shift
  /// the list shows nothing stored, so fall back to the opening figure.
  int _liveExpected(Shift shift) => shift.expectedCashMinorUnits ?? 0;

  Future<void> _showDetail(Shift shift) async {
    Map<String, int> summary;
    try {
      summary = await widget.shiftRepository.getShiftSummary(shift);
    } catch (_) {
      summary = const {};
    }
    if (!mounted) return;

    final isOpen = shift.status == 'OPEN';
    final discrepancy = shift.discrepancy;
    final expected = isOpen
        ? (summary['expected_cash'] ?? 0)
        : (shift.expectedCashMinorUnits ?? summary['expected_cash'] ?? 0);

    await showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('Register ${isOpen ? 'Open' : 'Closed'}'),
        content: SizedBox(
          width: 420,
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                _infoRow('Date', _formatDate(shift.openedAt)),
                _infoRow(
                  'Opened',
                  '${_formatDate(shift.openedAt)} ${_formatTime(shift.openedAt)}',
                ),
                if (shift.closedAt != null)
                  _infoRow(
                    'Closed',
                    '${_formatDate(shift.closedAt!)} ${_formatTime(shift.closedAt!)}',
                  ),
                _infoRow('Cashier', shift.cashierName ?? 'Unknown'),
                const Divider(height: 24),
                const Text(
                  'Cash drawer breakdown',
                  style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                _breakdownRow('Opening cash', shift.openingCashMinorUnits),
                _breakdownRow('+ Cash sales', summary['cash_sales'] ?? 0),
                _breakdownRow('+ Deposits', summary['deposits'] ?? 0),
                _breakdownRow('- Withdrawals', -(summary['withdrawals'] ?? 0)),
                _breakdownRow(
                  '- Cash refunds (other shifts)',
                  -(summary['cash_refunds'] ?? 0),
                ),
                _breakdownRow('= Expected cash', expected, emphasize: true),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                _breakdownRow(
                  'Bank sales (not in drawer)',
                  summary['bank_sales'] ?? 0,
                ),
                if ((summary['voids'] ?? 0) > 0)
                  _breakdownRow('Voids attributed to this shift', summary['voids']!),
                if (!isOpen) ...[
                  const Divider(height: 24),
                  _breakdownRow(
                    'Counted cash at close',
                    shift.closingCashMinorUnits ?? 0,
                  ),
                  _breakdownRow(
                    'Discrepancy (counted - expected)',
                    discrepancy ?? 0,
                    emphasize: true,
                    color: (discrepancy ?? 0) == 0
                        ? AppTheme.success
                        : AppTheme.danger,
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: const Text('Close'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoRow(String label, String value) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.muted),
          ),
          Flexible(
            child: Text(
              value,
              textAlign: TextAlign.right,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _breakdownRow(
    String label,
    int amount, {
    bool emphasize = false,
    Color? color,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: emphasize ? FontWeight.bold : null,
                color: emphasize ? null : AppTheme.muted,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            MoneyUtils.formatBr(amount),
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 13,
              fontWeight: emphasize ? FontWeight.bold : null,
              color: color ??
                  (emphasize ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary : null),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  String _formatTime(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:'
        '${date.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  String _dayLabel(DateTime date) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final today = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final day = DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day);
    final diff = today.difference(day).inDays;
    if (diff == 0) return 'Today';
    if (diff == 1) return 'Yesterday';
    return _formatDate(date);
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/date_range.dart';
import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';

class ReportsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;

  const ReportsScreen({super.key, required this.saleRepository});

  @override
  State<ReportsScreen> createState() => ReportsScreenState();
}

class ReportsScreenState extends State<ReportsScreen> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _products = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _cashiers = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  ReportPeriod _period = ReportPeriod.thisMonth;
  DateTimeRange? _customRange;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    loadReports();
  }

  DateRange? get _activeRange {
    if (_period == ReportPeriod.custom) {
      if (_customRange == null) return null;
      return DateRange.custom(_customRange!.start, _customRange!.end);
    }
    return _period.toDateRange();
  }

  Future<void> loadReports() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final results = await Future.wait([
        widget.saleRepository.getProductSalesSummary(period: _activeRange),
        widget.saleRepository.getCashierPerformance(period: _activeRange),
      ]);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _products = results[0];
        _cashiers = results[1];
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickCustomRange() async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 1, 12, 31),
      initialDateRange:
          _customRange ??
          DateTimeRange(
            start: DateTime(
              now.year,
              now.month,
              now.day,
            ).subtract(const Duration(days: 6)),
            end: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day),
          ),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      setState(() {
        _period = ReportPeriod.custom;
        _customRange = picked;
      });
      loadReports();
    }
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    if (_error != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(_error!),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            TextButton(onPressed: loadReports, child: const Text('Retry')),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Reports'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: loadReports),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            _buildPeriodBar(),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'Revenue figures come from completed sales only. Voided sales '
              'are excluded.',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                fontSize: 12,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraints) {
                final productsCard = _buildProductsCard();
                final cashiersCard = _buildCashiersCard();
                if (constraints.maxWidth < 1000) {
                  return Column(
                    children: [
                      productsCard,
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      cashiersCard,
                    ],
                  );
                }
                return Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Expanded(child: productsCard),
                    const SizedBox(width: 24),
                    Expanded(child: cashiersCard),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPeriodBar() {
    return Wrap(
      spacing: 8,
      runSpacing: 8,
      crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
      children: [
        ...ReportPeriod.values
            .where((p) => p != ReportPeriod.custom)
            .map(
              (period) => ChoiceChip(
                label: Text(period.label),
                selected: _period == period,
                onSelected: (_) {
                  setState(() => _period = period);
                  loadReports();
                },
              ),
            ),
        ActionChip(
          avatar: Icon(
            Icons.date_range,
            size: 16,
            color: _period == ReportPeriod.custom
                ? Colors.white
                : AppTheme.foreground,
          ),
          label: Text(
            _period == ReportPeriod.custom && _customRange != null
                ? '${_formatDate(_customRange!.start)} - '
                      '${_formatDate(_customRange!.end)}'
                : 'Custom',
          ),
          onPressed: _pickCustomRange,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProductsCard() {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                const Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'Top Products',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          if (_products.isEmpty)
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Text(
                'No completed sales in this period.',
                style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
              ),
            )
          else
            SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: DataTable(
                columns: const [
                  DataColumn(label: Text('Product')),
                  DataColumn(label: Text('Qty'), numeric: true),
                  DataColumn(label: Text('Revenue'), numeric: true),
                ],
                rows: _products.map((row) {
                  final revenue = (row['total_revenue'] as int?) ?? 0;
                  final qtyMilliunits =
                      (row['total_qty_milliunits'] as int?) ?? 0;
                  final lineCount = (row['line_count'] as int?) ?? 0;
                  final manualLines = qtyMilliunits == 0 && lineCount > 0;
                  return DataRow(
                    cells: [
                      DataCell(Text(row['product_name_snapshot'] as String)),
                      DataCell(
                        Text(
                          manualLines
                              ? '—'
                              : MoneyUtils.milliunitsToString(qtyMilliunits),
                        ),
                      ),
                      DataCell(Text(MoneyUtils.formatBr(revenue))),
                    ],
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCashiersCard() {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.people_outline,
                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                const Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'Cashier Performance',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          if (_cashiers.isEmpty)
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Text(
                'No completed sales in this period.',
                style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
              ),
            )
          else
            SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: DataTable(
                columns: const [
                  DataColumn(label: Text('Cashier')),
                  DataColumn(label: Text('Sales'), numeric: true),
                  DataColumn(label: Text('Revenue'), numeric: true),
                ],
                rows: _cashiers.map((row) {
                  final revenue = (row['total_revenue'] as int?) ?? 0;
                  return DataRow(
                    cells: [
                      DataCell(Text(row['username'] as String)),
                      DataCell(Text('${row['total_sales'] ?? 0}')),
                      DataCell(Text(MoneyUtils.formatBr(revenue))),
                    ],
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\sales\cart.dart
==================================================

import 'cart_item.dart';

class Cart {
  final List<CartItem> _items = [];

  List<CartItem> get items => List.unmodifiable(_items);

  /// Adds a line exactly as entered. Lines are never merged because each one
  /// keeps its own quantity description ("2 boxes" stays separate from
  /// "1 kunital") and possibly its own manual amount.
  void addItem(CartItem item) {
    _items.add(item);
  }

  void removeItem(String id) {
    _items.removeWhere((i) => i.id == id);
  }

  /// Replaces a line in place, keeping its position in the order.
  void replaceItem(CartItem item) {
    final index = _items.indexWhere((i) => i.id == item.id);
    if (index >= 0) _items[index] = item;
  }

  int get subtotalMinorUnits {
    return _items.fold(0, (sum, item) => sum + item.lineTotalMinorUnits);
  }

  int get discountMinorUnits => 0;

  int get totalMinorUnits => subtotalMinorUnits;

  void clear() {
    _items.clear();
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\sales\cart_item.dart
==================================================

import '../../core/utils/money_utils.dart';

class CartItem {
  final String id;
  final String productId;
  final String name;
  final int unitPriceMinorUnits;

  /// The quantity description exactly as typed, e.g. "2 boxes and 1 piece".
  final String quantityText;

  /// Numeric billing quantity in milliunits (1500 = 1.5). Null when the line
  /// total was entered manually.
  final int? billingQuantityMilliunits;

  /// Manually entered line amount. When set, it overrides the
  /// price x quantity calculation so descriptive quantities are never
  /// silently turned into numbers.
  final int? manualLineTotalMinorUnits;

  final String? notes;

  CartItem({
    required this.id,
    this.productId = 'MANUAL',
    required this.name,
    required this.unitPriceMinorUnits,
    required this.quantityText,
    this.billingQuantityMilliunits,
    this.manualLineTotalMinorUnits,
    this.notes,
  });

  bool get usesManualLineTotal => manualLineTotalMinorUnits != null;

  int get lineTotalMinorUnits {
    if (manualLineTotalMinorUnits != null) return manualLineTotalMinorUnits!;
    final milliunits = billingQuantityMilliunits;
    if (milliunits == null) {
      throw StateError('Cart item needs a billing quantity or manual total.');
    }
    final total = MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(
      unitPriceMinorUnits,
      milliunits,
    );
    if (total == null) {
      throw StateError('Line total is too large.');
    }
    return total;
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\sales\sale.dart
==================================================

import 'sale_item.dart';

class Sale {
  final String id;
  final String receiptNumber;
  final String cashierId;
  final String deviceId;
  final String shiftId;
  final String? customerName;
  final String paymentMethod;
  final int subtotalMinorUnits;
  final int discountMinorUnits;
  final int totalMinorUnits;
  final int amountPaidMinorUnits;
  final int changeMinorUnits;
  final String status;
  final String? voidReason;
  final DateTime? voidedAt;
  final String? voidedBy;
  final String? voidedShiftId;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime? syncedAt;

  /// Username of the cashier who recorded the sale. Hydrated from the users
  /// table for display only; never persisted on the sale row itself.
  final String? cashierName;

  /// True when a cashier has filed a not-yet-actioned void request against this
  /// sale. Hydrated from `void_requests` for display only; never persisted on
  /// the sale row itself.
  final bool hasPendingVoidRequest;

  final List<SaleItem> items;

  Sale({
    required this.id,
    required this.receiptNumber,
    required this.cashierId,
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.shiftId,
    this.customerName,
    this.paymentMethod = 'CASH',
    required this.subtotalMinorUnits,
    required this.discountMinorUnits,
    required this.totalMinorUnits,
    required this.amountPaidMinorUnits,
    required this.changeMinorUnits,
    this.status = 'COMPLETED',
    this.voidReason,
    this.voidedAt,
    this.voidedBy,
    this.voidedShiftId,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.syncedAt,
    this.cashierName,
    this.hasPendingVoidRequest = false,
    required this.items,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'receipt_number': receiptNumber,
      'cashier_id': cashierId,
      'device_id': deviceId,
      'shift_id': shiftId,
      'customer_name': customerName,
      'payment_method': paymentMethod,
      'subtotal_minor_units': subtotalMinorUnits,
      'discount_minor_units': discountMinorUnits,
      'total_minor_units': totalMinorUnits,
      'amount_paid_minor_units': amountPaidMinorUnits,
      'change_minor_units': changeMinorUnits,
      'status': status,
      'void_reason': voidReason,
      'voided_at': voidedAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'voided_by': voidedBy,
      'voided_shift_id': voidedShiftId,
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'synced_at': syncedAt?.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\lib\features\sales\sales_history_screen.dart
