import 'package:file_selector/file_selector.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

import '../../core/database/local_database.dart';
import '../../core/models/user.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/user_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/data_exporter.dart';
import '../../core/utils/data_importer.dart';

class SettingsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final User currentUser;
  final Database db;
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final VoidCallback onLogout;
  final bool isDarkMode;
  final VoidCallback onToggleTheme;

  const SettingsScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.currentUser,
    required this.db,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.onLogout,
    required this.isDarkMode,
    required this.onToggleTheme,
  });

  @override
  State<SettingsScreen> createState() => _SettingsScreenState();
}

class _SettingsScreenState extends State<SettingsScreen> {
  bool _isExporting = false;
  bool _isImporting = false;

  bool get _isOwner => widget.currentUser.role == 'OWNER';

  Future<void> _exportData() async {
    setState(() => _isExporting = true);
    try {
      final path = await DataExporter.exportToJson(
        widget.db,
        requestingUserId: widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Backup saved to: $path'),
            duration: const Duration(seconds: 5),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''))),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isExporting = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _importData() async {
    const typeGroup = XTypeGroup(label: 'JSON Backup', extensions: ['json']);
    final file = await openFile(acceptedTypeGroups: [typeGroup]);

    if (file == null) return;
    if (!mounted) return;

    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Restore Data?'),
        content: const Text(
          'WARNING: This will completely delete all current sales, products, '
          'and users (including passwords), replacing them with the backup '
          'data. This action cannot be undone.',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('Restore Data'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirm != true) return;

    setState(() => _isImporting = true);
    try {
      await DataImporter.importFromJson(
        widget.db,
        file.path,
        requestingUserId: widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      // Restores replace the devices table; make sure this machine still has
      // a device row so new sales do not violate the foreign key.
      await LocalDatabase.registerCurrentDevice(widget.db);
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              'Data restored successfully. Restart the app to refresh all '
              'screens.',
            ),
            duration: Duration(seconds: 5),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''))),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isImporting = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _changePassword() async {
    final changed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _ChangePasswordDialog(
        userRepository: widget.userRepository,
        userId: widget.currentUser.id,
      ),
    );
    if (changed == true && mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Password changed successfully.')),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Settings')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        children: [
          Card(
            child: ListTile(
              leading: CircleAvatar(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.primary,
                child: Text(
                  widget.currentUser.username.isEmpty
                      ? '?'
                      : widget.currentUser.username[0].toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ),
              title: Text(
                widget.currentUser.username,
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
              subtitle: Text('Role: ${widget.currentUser.role}'),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          const Text(
            'Appearance',
            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 18),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Card(
            child: SwitchListTile(
              secondary: Icon(
                widget.isDarkMode ? Icons.dark_mode : Icons.light_mode,
              ),
              title: const Text('Dark Mode'),
              value: widget.isDarkMode,
              onChanged: (value) => widget.onToggleTheme(),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          const Text(
            'Account',
            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 18),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Card(
            child: Column(
              children: [
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.lock_reset),
                  title: const Text('Change Password'),
                  subtitle: const Text('Update your sign-in password'),
                  trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                  onTap: _changePassword,
                ),
                const Divider(height: 1),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.logout, color: AppTheme.danger),
                  title: const Text(
                    'Logout',
                    style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.danger),
                  ),
                  onTap: widget.onLogout,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (_isOwner) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            const Text(
              'Data Management',
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 18),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Card(
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  ListTile(
                    leading: const Icon(Icons.download, color: Colors.blue),
                    title: const Text('Export Data (Backup)'),
                    subtitle: const Text(
                      'Owner only. The backup includes password hashes, so '
                      'store it securely.',
                    ),
                    trailing: _isExporting
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 24,
                            height: 24,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                          )
                        : const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                    onTap: _isExporting ? null : _exportData,
                  ),
                  const Divider(height: 1),
                  ListTile(
                    leading: const Icon(Icons.upload, color: Colors.orange),
                    title: const Text('Restore Data (Import)'),
                    subtitle: const Text('Restore from a previous JSON backup'),
                    trailing: _isImporting
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 24,
                            height: 24,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                          )
                        : const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                    onTap: _isImporting ? null : _importData,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Center(
            child: Text(
              'Wholesale POS • Database schema v${LocalDatabase.schemaVersion}',
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ChangePasswordDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final String userId;

  const _ChangePasswordDialog({
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.userId,
  });

  @override
  State<_ChangePasswordDialog> createState() => _ChangePasswordDialogState();
}

class _ChangePasswordDialogState extends State<_ChangePasswordDialog> {
  final _currentController = TextEditingController();
  final _newController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmController = TextEditingController();
  bool _isSaving = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _currentController.dispose();
    _newController.dispose();
    _confirmController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final current = _currentController.text;
    final next = _newController.text;
    if (next.length < UserRepository.minimumPasswordLength) {
      setState(
        () => _error =
            'New password must be at least '
            '${UserRepository.minimumPasswordLength} characters.',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (next != _confirmController.text) {
      setState(() => _error = 'New passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _isSaving = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await widget.userRepository.changePassword(
        userId: widget.userId,
        currentPassword: current,
        newPassword: next,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSaving = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Change Password'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 360,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: _currentController,
              obscureText: true,
              autofocus: true,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Current Password'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _newController,
              obscureText: true,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'New Password'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _confirmController,
              obscureText: true,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Confirm New Password',
              ),
              onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
            ),
            if (_error != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                _error!,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.danger, fontSize: 13),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: _isSaving ? null : () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        FilledButton(
          onPressed: _isSaving ? null : _submit,
          child: _isSaving
              ? const SizedBox(
                  width: 18,
                  height: 18,
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                )
              : const Text('Save'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/user.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/user_repository.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

class UserManagementScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final User currentUser;

  const UserManagementScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.currentUser,
  });

  @override
  State<UserManagementScreen> createState() => _UserManagementScreenState();
}

class _UserManagementScreenState extends State<UserManagementScreen> {
  List<User> _users = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadUsers();
  }

  Future<void> _loadUsers() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final users = await widget.userRepository.getAllUsers();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _users = users;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  void _showMessage(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
  }

  Future<void> _showAddUserDialog() async {
    final created = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) =>
          _AddUserDialog(userRepository: widget.userRepository),
    );
    if (created == true && mounted) {
      await _loadUsers();
      _showMessage('User created.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _resetPassword(User user) async {
    final reset = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _ResetPasswordDialog(
        userRepository: widget.userRepository,
        targetUserId: user.id,
        actingUserId: widget.currentUser.id,
        username: user.username,
      ),
    );
    if (reset == true && mounted) {
      _showMessage('Password reset for ${user.username}.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleActive(User user) async {
    final activating = !user.isActive;
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(activating ? 'Activate User?' : 'Deactivate User?'),
        content: Text(
          activating
              ? '${user.username} will be able to sign in again.'
              : '${user.username} will no longer be able to sign in. '
                    'Existing sales remain attributed to them.',
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: activating ? null : AppTheme.danger,
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: Text(activating ? 'Activate' : 'Deactivate'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirm != true || !mounted) return;

    try {
      await widget.userRepository.setUserActive(
        targetUserId: user.id,
        active: activating,
        actingUserId: widget.currentUser.id,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      await _loadUsers();
      _showMessage(activating ? 'User activated.' : 'User deactivated.');
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        _showMessage(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    if (_error != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(_error!),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            TextButton(onPressed: _loadUsers, child: const Text('Retry')),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('User Management'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _loadUsers),
        ],
      ),
      body: ListView.separated(
        itemCount: _users.length,
        separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(height: 1),
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final user = _users[index];
          final isSelf = user.id == widget.currentUser.id;
          return ListTile(
            leading: CircleAvatar(
              backgroundColor: user.role == 'OWNER' ? Colors.blue : Colors.grey,
              child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white),
            ),
            title: Text(
              user.username,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: user.isActive ? null : AppTheme.muted,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
            subtitle: Text(isSelf ? '${user.role} (you)' : user.role),
            trailing: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                if (!user.isActive)
                  const Chip(
                    label: Text('Disabled'),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
                    labelStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11),
                  ),
                PopupMenuButton<String>(
                  tooltip: 'User actions',
                  onSelected: (value) {
                    if (value == 'reset') _resetPassword(user);
                    if (value == 'toggle') _toggleActive(user);
                  },
                  itemBuilder: (context) => [
                    const PopupMenuItem(
                      value: 'reset',
                      child: Text('Reset Password'),
                    ),
                    if (!isSelf)
                      PopupMenuItem(
                        value: 'toggle',
                        child: Text(user.isActive ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _showAddUserDialog,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.person_add),
        label: const Text('Add User'),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AddUserDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserRepository userRepository;

  const _AddUserDialog({required this.userRepository});

  @override
  State<_AddUserDialog> createState() => _AddUserDialogState();
}

class _AddUserDialogState extends State<_AddUserDialog> {
  final _usernameController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmController = TextEditingController();
  String _role = 'CASHIER';
  bool _isSaving = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _usernameController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _confirmController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final username = _usernameController.text.trim();
    final password = _passwordController.text;
    if (username.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Enter a username.');
      return;
    }
    if (password.length < UserRepository.minimumPasswordLength) {
      setState(
        () => _error =
            'Password must be at least '
            '${UserRepository.minimumPasswordLength} characters.',
      );
      return;
    }
    if (password != _confirmController.text) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _isSaving = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await widget.userRepository.createUser(username, password, _role);
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _error = e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', ''));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSaving = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Add New User'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 360,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: _usernameController,
              autofocus: true,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Username'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _passwordController,
              obscureText: true,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Password'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _confirmController,
              obscureText: true,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Confirm Password'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              initialValue: _role,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Role'),
              items: const [
                DropdownMenuItem(value: 'CASHIER', child: Text('Cashier')),
                DropdownMenuItem(value: 'OWNER', child: Text('Owner / Admin')),
              ],
              onChanged: (value) => setState(() => _role = value ?? 'CASHIER'),
            ),
            if (_error != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                _error!,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.danger, fontSize: 13),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: _isSaving ? null : () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        FilledButton(
          onPressed: _isSaving ? null : _submit,
          child: _isSaving
              ? const SizedBox(
                  width: 18,
                  height: 18,
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                )
              : const Text('Create User'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

import 'core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'core/licensing/license_validator.dart';
import 'core/models/user.dart';
import 'core/repositories/finance_repository.dart';
import 'core/repositories/product_repository.dart';
import 'core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import 'core/repositories/shift_repository.dart';
import 'core/repositories/user_repository.dart';
import 'core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'features/audit/audit_log_screen.dart';
import 'features/auth/login_screen.dart';
import 'features/dashboard/dashboard_screen.dart';
import 'features/finance/finance_overview_screen.dart';
import 'features/licensing/activation_screen.dart';
import 'features/products/product_list_screen.dart';
import 'features/register/register_screen.dart';
import 'features/reports/reports_screen.dart';
import 'features/sales/sales_history_screen.dart';
import 'features/sales/sales_screen.dart';
import 'features/settings/settings_screen.dart';
import 'features/users/user_management_screen.dart';

Future<void> main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  final db = await LocalDatabase.open();
  final productRepository = ProductRepository(db);
  final saleRepository = SaleRepository(db);
  final userRepository = UserRepository(db);
  final shiftRepository = ShiftRepository(db);
  final financeRepository = FinanceRepository(db);

  final deviceId = await LocalDatabase.registerCurrentDevice(db);
  final themeSetting = await LocalDatabase.getSetting(
    db,
    LocalDatabase.themeSettingKey,
  );

  runApp(
    WholesalePosApp(
      db: db,
      productRepository: productRepository,
      saleRepository: saleRepository,
      userRepository: userRepository,
      shiftRepository: shiftRepository,
      financeRepository: financeRepository,
      deviceId: deviceId,
      initialDarkMode: themeSetting == 'dark',
    ),
  );
}

class WholesalePosApp extends StatefulWidget {
  final Database db;
  final ProductRepository productRepository;
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final ShiftRepository shiftRepository;
  final FinanceRepository financeRepository;
  final String deviceId;
  final bool initialDarkMode;

  const WholesalePosApp({
    super.key,
    required this.db,
    required this.productRepository,
    required this.saleRepository,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.shiftRepository,
    required this.financeRepository,
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.initialDarkMode,
  });

  @override
  State<WholesalePosApp> createState() => _WholesalePosAppState();
}

class _WholesalePosAppState extends State<WholesalePosApp> {
  late ThemeMode _themeMode;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _themeMode = widget.initialDarkMode ? ThemeMode.dark : ThemeMode.light;
  }

  void _toggleTheme(bool isDark) {
    setState(() => _themeMode = isDark ? ThemeMode.dark : ThemeMode.light);
    LocalDatabase.setSetting(
      widget.db,
      LocalDatabase.themeSettingKey,
      isDark ? 'dark' : 'light',
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'Wholesale POS',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.light(),
      darkTheme: AppTheme.dark(),
      themeMode: _themeMode,
      home: LicenseWrapper(
        db: widget.db,
        productRepository: widget.productRepository,
        saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
        userRepository: widget.userRepository,
        shiftRepository: widget.shiftRepository,
        financeRepository: widget.financeRepository,
        deviceId: widget.deviceId,
        isDarkMode: _themeMode == ThemeMode.dark,
        onToggleTheme: () => _toggleTheme(_themeMode == ThemeMode.light),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class LicenseWrapper extends StatefulWidget {
  final Database db;
  final ProductRepository productRepository;
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final ShiftRepository shiftRepository;
  final FinanceRepository financeRepository;
  final String deviceId;
  final bool isDarkMode;
  final VoidCallback onToggleTheme;

  const LicenseWrapper({
    super.key,
    required this.db,
    required this.productRepository,
    required this.saleRepository,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.shiftRepository,
    required this.financeRepository,
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.isDarkMode,
    required this.onToggleTheme,
  });

  @override
  State<LicenseWrapper> createState() => _LicenseWrapperState();
}

class _LicenseWrapperState extends State<LicenseWrapper> {
  bool _isChecking = true;
  bool _isLicensed = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _checkLicense();
  }

  Future<void> _checkLicense() async {
    setState(() => _isChecking = true);
    final license = await LicenseValidator.validateLicense();
    if (!mounted) return;

    setState(() {
      if (license != null && license['error'] != null) {
        _isLicensed = false;
        _error = license['error'];
      } else {
        _isLicensed = license != null;
        _error = null;
      }
      _isChecking = false;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isChecking) {
      return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }
    if (!_isLicensed) {
      return ActivationScreen(onActivated: _checkLicense, errorMessage: _error);
    }

    return AuthWrapper(
      db: widget.db,
      productRepository: widget.productRepository,
      saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
      userRepository: widget.userRepository,
      shiftRepository: widget.shiftRepository,
      financeRepository: widget.financeRepository,
      deviceId: widget.deviceId,
      isDarkMode: widget.isDarkMode,
      onToggleTheme: widget.onToggleTheme,
    );
  }
}

class AuthWrapper extends StatefulWidget {
  final Database db;
  final ProductRepository productRepository;
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final ShiftRepository shiftRepository;
  final FinanceRepository financeRepository;
  final String deviceId;
  final bool isDarkMode;
  final VoidCallback onToggleTheme;

  const AuthWrapper({
    super.key,
    required this.db,
    required this.productRepository,
    required this.saleRepository,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.shiftRepository,
    required this.financeRepository,
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.isDarkMode,
    required this.onToggleTheme,
  });

  @override
  State<AuthWrapper> createState() => _AuthWrapperState();
}

class _AuthWrapperState extends State<AuthWrapper> {
  User? _currentUser;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_currentUser == null) {
      return LoginScreen(
        userRepository: widget.userRepository,
        onLoginSuccess: (user) => setState(() => _currentUser = user),
      );
    }

    return MainLayout(
      db: widget.db,
      productRepository: widget.productRepository,
      saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
      userRepository: widget.userRepository,
      shiftRepository: widget.shiftRepository,
      financeRepository: widget.financeRepository,
      deviceId: widget.deviceId,
      currentUser: _currentUser!,
      onLogout: () => setState(() => _currentUser = null),
      isDarkMode: widget.isDarkMode,
      onToggleTheme: widget.onToggleTheme,
    );
  }
}

class MainLayout extends StatefulWidget {
  final Database db;
  final ProductRepository productRepository;
  final SaleRepository saleRepository;
  final UserRepository userRepository;
  final ShiftRepository shiftRepository;
  final FinanceRepository financeRepository;
  final String deviceId;
  final User currentUser;
  final VoidCallback onLogout;
  final bool isDarkMode;
  final VoidCallback onToggleTheme;

  const MainLayout({
    super.key,
    required this.db,
    required this.productRepository,
    required this.saleRepository,
    required this.userRepository,
    required this.shiftRepository,
    required this.financeRepository,
    required this.deviceId,
    required this.currentUser,
    required this.onLogout,
    required this.isDarkMode,
    required this.onToggleTheme,
  });

  @override
  State<MainLayout> createState() => _MainLayoutState();
}

class _MainLayoutState extends State<MainLayout> {
  int _selectedIndex = 0;
  final GlobalKey<RegisterScreenState> _registerKey =
      GlobalKey<RegisterScreenState>();
  final GlobalKey<DashboardScreenState> _dashboardKey =
      GlobalKey<DashboardScreenState>();
  final GlobalKey<ReportsScreenState> _reportsKey =
      GlobalKey<ReportsScreenState>();
  final GlobalKey<SalesScreenState> _salesKey = GlobalKey<SalesScreenState>();

  void _refreshDataScreens() {
    _registerKey.currentState?.loadData();
    _dashboardKey.currentState?.loadDashboard();
    _reportsKey.currentState?.loadReports();
    _salesKey.currentState?.loadShift();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isOwner = widget.currentUser.role == 'OWNER';

    final screens = <Widget>[
      SalesScreen(
        key: _salesKey,
        saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
        shiftRepository: widget.shiftRepository,
        productRepository: widget.productRepository,
        deviceId: widget.deviceId,
        currentUser: widget.currentUser,
        onSaleCompleted: _refreshDataScreens,
      ),
      SalesHistoryScreen(
        saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
        currentUser: widget.currentUser,
        onSaleVoided: _refreshDataScreens,
      ),
      RegisterScreen(
        key: _registerKey,
        onShiftUpdated: _refreshDataScreens,
        currentUser: widget.currentUser,
        shiftRepository: widget.shiftRepository,
        saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
      ),
      if (isOwner) ProductListScreen(repository: widget.productRepository),
      if (isOwner)
        DashboardScreen(
          key: _dashboardKey,
          saleRepository: widget.saleRepository,
        ),
      if (isOwner)
        ReportsScreen(key: _reportsKey, saleRepository: widget.saleRepository),
      if (isOwner)
        UserManagementScreen(
          userRepository: widget.userRepository,
          currentUser: widget.currentUser,
        ),
      if (isOwner) AuditLogScreen(saleRepository: widget.saleRepository),
      if (isOwner)
        FinanceOverviewScreen(
          financeRepository: widget.financeRepository,
          currentUser: widget.currentUser,
        ),
      SettingsScreen(
        currentUser: widget.currentUser,
        db: widget.db,
        userRepository: widget.userRepository,
        onLogout: widget.onLogout,
        isDarkMode: widget.isDarkMode,
        onToggleTheme: widget.onToggleTheme,
      ),
    ];

    if (_selectedIndex >= screens.length) _selectedIndex = 0;
    final settingsIndex = screens.length - 1;

    return Scaffold(
      body: LayoutBuilder(
        builder: (context, constraints) {
          final sidebarWidth = constraints.maxWidth < 900 ? 200.0 : 250.0;
          return Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: sidebarWidth,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                  border: Border(
                    right: BorderSide(
                      color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor,
                      width: 1,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                            ),
                            child: Icon(
                              Icons.storefront,
                              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onPrimary,
                              size: 16,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Flexible(
                            child: Text(
                              'Wholesale POS',
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                fontSize: 16,
                                letterSpacing: -0.5,
                                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Expanded(
                      child: ListView(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                        children: [
                          _buildNavItem(0, Icons.shopping_cart, 'POS'),
                          _buildNavItem(1, Icons.history, 'History'),
                          _buildNavItem(2, Icons.point_of_sale, 'Register'),
                          if (isOwner) ...[
                            Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 24, 12, 8),
                              child: Text(
                                'ADMINISTRATION',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 11,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                  color: Theme.of(context)
                                      .textTheme
                                      .bodySmall
                                      ?.color,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            _buildNavItem(3, Icons.label, 'Saved Names'),
                            _buildNavItem(4, Icons.dashboard, 'Dashboard'),
                            _buildNavItem(5, Icons.bar_chart, 'Reports'),
                            _buildNavItem(6, Icons.people, 'Users'),
                            _buildNavItem(7, Icons.security, 'Audit Logs'),
                            _buildNavItem(
                              8,
                              Icons.account_balance_wallet,
                              'Money Tracking',
                            ),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Divider(),
                    ListTile(
                      onTap: () =>
                          setState(() => _selectedIndex = settingsIndex),
                      leading: CircleAvatar(
                        radius: 16,
                        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                        child: Text(
                          widget.currentUser.username.isEmpty
                              ? '?'
                              : widget.currentUser.username[0].toUpperCase(),
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onPrimary,
                            fontSize: 12,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      title: Text(
                        widget.currentUser.username,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          fontSize: 13,
                        ),
                      ),
                      subtitle: Text(
                        widget.currentUser.role,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                        ),
                      ),
                      trailing: Icon(
                        Icons.settings,
                        size: 16,
                        color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                      ),
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 20,
                        vertical: 4,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: IndexedStack(index: _selectedIndex, children: screens),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNavItem(int index, IconData icon, String label) {
    final isSelected = _selectedIndex == index;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () => setState(() => _selectedIndex = index),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: isSelected
                ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface
                : Colors.transparent,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
            border: isSelected
                ? Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor)
                : null,
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Icon(
                icon,
                size: 18,
                color: isSelected
                    ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface
                    : Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Text(
                label,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.w500,
                  color: isSelected
                      ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface
                      : Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: app\test\core\database\local_database_test.dart

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';

void main() {
  test('Database initializes and creates all required tables', () async {
    final db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();

    final tables = await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' AND name NOT LIKE 'android_%'",
    );

    final tableNames = tables.map((t) => t['name'] as String).toList();

    expect(tableNames, contains('users'));
    expect(tableNames, contains('products'));
    expect(tableNames, contains('sales'));
    expect(tableNames, contains('sale_items'));
    expect(tableNames, contains('audit_logs'));
    expect(tableNames, contains('devices'));

    await db.close();
  });

  test('Registers and reuses a generated device ID', () async {
    final db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    final first = await LocalDatabase.registerCurrentDevice(db);
    final second = await LocalDatabase.registerCurrentDevice(db);
    expect(first, isNot('device-1'));
    expect(second, first);
    await db.close();
  });
}

==================================================
FILE: app\test\core\database\migration_test.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';

void main() {
  late Directory tempDir;
  late String dbPath;

  setUp(() async {
    tempDir = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('wholesale_pos_test_');
    dbPath = p.join(tempDir.path, 'test.db');
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    try {
      await tempDir.delete(recursive: true);
    } catch (_) {
      // Windows occasionally holds the file handle a moment longer.
    }
  });

  test('Upgrades a v5 database to v6 without losing cash movements', () async {
    var db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'user-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'created_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'user-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 10000,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('cash_movements', {
      'id': 'movement-1',
      'shift_id': 'shift-1',
      'type': 'CASH_IN',
      'amount_minor_units': 5000,
      'reason': 'float top-up',
      'created_at': now,
      'user_id': 'user-1',
    });

    // Rewind the database to the v5 shape: cash_movements had user_id added
    // with ALTER TABLE (so no foreign key), and counters/settings did not
    // exist yet.
    await db.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE cash_movements_v5 (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        type TEXT NOT NULL,
        amount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        reason TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        user_id TEXT
      )
    ''');
    await db.execute('''
      INSERT INTO cash_movements_v5
        (id, shift_id, type, amount_minor_units, reason, created_at, user_id)
      SELECT id, shift_id, type, amount_minor_units, reason, created_at, user_id
      FROM cash_movements
    ''');
    await db.execute('DROP TABLE cash_movements');
    await db.execute('ALTER TABLE cash_movements_v5 RENAME TO cash_movements');
    await db.execute('DROP TABLE counters');
    await db.execute('DROP TABLE settings');
    await db.execute('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS sales_created_at');
    await db.execute('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS sale_items_sale_id');
    await db.execute('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS audit_logs_created_at');
    await db.execute('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS cash_movements_shift_id');
    await db.execute('PRAGMA user_version = 5');
    await db.close();

    db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);

    final version = await db.rawQuery('PRAGMA user_version');
    expect(version.first.values.first, LocalDatabase.schemaVersion);

    final tables = (await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table'",
    )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
    expect(tables, containsAll(['counters', 'settings', 'cash_movements']));

    final foreignKeys = await db.rawQuery(
      'PRAGMA foreign_key_list(cash_movements)',
    );
    expect(foreignKeys.any((row) => row['table'] == 'users'), isTrue);

    final movement = (await db.query(
      'cash_movements',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['movement-1'],
    )).single;
    expect(movement['amount_minor_units'], 5000);
    expect(movement['user_id'], 'user-1');

    final indexes = (await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'index'",
    )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
    expect(
      indexes,
      containsAll([
        'sales_shift_id',
        'sales_created_at',
        'sale_items_sale_id',
        'audit_logs_created_at',
        'cash_movements_shift_id',
      ]),
    );

    final triggers = (await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'trigger'",
    )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
    expect(
      triggers,
      containsAll([
        'prevent_second_open_shift_insert',
        'prevent_second_open_shift_update',
      ]),
    );

    await db.close();
  });

  test('Upgrades a v6 database to v7, preserving sale items and adding finance tables', () async {
    var db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'user-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'created_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'user-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'CLOSED',
      'opened_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('sales', {
      'id': 'sale-1',
      'receipt_number': 'REC-001',
      'cashier_id': 'user-1',
      'device_id': 'device-1',
      'shift_id': 'shift-1',
      'subtotal_minor_units': 7000,
      'discount_minor_units': 0,
      'total_minor_units': 7000,
      'amount_paid_minor_units': 7000,
      'change_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'COMPLETED',
      'created_at': now,
    });

    // Rewind sale_items to the v6 shape: an integer quantity column and no
    // flexible-quantity columns.
    await db.execute('DROP TABLE sale_items');
    await db.execute('''
      CREATE TABLE sale_items (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        sale_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        product_name_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
        unit_price_snapshot_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
        line_discount_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        line_total_minor_units INTEGER NOT NULL,
        FOREIGN KEY(sale_id) REFERENCES sales(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
      )
    ''');
    await db.insert('sale_items', {
      'id': 'item-1',
      'sale_id': 'sale-1',
      'product_id': 'prod-1',
      'product_name_snapshot': 'Sugar',
      'unit_price_snapshot_minor_units': 3500,
      'quantity': 2,
      'line_discount_minor_units': 0,
      'line_total_minor_units': 7000,
    });
    await db.execute('PRAGMA user_version = 6');
    await db.close();

    db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);

    final version = await db.rawQuery('PRAGMA user_version');
    expect(version.first.values.first, LocalDatabase.schemaVersion);

    // Legacy values were copied into both new columns: quantity 2 becomes
    // text "2" and billing milliunits 2000.
    final item = (await db.query(
      'sale_items',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['item-1'],
    )).single;
    expect(item['quantity_text'], '2');
    expect(item['billing_quantity_milliunits'], 2000);
    expect(item['line_total_minor_units'], 7000);
    final columns = await db.rawQuery('PRAGMA table_info(sale_items)');
    expect(
      columns.map((row) => row['name']),
      containsAll(['quantity_text', 'billing_quantity_milliunits', 'notes']),
    );
    expect(columns.map((row) => row['name']), isNot(contains('quantity')));

    // The finance tables and their indexes exist after the upgrade.
    final tables = (await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table'",
    )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
    expect(tables, containsAll(['finance_categories', 'finance_entries']));
    final indexes = (await db.rawQuery(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'index'",
    )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
    expect(
      indexes,
      containsAll([
        'finance_entries_category_id',
        'finance_entries_status',
        'finance_entries_entry_date',
      ]),
    );

    // The sale itself survived untouched.
    final sale = (await db.query(
      'sales',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['sale-1'],
    )).single;
    expect(sale['total_minor_units'], 7000);

    await db.close();
  });

  test(
    'Upgrades a v7 database to v8, adding void_requests and its indexes',
    () async {
      var db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);
      final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
      await db.insert('users', {
        'id': 'user-1',
        'username': 'owner',
        'password_hash': 'x',
        'salt': 'x',
        'role': 'OWNER',
        'is_active': 1,
        'created_at': now,
      });
      await db.insert('devices', {
        'id': 'device-1',
        'name': 'PC',
        'platform': 'Windows',
      });
      await db.insert('shifts', {
        'id': 'shift-1',
        'cashier_id': 'user-1',
        'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
        'status': 'CLOSED',
        'opened_at': now,
      });
      await db.insert('sales', {
        'id': 'sale-1',
        'receipt_number': 'REC-001',
        'cashier_id': 'user-1',
        'device_id': 'device-1',
        'shift_id': 'shift-1',
        'subtotal_minor_units': 5000,
        'discount_minor_units': 0,
        'total_minor_units': 5000,
        'amount_paid_minor_units': 5000,
        'change_minor_units': 0,
        'status': 'COMPLETED',
        'created_at': now,
      });

      // Rewind to the v7 shape: no void_requests table or indexes.
      await db.execute('DROP TABLE void_requests');
      await db.execute('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS void_requests_sale_id');
      await db.execute('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS void_requests_status');
      await db.execute('PRAGMA user_version = 7');
      await db.close();

      db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);

      final version = await db.rawQuery('PRAGMA user_version');
      expect(version.first.values.first, LocalDatabase.schemaVersion);

      final tables = (await db.rawQuery(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table'",
      )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
      expect(tables, contains('void_requests'));

      final indexes = (await db.rawQuery(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'index'",
      )).map((row) => row['name'] as String).toSet();
      expect(
        indexes,
        containsAll(['void_requests_sale_id', 'void_requests_status']),
      );

      // void_requests references both sales and users.
      final foreignKeys = await db.rawQuery(
        'PRAGMA foreign_key_list(void_requests)',
      );
      final fkTables = foreignKeys.map((row) => row['table']).toSet();
      expect(fkTables, containsAll(['sales', 'users']));

      // The pre-existing sale survived the upgrade untouched.
      final sale = (await db.query(
        'sales',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: ['sale-1'],
      )).single;
      expect(sale['total_minor_units'], 5000);

      await db.close();
    },
  );

  test('Re-registers the persisted device ID after a restore', () async {
    var db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);
    final deviceId = await LocalDatabase.registerCurrentDevice(db);
    expect(
      await File(p.join(tempDir.path, LocalDatabase.deviceIdFileName)).exists(),
      isTrue,
    );

    // Simulate a restore that replaced the devices table.
    await db.delete('devices');
    expect(await db.query('devices'), isEmpty);
    await db.close();

    db = await LocalDatabase.openAt(dbPath);
    final restoredId = await LocalDatabase.registerCurrentDevice(db);

    expect(restoredId, deviceId);
    final devices = await db.query('devices');
    expect(devices.length, 1);
    expect(devices.single['id'], deviceId);
    await db.close();
  });
}

==================================================
FILE: app\test\core\licensing\license_validator_test.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:cryptography/cryptography.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/licensing/license_validator.dart';

Object? canonical(Object? value) {
  if (value is Map) {
    final keys = value.keys.cast<String>().toList()..sort();
    return {for (final key in keys) key: canonical(value[key])};
  }
  if (value is List) return value.map(canonical).toList();
  return value;
}

void main() {
  late Directory directory;
  late SimpleKeyPairData pair;
  late String publicHex;

  setUp(() async {
    directory = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('license-test-');
    final generated = await Ed25519().newKeyPair();
    pair = await generated.extract();
    publicHex = pair.publicKey.bytes
        .map((b) => b.toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0'))
        .join();
  });
  tearDown(() async => directory.delete(recursive: true));

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> validate(Map<String, Object?> payload) async {
    final bytes = utf8.encode(jsonEncode(canonical(payload)));
    final signature = await Ed25519().sign(bytes, keyPair: pair);
    final path = '${directory.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}license.key';
    await File(path).writeAsString(
      '${base64Url.encode(bytes)}.${base64Url.encode(signature.bytes)}',
    );
    final previous = Directory.current;
    Directory.current = directory;
    try {
      return await LicenseValidator.validateLicense(
        hardwareId: 'test-device',
        now: DateTime.utc(2030),
        verificationKeyHex: publicHex,
      );
    } finally {
      Directory.current = previous;
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> validateTamperedPayload(
    Map<String, Object?> payload,
  ) async {
    final originalBytes = utf8.encode(jsonEncode(canonical(payload)));
    final signature = await Ed25519().sign(originalBytes, keyPair: pair);
    final changed = Map<String, Object?>.from(payload)
      ..['entitlements'] = ['pos', 'admin'];
    final changedBytes = utf8.encode(jsonEncode(canonical(changed)));
    final path = '${directory.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}license.key';
    await File(path).writeAsString(
      '${base64Url.encode(changedBytes)}.${base64Url.encode(signature.bytes)}',
    );
    final previous = Directory.current;
    Directory.current = directory;
    try {
      return await LicenseValidator.validateLicense(
        hardwareId: 'test-device',
        now: DateTime.utc(2030),
        verificationKeyHex: publicHex,
      );
    } finally {
      Directory.current = previous;
    }
  }

  Map<String, Object?> payload() => {
    'format': 'wholesale-pos-license',
    'version': 1,
    'product': 'wholesale_pos',
    'entitlements': ['pos'],
    'hardware_id': 'test-device',
    'issued_at': '2029-01-01T00:00:00Z',
    'expires_at': '2031-01-01T00:00:00Z',
  };

  test('accepts a valid Ed25519 license', () async {
    expect(await validate(payload()), containsPair('product', 'wholesale_pos'));
  });

  test('rejects a modified payload and wrong hardware ID', () async {
    expect(
      (await validateTamperedPayload(payload()))?['error'],
      'Invalid license signature.',
    );
    expect(
      (await validate(payload()..['hardware_id'] = 'other-device'))?['error'],
      'License is for a different device.',
    );
  });

  test('rejects expired, malformed, and incomplete licenses', () async {
    expect(
      (await validate(
        payload()..['expires_at'] = '2029-02-01T00:00:00Z',
      ))?['error'],
      'License has expired.',
    );
    expect(
      (await validate(payload()..remove('product')))?['error'],
      'License is missing required fields.',
    );
    final path = '${directory.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}license.key';
    await File(path).writeAsString('not-a-license');
    final previous = Directory.current;
    Directory.current = directory;
    try {
      expect(
        (await LicenseValidator.validateLicense(
          verificationKeyHex: publicHex,
        ))?['error'],
        'Invalid license format.',
      );
    } finally {
      Directory.current = previous;
    }
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\models\product_repository_test.dart

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/models/product.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/product_repository.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late ProductRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = ProductRepository(db);
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  test('Can save and retrieve a product', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final product = Product(
      id: 'prod-1',
      name: 'White Flour 50kg',
      code: 'FLOUR-50',
      category: 'Baking',
      unit: 'Bag',
      sellingPriceMinorUnits: 450000,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    );

    await repository.save(product);
    final fetched = await repository.getById('prod-1');

    expect(fetched, isNotNull);
    expect(fetched!.name, 'White Flour 50kg');
    expect(fetched.sellingPriceMinorUnits, 450000);
    expect(fetched.unit, 'Bag');
  });

  test('Returns null for a missing product', () async {
    final fetched = await repository.getById('unknown-id');
    expect(fetched, isNull);
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\repositories\dashboard_metrics_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/utils/date_range.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale_item.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late SaleRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = SaleRepository(db);
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'cashier-1',
      'username': 'cashier',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'CASHIER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': now,
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  Future<void> seedSale(
    String id,
    int total,
    DateTime createdAt, {
    String status = 'COMPLETED',
    String cashierId = 'owner-1',
  }) async {
    await repository.saveSale(
      Sale(
        id: id,
        receiptNumber: 'REC-$id',
        cashierId: cashierId,
        deviceId: 'device-1',
        shiftId: 'shift-1',
        subtotalMinorUnits: total,
        discountMinorUnits: 0,
        totalMinorUnits: total,
        amountPaidMinorUnits: total,
        changeMinorUnits: 0,
        status: status,
        createdAt: createdAt,
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-$id',
            saleId: id,
            productId: 'MANUAL',
            productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar',
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: total,
            quantityText: '1',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: total,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  test('getRevenueTrend groups completed revenue per calendar day', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final today = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 10);
    final yesterday = today.subtract(const Duration(days: 1));

    await seedSale('t1', 3000, today);
    await seedSale('t2', 3000, today.add(const Duration(hours: 1)));
    await seedSale('y1', 5000, yesterday);

    final trend = await repository.getRevenueTrend(
      DateRange.custom(yesterday, today),
    );

    final byDay = {for (final r in trend) r['day'] as String: r};
    final yesterdayKey =
        '${yesterday.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${yesterday.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${yesterday.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
    final todayKey =
        '${today.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${today.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${today.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

    expect(byDay[yesterdayKey]!['total'], 5000);
    expect(byDay[yesterdayKey]!['count'], 1);
    expect(byDay[todayKey]!['total'], 6000);
    expect(byDay[todayKey]!['count'], 2);

    // Oldest to newest.
    expect(trend.first['day'], yesterdayKey);
    expect(trend.last['day'], todayKey);
  });

  test('getRevenueTrend excludes voided sales', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    await seedSale('v1', 4000, now, status: 'VOIDED');
    await seedSale('c1', 2000, now);

    final trend = await repository.getRevenueTrend(DateRange.today());
    expect(trend.single['total'], 2000);
    expect(trend.single['count'], 1);
  });

  test('dashboard stats report void and pending-request counts', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    await seedSale('s1', 5000, now);
    await seedSale('s2', 5000, now);

    var stats = await repository.getDashboardStats(period: DateRange.today());
    expect(stats['revenue'], 10000);
    expect(stats['sales_count'], 2);
    expect(stats['void_count'], 0);
    expect(stats['pending_void_requests'], 0);

    // One cashier requests a void: pending count rises, revenue unchanged.
    await repository.requestVoid('s1', 'cashier-1', 'wrong price');
    stats = await repository.getDashboardStats(period: DateRange.today());
    expect(stats['revenue'], 10000);
    expect(stats['pending_void_requests'], 1);
    expect(stats['void_count'], 0);

    // Owner approves: it becomes a void, pending clears.
    final requestId = (await repository.getPendingVoidRequests()).first.id;
    await repository.approveVoidRequest(requestId, 'owner-1');
    stats = await repository.getDashboardStats(period: DateRange.today());
    expect(stats['revenue'], 5000);
    expect(stats['void_count'], 1);
    expect(stats['pending_void_requests'], 0);
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\repositories\finance_repository_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/finance_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/models/finance_entry.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late FinanceRepository repository;

  Future<void> insertUser(String id, String role, {int isActive = 1}) async {
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': id,
      'username': id,
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': role,
      'is_active': isActive,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  }

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = FinanceRepository(db);
    await insertUser('owner-1', 'OWNER');
    await insertUser('cashier-1', 'CASHIER');
    await insertUser('former-owner', 'OWNER', isActive: 0);
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  group('owner-only enforcement', () {
    test('a cashier is rejected by every finance method', () async {
      await expectLater(repository.getCategories('cashier-1'), throwsException);
      await expectLater(
        repository.getCategorySummaries('cashier-1'),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.createCategory(ownerId: 'cashier-1', name: 'Bob'),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.renameCategory(
          ownerId: 'cashier-1',
          categoryId: 'anything',
          newName: 'X',
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.addEntry(
          ownerId: 'cashier-1',
          categoryId: 'anything',
          source: 'Bank',
          amountMinorUnits: 100,
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.updateEntry(
          ownerId: 'cashier-1',
          entryId: 'anything',
          source: 'Bank',
          amountMinorUnits: 100,
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.markCompleted(ownerId: 'cashier-1', entryId: 'anything'),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'cashier-1'),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.getPersonTotals('cashier-1', 'anything'),
        throwsException,
      );

      // Nothing was written by the denied attempts.
      expect(await db.query('finance_categories'), isEmpty);
      expect(await db.query('finance_entries'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('a deactivated owner is rejected', () async {
      await expectLater(
        repository.getCategories('former-owner'),
        throwsException,
      );
    });
  });

  group('people (categories)', () {
    test('creates and lists people; duplicate names are rejected', () async {
      await repository.createCategory(ownerId: 'owner-1', name: 'Alem');
      await repository.createCategory(ownerId: 'owner-1', name: 'Hanna');

      final people = await repository.getCategories('owner-1');
      expect(people.map((p) => p.name), ['Alem', 'Hanna']);

      await expectLater(
        repository.createCategory(ownerId: 'owner-1', name: ' alem '),
        throwsException,
      );
    });

    test('renaming keeps all entries attached to the person', () async {
      final category = await repository.createCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        name: 'Alem',
      );
      await repository.addEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: category.id,
        source: 'CBE Birr',
        amountMinorUnits: 500000,
      );

      await repository.renameCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: category.id,
        newName: 'Alem Tadesse',
      );

      final entries = await repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'owner-1');
      expect(entries.length, 1);
      expect(entries.first.categoryName, 'Alem Tadesse');
    });

    test('empty names are rejected', () async {
      await expectLater(
        repository.createCategory(ownerId: 'owner-1', name: '   '),
        throwsException,
      );
    });
  });

  group('entry lifecycle', () {
    test('pending entry becomes completed and stays in history', () async {
      final category = await repository.createCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        name: 'Alem',
      );
      final entry = await repository.addEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: category.id,
        source: 'Dashen Bank',
        amountMinorUnits: 100000,
        description: 'monthly withdrawal',
      );
      expect(entry.status, FinanceEntry.statusPending);

      var pending = await repository.searchEntries(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        status: FinanceEntry.statusPending,
      );
      expect(pending.length, 1);

      await repository.markCompleted(ownerId: 'owner-1', entryId: entry.id);

      // Gone from pending...
      pending = await repository.searchEntries(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        status: FinanceEntry.statusPending,
      );
      expect(pending, isEmpty);

      // ...but still present in history, searchable, with a completion stamp.
      final completed = await repository.searchEntries(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        status: FinanceEntry.statusCompleted,
      );
      expect(completed.length, 1);
      expect(completed.first.completedAt, isNotNull);
      expect(completed.first.completedBy, 'owner-1');

      final all = await repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'owner-1');
      expect(all.length, 1);
    });

    test('completed entries are immutable', () async {
      final category = await repository.createCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        name: 'Alem',
      );
      final entry = await repository.addEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: category.id,
        source: 'Bank',
        amountMinorUnits: 100000,
      );
      await repository.markCompleted(ownerId: 'owner-1', entryId: entry.id);

      await expectLater(
        repository.updateEntry(
          ownerId: 'owner-1',
          entryId: entry.id,
          source: 'Changed',
          amountMinorUnits: 999,
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.markCompleted(ownerId: 'owner-1', entryId: entry.id),
        throwsException,
      );
    });

    test('pending entries can be edited', () async {
      final category = await repository.createCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        name: 'Alem',
      );
      final entry = await repository.addEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: category.id,
        source: 'Bank',
        amountMinorUnits: 100000,
      );
      await repository.updateEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        entryId: entry.id,
        source: 'Liberty Bank',
        amountMinorUnits: 150000,
      );

      final entries = await repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'owner-1');
      expect(entries.single.source, 'Liberty Bank');
      expect(entries.single.amountMinorUnits, 150000);
    });

    test('amounts must be positive and a source is required', () async {
      final category = await repository.createCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        name: 'Alem',
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.addEntry(
          ownerId: 'owner-1',
          categoryId: category.id,
          source: 'Bank',
          amountMinorUnits: 0,
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
      await expectLater(
        repository.addEntry(
          ownerId: 'owner-1',
          categoryId: category.id,
          source: '  ',
          amountMinorUnits: 100,
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
    });
  });

  group('search and totals', () {
    late String categoryId;

    setUp(() async {
      final category = await repository.createCategory(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        name: 'Alem',
      );
      categoryId = category.id;
      await repository.addEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: categoryId,
        source: 'CBE Birr app',
        amountMinorUnits: 500000,
        description: 'rent money',
      );
      await repository.addEntry(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        categoryId: categoryId,
        source: 'Abysseum branch',
        amountMinorUnits: 250000,
      );
    });

    test('matches source, description, and person name', () async {
      expect(
        await repository.searchEntries(
          ownerId: 'owner-1',
          searchQuery: 'abysseum',
        ),
        hasLength(1),
      );
      expect(
        await repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'owner-1', searchQuery: 'rent'),
        hasLength(1),
      );
      expect(
        await repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'owner-1', searchQuery: 'alem'),
        hasLength(2),
      );
    });

    test('wildcards in the query match literally', () async {
      expect(
        await repository.searchEntries(ownerId: 'owner-1', searchQuery: '%'),
        isEmpty,
      );
    });

    test('person totals separate pending from completed', () async {
      final entries = await repository.searchEntries(
        ownerId: 'owner-1',
        status: FinanceEntry.statusPending,
      );
      // Complete the 500000 entry specifically so the totals are deterministic.
      final target = entries.firstWhere((e) => e.amountMinorUnits == 500000);
      await repository.markCompleted(ownerId: 'owner-1', entryId: target.id);

      final totals = await repository.getPersonTotals('owner-1', categoryId);
      expect(totals['pending_total'], 250000);
      expect(totals['completed_total'], 500000);

      final summaries = await repository.getCategorySummaries('owner-1');
      expect(summaries.single['pending_count'], 1);
      expect(summaries.single['pending_total'], 250000);
    });

    test('every mutation is written to the audit log', () async {
      final logs = await db.query(
        'audit_logs',
        where: "action LIKE 'FINANCE%'",
      );
      expect(logs.length, 3); // 1 category + 2 entries
    });
  });
}

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late SaleRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = SaleRepository(db);

    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  test('Allocates sequential daily receipt numbers', () async {
    final first = await repository.nextReceiptNumber();
    final second = await repository.nextReceiptNumber();

    final now = DateTime.now();
    final day =
        '${now.year}'
        '${now.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        '${now.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
    expect(first, 'REC-$day-0001');
    expect(second, 'REC-$day-0002');
  });

  test('Dashboard reports whether a register shift is open', () async {
    var stats = await repository.getDashboardStats();
    expect(stats['is_shift_open'], 0);

    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 10000,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });

    stats = await repository.getDashboardStats();
    expect(stats['is_shift_open'], 1);
    expect(stats['expected_cash'], 10000);
  });
}
