FILE: app\test\core\repositories\report_repository_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/utils/date_range.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale_item.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late SaleRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = SaleRepository(db);

    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'cashier-1',
      'username': 'test_cashier',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'CASHIER',
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'cashier-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  Future<void> seedSale({
    required String id,
    required int totalMinorUnits,
    required DateTime createdAt,
    String product = 'Sugar',
    String paymentMethod = 'CASH',
  }) async {
    await repository.saveSale(
      Sale(
        id: id,
        receiptNumber: 'REC-$id',
        cashierId: 'cashier-1',
        deviceId: 'device-1',
        shiftId: 'shift-1',
        paymentMethod: paymentMethod,
        subtotalMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
        discountMinorUnits: 0,
        totalMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
        amountPaidMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
        changeMinorUnits: 0,
        createdAt: createdAt,
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-$id',
            saleId: id,
            productId: 'MANUAL',
            productNameSnapshot: product,
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
            quantityText: '1',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  test('Dashboard stats split revenue by payment method', () async {
    await seedSale(
      id: 'sale-1',
      totalMinorUnits: 5000,
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
    await seedSale(
      id: 'sale-2',
      totalMinorUnits: 3000,
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
      paymentMethod: 'BANK',
    );

    final stats = await repository.getDashboardStats();
    expect(stats['revenue'], 8000);
    expect(stats['sales_count'], 2);
    expect(stats['cash_revenue'], 5000);
    expect(stats['bank_revenue'], 3000);
    expect(stats['average_sale'], 4000);
  });

  test('Dashboard periods exclude older sales by date filtering', () async {
    // Two days ago is outside today, yesterday, and (when today is Monday)
    // this week — but inside all time.
    final old = DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 2));
    await seedSale(id: 'sale-old', totalMinorUnits: 5000, createdAt: old);
    await seedSale(
      id: 'sale-new',
      totalMinorUnits: 3000,
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    final today = await repository.getDashboardStats(period: DateRange.today());
    expect(today['revenue'], 3000);
    expect(today['sales_count'], 1);

    final yesterday = await repository.getDashboardStats(
      period: DateRange.yesterday(),
    );
    expect(yesterday['revenue'], 0);

    final yesterdayCustom = await repository.getDashboardStats(
      period: DateRange.custom(
        DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 2)),
        DateTime.now(),
      ),
    );
    expect(yesterdayCustom['revenue'], 8000);

    final allTime = await repository.getDashboardStats(
      period: DateRange.allTime(),
    );
    expect(allTime['revenue'], 8000);
  });

  test(
    'Product summary sums milliunit quantities and filters by period',
    () async {
      final old = DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 2));
      await seedSale(
        id: 'p-old',
        totalMinorUnits: 1000,
        createdAt: old,
        product: 'Sugar',
      );
      await seedSale(
        id: 'p-1',
        totalMinorUnits: 5000,
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        product: 'Sugar',
      );
      await seedSale(
        id: 'p-2',
        totalMinorUnits: 3000,
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        product: 'Sugar',
      );

      final todaySummary = await repository.getProductSalesSummary(
        period: DateRange.today(),
      );
      expect(todaySummary.length, 1);
      expect(todaySummary.first['product_name_snapshot'], 'Sugar');
      expect(todaySummary.first['total_qty_milliunits'], 2000);
      expect(todaySummary.first['line_count'], 2);
      expect(todaySummary.first['total_revenue'], 8000);

      final allSummary = await repository.getProductSalesSummary();
      expect(allSummary.first['total_qty_milliunits'], 3000);
      expect(allSummary.first['line_count'], 3);
      expect(allSummary.first['total_revenue'], 9000);
    },
  );

  test('Manual-amount lines report no numeric quantity', () async {
    await repository.saveSale(
      Sale(
        id: 'sale-manual',
        receiptNumber: 'REC-MANUAL',
        cashierId: 'cashier-1',
        deviceId: 'device-1',
        shiftId: 'shift-1',
        subtotalMinorUnits: 2500,
        discountMinorUnits: 0,
        totalMinorUnits: 2500,
        amountPaidMinorUnits: 2500,
        changeMinorUnits: 0,
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-manual',
            saleId: 'sale-manual',
            productId: 'MANUAL',
            productNameSnapshot: 'Mixed Boxes',
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 2500,
            quantityText: '2 boxes and 1 piece',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: null,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: 2500,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    final summary = await repository.getProductSalesSummary();
    expect(summary.single['product_name_snapshot'], 'Mixed Boxes');
    expect(summary.single['total_qty_milliunits'], 0);
    expect(summary.single['line_count'], 1);
    expect(summary.single['total_revenue'], 2500);
  });

  test('Cashier performance reports sales counts and revenue', () async {
    await seedSale(id: 'c-1', totalMinorUnits: 5000, createdAt: DateTime.now());
    await seedSale(id: 'c-2', totalMinorUnits: 3000, createdAt: DateTime.now());

    final performance = await repository.getCashierPerformance();
    expect(performance.length, 1);
    expect(performance.first['username'], 'test_cashier');
    expect(performance.first['total_sales'], 2);
    expect(performance.first['total_revenue'], 8000);
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\repositories\sale_repository_test.dart

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale_item.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late SaleRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = SaleRepository(db);

    // Insert foreign key dependencies
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'user-1',
      'username': 'abebe',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'user-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  Sale buildSale({
    required String id,
    required String receiptNumber,
    required List<SaleItem> items,
    String? customerName,
    int totalMinorUnits = 5000,
  }) {
    return Sale(
      id: id,
      receiptNumber: receiptNumber,
      cashierId: 'user-1',
      deviceId: 'device-1',
      shiftId: 'shift-1',
      customerName: customerName,
      subtotalMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
      discountMinorUnits: 0,
      totalMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
      amountPaidMinorUnits: totalMinorUnits,
      changeMinorUnits: 0,
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
      items: items,
    );
  }

  test(
    'Saves a complete sale with numeric quantity lines transactionally',
    () async {
      final sale = buildSale(
        id: 'sale-1',
        receiptNumber: 'REC-001',
        totalMinorUnits: 5000,
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-1',
            saleId: 'sale-1',
            productId: 'prod-1',
            productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar',
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 5000,
            quantityText: '1',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: 5000,
          ),
        ],
      );

      await repository.saveSale(sale);

      final sales = await db.query('sales');
      expect(sales.length, 1);

      final items = await db.query('sale_items');
      expect(items.length, 1);
      expect(items.first['quantity_text'], '1');
      expect(items.first['billing_quantity_milliunits'], 1000);

      final logs = await db.query('audit_logs');
      expect(logs.length, 1);
    },
  );

  test(
    'Saves a descriptive quantity with a manually entered line amount',
    () async {
      // "2 boxes and 1 piece" cannot be a number; the total was typed by hand
      // and the billing quantity is stored as NULL, never guessed.
      final sale = buildSale(
        id: 'sale-2',
        receiptNumber: 'REC-002',
        totalMinorUnits: 137500,
        customerName: 'Kebede Trading',
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-2',
            saleId: 'sale-2',
            productId: 'MANUAL',
            productNameSnapshot: 'Berbere',
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 25000,
            quantityText: '2 boxes and 1 piece',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: null,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: 137500,
            notes: 'mixed boxes',
          ),
        ],
      );

      await repository.saveSale(sale);

      final items = await db.query('sale_items');
      expect(items.single['quantity_text'], '2 boxes and 1 piece');
      expect(items.single['billing_quantity_milliunits'], isNull);
      expect(items.single['notes'], 'mixed boxes');
    },
  );

  test('Rejects a line total that does not match price and quantity', () async {
    // The cashier described "2 boxes" but the total is for a single box.
    // The repository must refuse rather than silently misinterpret.
    final sale = buildSale(
      id: 'sale-3',
      receiptNumber: 'REC-003',
      totalMinorUnits: 5000,
      items: [
        SaleItem(
          id: 'item-3',
          saleId: 'sale-3',
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar',
          unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 5000,
          quantityText: '2 boxes',
          billingQuantityMilliunits: 2000,
          lineTotalMinorUnits: 5000, // correct value would be 10000
        ),
      ],
    );

    await expectLater(repository.saveSale(sale), throwsException);
    expect(await db.query('sales'), isEmpty);
    expect(await db.query('sale_items'), isEmpty);
  });

  test('Rejects a manual line amount of zero', () async {
    final sale = buildSale(
      id: 'sale-4',
      receiptNumber: 'REC-004',
      totalMinorUnits: 0,
      items: [
        SaleItem(
          id: 'item-4',
          saleId: 'sale-4',
          productId: 'MANUAL',
          productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar',
          unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 5000,
          quantityText: 'some bags',
          billingQuantityMilliunits: null,
          lineTotalMinorUnits: 0,
        ),
      ],
    );

    await expectLater(repository.saveSale(sale), throwsException);
    expect(await db.query('sales'), isEmpty);
  });

  test('rejects a sale when its referenced shift is closed', () async {
    await db.update(
      'shifts',
      {'status': 'CLOSED'},
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['shift-1'],
    );
    final sale = buildSale(
      id: 'closed-shift-sale',
      receiptNumber: 'REC-CLOSED',
      totalMinorUnits: 100,
      items: [
        SaleItem(
          id: 'closed-item',
          saleId: 'closed-shift-sale',
          productId: 'MANUAL',
          productNameSnapshot: 'Manual item',
          unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 100,
          quantityText: '1',
          billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
          lineTotalMinorUnits: 100,
        ),
      ],
    );

    await expectLater(repository.saveSale(sale), throwsException);
    expect(await db.query('sales'), isEmpty);
    expect(await db.query('audit_logs'), isEmpty);
  });

  group('getSales search', () {
    late String sugarSaleId;
    late String flourSaleId;

    setUp(() async {
      sugarSaleId = 'sale-sugar';
      flourSaleId = 'sale-flour';
      await repository.saveSale(
        buildSale(
          id: sugarSaleId,
          receiptNumber: 'REC-SUGAR',
          totalMinorUnits: 5000,
          customerName: 'Kebede Trading',
          items: [
            SaleItem(
              id: 'item-sugar',
              saleId: sugarSaleId,
              productId: 'MANUAL',
              productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar 50kg',
              unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 5000,
              quantityText: '1 bag',
              billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
              lineTotalMinorUnits: 5000,
              notes: 'deliver tomorrow',
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
      await repository.saveSale(
        buildSale(
          id: flourSaleId,
          receiptNumber: 'REC-FLOUR',
          totalMinorUnits: 1500,
          items: [
            SaleItem(
              id: 'item-flour',
              saleId: flourSaleId,
              productId: 'MANUAL',
              productNameSnapshot: 'Flour',
              unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 1500,
              quantityText: '1',
              billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
              lineTotalMinorUnits: 1500,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    });

    test('finds sales by product name', () async {
      final sales = await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'sugar');
      expect(sales.length, 1);
      expect(sales.first.id, sugarSaleId);
      expect(sales.first.items.first.productNameSnapshot, 'Sugar 50kg');
    });

    test('finds sales by customer name', () async {
      final sales = await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'kebede');
      expect(sales.length, 1);
      expect(sales.first.id, sugarSaleId);
    });

    test('finds sales by cashier username', () async {
      final sales = await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'abebe');
      expect(sales.length, 2);
    });

    test('finds sales by quantity text and notes', () async {
      expect((await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'bag')).map((s) => s.id), [
        sugarSaleId,
      ]);
      expect(
        (await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'deliver')).map((s) => s.id),
        [sugarSaleId],
      );
    });

    test('never matches receipt numbers', () async {
      // Receipt numbers are internal identifiers; searching them must find
      // nothing even though they exist on the rows.
      expect(await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'REC-SUGAR'), isEmpty);
      expect(await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'rec'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('LIKE wildcards in the query match literally', () async {
      expect(await repository.getSales(searchQuery: '%'), isEmpty);
      expect(await repository.getSales(searchQuery: '_'), isEmpty);
      expect(await repository.getSales(searchQuery: 'S_gar'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('filters by status', () async {
      await repository.voidSale(sugarSaleId, 'user-1', 'wrong entry');
      final voided = await repository.getSales(status: 'VOIDED');
      expect(voided.length, 1);
      expect(voided.first.id, sugarSaleId);
      final completed = await repository.getSales(status: 'COMPLETED');
      expect(completed.length, 1);
      expect(completed.first.id, flourSaleId);
    });
  });
}

==================================================
FILE: app\test\core\repositories\shift_cash_math_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/shift_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/shift.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

/// Guards the corrected expected-cash formula:
///   opening + cashSales + deposits - withdrawals - crossShiftCashRefunds
/// A sale rung up and voided in the SAME shift must net to zero (it is never a
/// refund against this drawer); only refunds of an EARLIER shift's cash sale
/// reduce the drawer.
void main() {
  late Database db;
  late ShiftRepository repository;

  const opening = 10000;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = ShiftRepository(db);
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();

    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    // An earlier, closed shift used as the origin of cross-shift refunds.
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-A',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': opening,
      'status': 'CLOSED',
      'opened_at': now,
    });
    // The current open shift under test.
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-B',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': opening,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': now,
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  Future<Shift> openShiftB() async {
    final row = (await db.query(
      'shifts',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['shift-B'],
    )).single;
    return Shift.fromMap(row);
  }

  Future<void> insertSale({
    required String id,
    required String originShiftId,
    required int total,
    required String status,
    String paymentMethod = 'CASH',
    String? voidedShiftId,
  }) async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    await db.insert('sales', {
      'id': id,
      'receipt_number': 'REC-$id',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'device_id': 'device-1',
      'shift_id': originShiftId,
      'payment_method': paymentMethod,
      'subtotal_minor_units': total,
      'discount_minor_units': 0,
      'total_minor_units': total,
      'amount_paid_minor_units': total,
      'change_minor_units': 0,
      'status': status,
      'voided_shift_id': voidedShiftId,
      'created_at': now,
    });
  }

  test(
    'a same-shift void nets to zero and does not reduce expected cash',
    () async {
      // A completed cash sale of 5000 counted in cash sales.
      await insertSale(
        id: 's1',
        originShiftId: 'shift-B',
        total: 5000,
        status: 'COMPLETED',
      );
      // A cash sale rung up AND voided in shift B: not in cash sales, and must
      // not be subtracted again as a refund.
      await insertSale(
        id: 's2',
        originShiftId: 'shift-B',
        total: 3000,
        status: 'VOIDED',
        voidedShiftId: 'shift-B',
      );

      final summary = await repository.getShiftSummary(await openShiftB());

      expect(summary['cash_sales'], 5000);
      expect(summary['cash_refunds'], 0);
      // opening 10000 + cashSales 5000 - refunds 0 = 15000 (the 3000 void is a
      // pure no-op, exactly as it should be).
      expect(summary['expected_cash'], 15000);
    },
  );

  test('a cross-shift cash refund reduces expected cash', () async {
    // A sale originally rung up in shift A, refunded while shift B is open:
    // real cash leaving drawer B now.
    await insertSale(
      id: 'x1',
      originShiftId: 'shift-A',
      total: 4000,
      status: 'VOIDED',
      voidedShiftId: 'shift-B',
    );

    final summary = await repository.getShiftSummary(await openShiftB());

    expect(summary['cash_refunds'], 4000);
    expect(summary['expected_cash'], opening - 4000);
  });

  test('a non-cash (bank) refund never touches the cash drawer', () async {
    await insertSale(
      id: 'b1',
      originShiftId: 'shift-A',
      total: 4000,
      status: 'VOIDED',
      paymentMethod: 'BANK',
      voidedShiftId: 'shift-B',
    );

    final summary = await repository.getShiftSummary(await openShiftB());

    expect(summary['cash_refunds'], 0);
    expect(summary['expected_cash'], opening);
  });

  test('deposits add and withdrawals subtract from expected cash', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    await db.insert('cash_movements', {
      'id': 'm-in',
      'shift_id': 'shift-B',
      'user_id': 'owner-1',
      'type': 'DEPOSIT',
      'amount_minor_units': 2000,
      'reason': 'float top-up',
      'created_at': now,
    });
    await db.insert('cash_movements', {
      'id': 'm-out',
      'shift_id': 'shift-B',
      'user_id': 'owner-1',
      'type': 'WITHDRAWAL',
      'amount_minor_units': 1500,
      'reason': 'bank drop',
      'created_at': now,
    });

    final summary = await repository.getShiftSummary(await openShiftB());

    expect(summary['deposits'], 2000);
    expect(summary['withdrawals'], 1500);
    expect(summary['expected_cash'], opening + 2000 - 1500);
  });

  test(
    'closeShift stores the same expected figure as getShiftSummary',
    () async {
      await insertSale(
        id: 'c1',
        originShiftId: 'shift-B',
        total: 5000,
        status: 'COMPLETED',
      );
      await insertSale(
        id: 'c2',
        originShiftId: 'shift-A',
        total: 1200,
        status: 'VOIDED',
        voidedShiftId: 'shift-B',
      );
      final shift = await openShiftB();
      final summary = await repository.getShiftSummary(shift);
      final expected = summary['expected_cash'];

      await repository.closeShift(shift, expected!);

      final stored = (await db.query(
        'shifts',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: ['shift-B'],
      )).single;
      expect(stored['expected_cash_minor_units'], expected);
      expect(stored['status'], 'CLOSED');
    },
  );
}

==================================================
FILE: app\test\core\repositories\shift_history_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/shift_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/utils/date_range.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late ShiftRepository repository;

  final now = DateTime.now();
  final todayOpen = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 9);
  final yesterdayOpen = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day - 1, 9);
  final twoDaysAgoOpen = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day - 2, 9);

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = ShiftRepository(db);
    final stamp = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();

    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': stamp,
    });
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'cashier-1',
      'username': 'meron',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'CASHIER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': stamp,
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });

    await _insertShift(
      db,
      id: 'two-days-ago',
      status: 'CLOSED',
      openedAt: twoDaysAgoOpen,
      closedAt: twoDaysAgoOpen.add(const Duration(hours: 10)),
      expected: 10000,
      closing: 9500, // short by 500
      cashierId: 'owner-1',
    );
    await _insertShift(
      db,
      id: 'yesterday',
      status: 'CLOSED',
      openedAt: yesterdayOpen,
      closedAt: yesterdayOpen.add(const Duration(hours: 10)),
      expected: 5000,
      closing: 5000, // balanced
      cashierId: 'cashier-1',
    );
    await _insertShift(
      db,
      id: 'today',
      status: 'OPEN',
      openedAt: todayOpen,
      cashierId: 'owner-1',
    );
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  test('returns all shifts newest-first with the cashier name', () async {
    final shifts = await repository.getShiftHistory();

    expect(shifts.map((s) => s.id).toList(), [
      'today',
      'yesterday',
      'two-days-ago',
    ]);
    final yesterday = shifts.firstWhere((s) => s.id == 'yesterday');
    expect(yesterday.cashierName, 'meron');
  });

  test('filters by a single day (today) by opened date', () async {
    final shifts = await repository.getShiftHistory(period: DateRange.today());
    expect(shifts.map((s) => s.id).toList(), ['today']);
  });

  test('filters by yesterday', () async {
    final shifts = await repository.getShiftHistory(
      period: DateRange.yesterday(),
    );
    expect(shifts.map((s) => s.id).toList(), ['yesterday']);
  });

  test('status filter separates open and closed shifts', () async {
    final open = await repository.getShiftHistory(status: 'OPEN');
    expect(open.map((s) => s.id).toList(), ['today']);

    final closed = await repository.getShiftHistory(status: 'CLOSED');
    expect(closed.map((s) => s.id).toSet(), {'yesterday', 'two-days-ago'});
  });

  test('combined date + status filter', () async {
    // Yesterday restricted to CLOSED shifts is exactly one row.
    final shifts = await repository.getShiftHistory(
      period: DateRange.yesterday(),
      status: 'CLOSED',
    );
    expect(shifts.single.id, 'yesterday');
  });

  test('exposes the counted-minus-expected discrepancy', () async {
    final shifts = await repository.getShiftHistory(status: 'CLOSED');
    final yesterday = shifts.firstWhere((s) => s.id == 'yesterday');
    final short = shifts.firstWhere((s) => s.id == 'two-days-ago');
    final open = await repository.getShiftHistory(status: 'OPEN');

    expect(yesterday.discrepancy, 0);
    expect(short.discrepancy, -500);
    // An open shift has no closing figure yet.
    expect(open.single.discrepancy, isNull);
  });
}

Future<void> _insertShift(
  Database db, {
  required String id,
  required String status,
  required DateTime openedAt,
  DateTime? closedAt,
  int? expected,
  int? closing,
  required String cashierId,
}) async {
  await db.insert('shifts', {
    'id': id,
    'cashier_id': cashierId,
    'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
    'closing_cash_minor_units': closing,
    'expected_cash_minor_units': expected,
    'status': status,
    'opened_at': openedAt.toIso8601String(),
    'closed_at': closedAt?.toIso8601String(),
  });
}

==================================================
FILE: app\test\core\repositories\user_repository_test.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/user_repository.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late UserRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = UserRepository(db);
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  test('Sets up a first owner with a secure password', () async {
    await repository.createFirstOwner('owner', 'long-strong-password');

    final user = await repository.authenticate('owner', 'long-strong-password');

    expect(user, isNotNull);
    expect(user!.username, 'owner');
    expect(user.role, 'OWNER');
  });

  test('Rejects a wrong owner password and repeat setup', () async {
    await repository.createFirstOwner('owner', 'long-strong-password');

    final user = await repository.authenticate('owner', 'wrongpass');

    expect(user, isNull);
    await expectLater(
      repository.createFirstOwner('another', 'long-strong-password'),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test('Creates a new cashier and lists all users', () async {
    await repository.createFirstOwner('owner', 'long-strong-password');
    await repository.createUser('cashier1', 'password123', 'CASHIER');

    final users = await repository.getAllUsers();

    expect(users.length, 2); // Owner + Cashier
    expect(users.any((u) => u.username == 'cashier1'), isTrue);

    final newUser = await repository.authenticate('cashier1', 'password123');
    expect(newUser, isNotNull);
    expect(newUser!.role, 'CASHIER');
  });

  test('Authenticates and upgrades a legacy password hash', () async {
    const password = 'legacy-password';
    const salt = 'old-static-salt';
    final key = utf8.encode(password);
    var bytes = Hmac(sha256, key).convert(utf8.encode(salt)).bytes;
    for (var i = 0; i < 10000; i++) {
      bytes = Hmac(sha256, key).convert(bytes).bytes;
    }
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'legacy-user',
      'username': 'legacy',
      'password_hash': base64.encode(bytes),
      'salt': salt,
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });

    expect(await repository.authenticate('legacy', password), isNotNull);
    final stored = (await db.query(
      'users',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['legacy-user'],
    )).single;
    expect(stored['password_hash'], startsWith(r'pbkdf2-sha256$v=1$i=210000$'));
    expect(stored['salt'], isNot(salt));
    expect(await repository.authenticate('legacy', password), isNotNull);
    expect(await repository.authenticate('legacy', 'wrong-password'), isNull);
  });

  test('Authenticates usernames case-insensitively', () async {
    await repository.createFirstOwner('OwnerOne', 'long-strong-password');

    expect(
      await repository.authenticate('ownerone', 'long-strong-password'),
      isNotNull,
    );
    expect(
      await repository.authenticate('OWNERONE', 'long-strong-password'),
      isNotNull,
    );
  });

  test('Changes a password and rejects the old one', () async {
    final owner = await repository.createFirstOwner(
      'owner',
      'long-strong-password',
    );

    await repository.changePassword(
      userId: owner.id,
      currentPassword: 'long-strong-password',
      newPassword: 'new-strong-password',
    );

    expect(
      await repository.authenticate('owner', 'long-strong-password'),
      isNull,
    );
    expect(
      await repository.authenticate('owner', 'new-strong-password'),
      isNotNull,
    );
  });

  test('Rejects a wrong current password and invalid roles', () async {
    final owner = await repository.createFirstOwner(
      'owner',
      'long-strong-password',
    );

    await expectLater(
      repository.changePassword(
        userId: owner.id,
        currentPassword: 'not-the-password',
        newPassword: 'new-strong-password',
      ),
      throwsException,
    );
    await expectLater(
      repository.createUser('bad-role', 'password123', 'ADMIN'),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test(
    'Owner can reset a cashier password and deactivate the account',
    () async {
      final owner = await repository.createFirstOwner(
        'owner',
        'long-strong-password',
      );
      await repository.createUser('cashier1', 'password123', 'CASHIER');
      final cashier = (await repository.getAllUsers()).firstWhere(
        (user) => user.username == 'cashier1',
      );

      await repository.resetPassword(
        targetUserId: cashier.id,
        newPassword: 'brand-new-password',
        actingUserId: owner.id,
      );
      expect(
        await repository.authenticate('cashier1', 'brand-new-password'),
        isNotNull,
      );

      await repository.setUserActive(
        targetUserId: cashier.id,
        active: false,
        actingUserId: owner.id,
      );
      expect(
        await repository.authenticate('cashier1', 'brand-new-password'),
        isNull,
      );

      await expectLater(
        repository.setUserActive(
          targetUserId: owner.id,
          active: false,
          actingUserId: owner.id,
        ),
        throwsException,
      );
    },
  );
}

FILE: app\test\core\repositories\void_request_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale_item.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/void_request.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late SaleRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = SaleRepository(db);

    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'cashier-1',
      'username': 'cashier',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'CASHIER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  Future<void> seedSale(String id) async {
    await repository.saveSale(
      Sale(
        id: id,
        receiptNumber: 'REC-$id',
        cashierId: 'cashier-1',
        deviceId: 'device-1',
        shiftId: 'shift-1',
        subtotalMinorUnits: 5000,
        discountMinorUnits: 0,
        totalMinorUnits: 5000,
        amountPaidMinorUnits: 5000,
        changeMinorUnits: 0,
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-$id',
            saleId: id,
            productId: 'MANUAL',
            productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar',
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 5000,
            quantityText: '1',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: 5000,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  test('a cashier can request a void; the sale stays completed', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-req');

    await repository.requestVoid('sale-req', 'cashier-1', 'wrong item scanned');

    // Revenue is untouched until an owner decides.
    final stats = await repository.getDashboardStats();
    expect(stats['revenue'], 5000);

    final sales = await repository.getSales();
    expect(sales.first.status, 'COMPLETED');
    expect(sales.first.hasPendingVoidRequest, isTrue);

    final pending = await repository.getPendingVoidRequests();
    expect(pending.length, 1);
    expect(pending.first.requesterName, 'cashier');
    expect(pending.first.reason, 'wrong item scanned');
    expect(pending.first.status, VoidRequest.statusPending);
    expect(pending.first.sale?.id, 'sale-req');
  });

  test('a duplicate pending request on the same sale is blocked', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-dup');
    await repository.requestVoid('sale-dup', 'cashier-1', 'first');

    await expectLater(
      repository.requestVoid('sale-dup', 'cashier-1', 'second'),
      throwsException,
    );
    final pending = await repository.getPendingVoidRequests();
    expect(pending.length, 1);
  });

  test('a void request requires a reason', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-noreason');
    await expectLater(
      repository.requestVoid('sale-noreason', 'cashier-1', '   '),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test('a request cannot be filed on an already-voided sale', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-voided');
    await repository.voidSale('sale-voided', 'owner-1', 'mistake');

    await expectLater(
      repository.requestVoid('sale-voided', 'cashier-1', 'again'),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test('a non-owner cannot approve or reject a request', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-guard');
    await repository.requestVoid('sale-guard', 'cashier-1', 'oops');
    final requestId = (await repository.getPendingVoidRequests()).first.id;

    await expectLater(
      repository.approveVoidRequest(requestId, 'cashier-1'),
      throwsException,
    );
    await expectLater(
      repository.rejectVoidRequest(requestId, 'cashier-1'),
      throwsException,
    );

    // Still pending, sale untouched.
    final pending = await repository.getPendingVoidRequests();
    expect(pending.length, 1);
    final sale = (await repository.getSales()).first;
    expect(sale.status, 'COMPLETED');
  });

  test('owner approval voids the sale and removes it from revenue', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-approve');
    await repository.requestVoid('sale-approve', 'cashier-1', 'double rung');
    final requestId = (await repository.getPendingVoidRequests()).first.id;

    await repository.approveVoidRequest(requestId, 'owner-1');

    final stats = await repository.getDashboardStats();
    expect(stats['revenue'], 0);
    expect(stats['void_count'], 1);
    expect(stats['pending_void_requests'], 0);

    final sale = (await repository.getSales()).first;
    expect(sale.status, 'VOIDED');
    expect(sale.voidedBy, 'owner-1');
    expect(sale.hasPendingVoidRequest, isFalse);

    expect(await repository.getPendingVoidRequests(), isEmpty);
    final requests = await db.query(
      'void_requests',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [requestId],
    );
    expect(requests.single['status'], VoidRequest.statusApproved);
    expect(requests.single['decided_by'], 'owner-1');
  });

  test('owner rejection leaves the sale completed', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-reject');
    await repository.requestVoid('sale-reject', 'cashier-1', 'thought wrong');
    final requestId = (await repository.getPendingVoidRequests()).first.id;

    await repository.rejectVoidRequest(requestId, 'owner-1');

    final stats = await repository.getDashboardStats();
    expect(stats['revenue'], 5000);
    final sale = (await repository.getSales()).first;
    expect(sale.status, 'COMPLETED');
    expect(sale.hasPendingVoidRequest, isFalse);

    final requests = await db.query(
      'void_requests',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [requestId],
    );
    expect(requests.single['status'], VoidRequest.statusRejected);
  });

  test('a decided request cannot be acted on again', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-twice-decide');
    await repository.requestVoid('sale-twice-decide', 'cashier-1', 'oops');
    final requestId = (await repository.getPendingVoidRequests()).first.id;
    await repository.rejectVoidRequest(requestId, 'owner-1');

    await expectLater(
      repository.approveVoidRequest(requestId, 'owner-1'),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test('a direct owner void auto-closes any pending request', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-direct-void');
    await repository.requestVoid('sale-direct-void', 'cashier-1', 'oops');

    await repository.voidSale('sale-direct-void', 'owner-1', 'mistake');

    expect(await repository.getPendingVoidRequests(), isEmpty);
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\repositories\void_sale_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/repositories/sale_repository.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/sale_item.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';

void main() {
  late Database db;
  late SaleRepository repository;

  setUp(() async {
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    repository = SaleRepository(db);

    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'cashier-1',
      'username': 'cashier',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'CASHIER',
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
    await db.insert('devices', {
      'id': 'device-1',
      'name': 'PC',
      'platform': 'Windows',
    });
    await db.insert('shifts', {
      'id': 'shift-1',
      'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
      'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
      'status': 'OPEN',
      'opened_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
  });

  Future<void> seedSale(String id) async {
    await repository.saveSale(
      Sale(
        id: id,
        receiptNumber: 'REC-$id',
        cashierId: 'owner-1',
        deviceId: 'device-1',
        shiftId: 'shift-1',
        subtotalMinorUnits: 5000,
        discountMinorUnits: 0,
        totalMinorUnits: 5000,
        amountPaidMinorUnits: 5000,
        changeMinorUnits: 0,
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        items: [
          SaleItem(
            id: 'item-$id',
            saleId: id,
            productId: 'MANUAL',
            productNameSnapshot: 'Sugar',
            unitPriceSnapshotMinorUnits: 5000,
            quantityText: '1',
            billingQuantityMilliunits: 1000,
            lineTotalMinorUnits: 5000,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  test(
    'Voiding a sale excludes it from revenue but keeps the record',
    () async {
      await seedSale('sale-to-void');

      final before = await repository.getDashboardStats();
      expect(before['revenue'], 5000);
      expect(before['sales_count'], 1);

      await repository.voidSale('sale-to-void', 'owner-1', 'mistake');

      final after = await repository.getDashboardStats();
      expect(after['revenue'], 0);
      expect(after['sales_count'], 0);
      expect(after['average_sale'], 0);

      // History keeps the record forever; nothing is deleted.
      final sales = await repository.getSales();
      expect(sales.length, 1);
      expect(sales.first.status, 'VOIDED');
      expect(sales.first.voidReason, 'mistake');
      expect(sales.first.voidedBy, 'owner-1');

      final logs = await repository.getAuditLogs();
      expect(logs.length, 2);
      expect(logs.first['action'], 'SALE_VOIDED');
      // The audit detail references the amount, not a receipt number.
      expect(logs.first['details'].contains('50.00 Br'), isTrue);
      expect(logs.first['details'].contains('REC-'), isFalse);
    },
  );

  test('Voiding the same sale twice is rejected', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-twice');
    await repository.voidSale('sale-twice', 'owner-1', 'mistake');

    await expectLater(
      repository.voidSale('sale-twice', 'owner-1', 'other reason'),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test('A cashier cannot void sales', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-cashier');

    await expectLater(
      repository.voidSale('sale-cashier', 'cashier-1', 'cashier attempt'),
      throwsException,
    );

    final sales = await repository.getSales();
    expect(sales.first.status, 'COMPLETED');
  });

  test('A void reason is required', () async {
    await seedSale('sale-noreason');

    await expectLater(
      repository.voidSale('sale-noreason', 'owner-1', '   '),
      throwsException,
    );
  });
}

==================================================
FILE: app\test\core\utils\data_importer_test.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
// ignore: depend_on_referenced_packages
import 'package:path_provider_platform_interface/path_provider_platform_interface.dart';
import 'package:sqflite_common_ffi/sqflite_ffi.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/database/local_database.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/utils/data_importer.dart';

/// Backups written by schema 6 stored sale-item quantities as plain
/// integers. The importer must accept them and normalize rows to the
/// schema 7 shape instead of rejecting the whole restore.
class _FakePathProvider extends PathProviderPlatform {
  _FakePathProvider(this.documentsPath);

  final String documentsPath;

  @override
  Future<String?> getApplicationDocumentsPath() async => documentsPath;
}

void main() {
  late Directory tempDir;
  late Database db;

  setUp(() async {
    tempDir = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('wholesale_pos_import_');
    PathProviderPlatform.instance = _FakePathProvider(tempDir.path);
    db = await LocalDatabase.openInMemory();
    await db.insert('users', {
      'id': 'owner-1',
      'username': 'owner',
      'password_hash': 'x',
      'salt': 'x',
      'role': 'OWNER',
      'is_active': 1,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  });

  tearDown(() async {
    await db.close();
    try {
      await tempDir.delete(recursive: true);
    } catch (_) {
      // Windows occasionally holds a handle a moment longer.
    }
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> buildBackup({required int version}) {
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    final saleItem = <String, dynamic>{
      'id': 'item-1',
      'sale_id': 'sale-1',
      'product_id': 'prod-1',
      'product_name_snapshot': 'Sugar',
      'unit_price_snapshot_minor_units': 2000,
      'line_discount_minor_units': 0,
      'line_total_minor_units': 6000,
    };
    if (version < 7) {
      saleItem['quantity'] = 3; // legacy integer column
    } else {
      saleItem['quantity_text'] = '3 bags';
      saleItem['billing_quantity_milliunits'] = null;
      saleItem['notes'] = 'haggled price';
    }
    return {
      'exported_at': now,
      'version': version,
      'users': [
        {
          'id': 'owner-1',
          'username': 'owner',
          'password_hash': 'x',
          'salt': 'x',
          'role': 'OWNER',
          'is_active': 1,
          'created_at': now,
        },
      ],
      'products': <dynamic>[],
      'sales': [
        {
          'id': 'sale-1',
          'receipt_number': 'REC-001',
          'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
          'device_id': 'device-1',
          'shift_id': 'shift-1',
          'payment_method': 'CASH',
          'subtotal_minor_units': 6000,
          'discount_minor_units': 0,
          'total_minor_units': 6000,
          'amount_paid_minor_units': 6000,
          'change_minor_units': 0,
          'status': 'COMPLETED',
          'created_at': now,
        },
      ],
      'sale_items': [saleItem],
      'shifts': [
        {
          'id': 'shift-1',
          'cashier_id': 'owner-1',
          'opening_cash_minor_units': 0,
          'status': 'CLOSED',
          'opened_at': now,
        },
      ],
      'cash_movements': <dynamic>[],
      'audit_logs': [
        {
          'id': 'log-1',
          'user_id': 'owner-1',
          'action': 'SALE_COMPLETED',
          'created_at': now,
        },
      ],
      'devices': [
        {'id': 'device-1', 'name': 'PC', 'platform': 'Windows'},
      ],
      'counters': [
        {'name': 'receipt-20261022', 'value': 1},
      ],
      'settings': [
        {'key': 'theme_mode', 'value': 'light'},
      ],
    };
  }

  Future<String> writeBackup(Map<String, dynamic> data) async {
    final path = p.join(tempDir.path, 'backup.json');
    await File(path).writeAsString(jsonEncode(data));
    return path;
  }

  test(
    'imports a v6 backup and normalizes legacy integer quantities',
    () async {
      final path = await writeBackup(buildBackup(version: 6));

      await DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1');

      final item = (await db.query(
        'sale_items',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: ['item-1'],
      )).single;
      expect(item['quantity_text'], '3');
      expect(item['billing_quantity_milliunits'], 3000);

      final sale = (await db.query(
        'sales',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: ['sale-1'],
      )).single;
      expect(sale['total_minor_units'], 6000);

      // V6 backups simply start with no finance records.
      expect(await db.query('finance_categories'), isEmpty);
      expect(await db.query('finance_entries'), isEmpty);
    },
  );

  test('imports a v7 backup with flexible quantities unchanged', () async {
    final path = await writeBackup(buildBackup(version: 7));

    await DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1');

    final item = (await db.query(
      'sale_items',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: ['item-1'],
    )).single;
    expect(item['quantity_text'], '3 bags');
    expect(item['billing_quantity_milliunits'], isNull);
    expect(item['notes'], 'haggled price');
  });

  test('rejects backups older than v6 instead of guessing', () async {
    final path = await writeBackup(buildBackup(version: 5));

    await expectLater(
      DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1'),
      throwsException,
    );
    // The original data is untouched.
    expect(await db.query('sales'), isEmpty);
  });

  test('restores finance tables from a v7 backup', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    final data = buildBackup(version: 7)
      ..['finance_categories'] = [
        {'id': 'cat-1', 'name': 'Alem', 'created_at': now, 'updated_at': now},
      ]
      ..['finance_entries'] = [
        {
          'id': 'entry-1',
          'category_id': 'cat-1',
          'source': 'CBE Birr',
          'amount_minor_units': 500000,
          'description': null,
          'status': 'PENDING',
          'entry_date': now,
          'created_at': now,
          'created_by': 'owner-1',
          'completed_at': null,
          'completed_by': null,
          'updated_at': now,
        },
      ];
    final path = await writeBackup(data);

    await DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1');

    expect((await db.query('finance_categories')).length, 1);
    final entry = (await db.query('finance_entries')).single;
    expect(entry['source'], 'CBE Birr');
    expect(entry['status'], 'PENDING');
  });

  test('a cashier cannot restore a backup', () async {
    final path = await writeBackup(buildBackup(version: 7));

    await expectLater(
      DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'cashier-9'),
      throwsException,
    );
  });

  test('restores void_requests from a v8 backup', () async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    final data = buildBackup(version: 8)
      ..['void_requests'] = [
        {
          'id': 'vr-1',
          'sale_id': 'sale-1',
          'requested_by': 'owner-1',
          'reason': 'wrong price',
          'status': 'PENDING',
          'created_at': now,
          'decided_by': null,
          'decided_at': null,
        },
      ];
    final path = await writeBackup(data);

    await DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1');

    final request = (await db.query('void_requests')).single;
    expect(request['sale_id'], 'sale-1');
    expect(request['status'], 'PENDING');
    expect(request['reason'], 'wrong price');
  });

  test('v6 and v7 backups restore with an empty void_requests table', () async {
    // void_requests was introduced in schema 8; older backups omit it.
    final path = await writeBackup(buildBackup(version: 7));

    await DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1');

    expect(await db.query('void_requests'), isEmpty);
  });

  test('rejects a malformed void_requests record', () async {
    final data = buildBackup(version: 8)
      ..['void_requests'] = [
        {
          // Missing reason and status.
          'id': 'vr-bad',
          'sale_id': 'sale-1',
          'requested_by': 'owner-1',
          'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        },
      ];
    final path = await writeBackup(data);

    await expectLater(
      DataImporter.importFromJson(db, path, requestingUserId: 'owner-1'),
      throwsException,
    );
    // Original data untouched.
    expect(await db.query('sales'), isEmpty);
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\utils\date_range_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/utils/date_range.dart';

void main() {
  group('DateRange.today', () {
    test('spans local midnight to midnight', () {
      final now = DateTime.now();
      final range = DateRange.today();

      expect(range.start, DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day));
      expect(
        range.end,
        DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day).add(const Duration(days: 1)),
      );
      expect(range.contains(now), isTrue);
    });

    test('does not include yesterday or tomorrow', () {
      final range = DateRange.today();
      expect(
        range.contains(range.start.subtract(const Duration(hours: 1))),
        isFalse,
      );
      expect(range.contains(range.start.add(const Duration(days: 1))), isFalse);
    });
  });

  test('DateRange.yesterday covers the previous calendar day', () {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final range = DateRange.yesterday();

    expect(range.contains(DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 12)), isFalse);
    expect(
      range.contains(
        DateTime(
          now.year,
          now.month,
          now.day,
        ).subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
      ),
      isTrue,
    );
  });

  test('DateRange.thisWeek starts on Monday', () {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final range = DateRange.thisWeek();

    expect(range.start.weekday, DateTime.monday);
    expect(range.end.difference(range.start), const Duration(days: 7));
    expect(range.contains(now), isTrue);
  });

  test('DateRange.thisMonth spans the whole local month', () {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final range = DateRange.thisMonth();

    expect(range.start, DateTime(now.year, now.month, 1));
    expect(range.contains(now), isTrue);
    expect(
      range.contains(range.start.subtract(const Duration(milliseconds: 1))),
      isFalse,
    );
  });

  test('DateRange.thisYear spans January to January', () {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final range = DateRange.thisYear();

    expect(range.start, DateTime(now.year, 1, 1));
    expect(range.end, DateTime(now.year + 1, 1, 1));
  });

  group('DateRange.custom', () {
    test('includes both picked calendar days', () {
      final range = DateRange.custom(
        DateTime(2026, 10, 20),
        DateTime(2026, 10, 22),
      );

      expect(range.start, DateTime(2026, 10, 20));
      expect(range.end, DateTime(2026, 10, 23)); // exclusive boundary
      expect(range.contains(DateTime(2026, 10, 20, 0, 0)), isTrue);
      expect(range.contains(DateTime(2026, 10, 22, 23, 59, 59)), isTrue);
      expect(range.contains(DateTime(2026, 10, 23, 0, 0)), isFalse);
    });

    test('clamps a reversed range to a single day', () {
      final range = DateRange.custom(
        DateTime(2026, 10, 22),
        DateTime(2026, 10, 20),
      );

      expect(range.start, DateTime(2026, 10, 22));
      expect(range.end, DateTime(2026, 10, 23));
    });
  });

  test('ReportPeriod.custom resolves to null; presets resolve to ranges', () {
    expect(ReportPeriod.custom.toDateRange(), isNull);
    expect(ReportPeriod.today.toDateRange(), isNotNull);
    expect(
      ReportPeriod.allTime.toDateRange()!.contains(DateTime(2025, 6, 1)),
      isTrue,
    );
  });
}

FILE: app\test\core\utils\money_utils_test.dart

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/core/utils/money_utils.dart';

void main() {
  group('stringToMinorUnits', () {
    test('parses exact whole and fractional amounts', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('0'), 0);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('12'), 1200);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('12.3'), 1230);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('12.34'), 1234);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(' 1,234.50 '), 123450);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('00012.05'), 1205);
    });

    test('rejects invalid, negative, and over-precise input', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits(''), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('   '), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('abc'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('-1'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('1.234'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('.5'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('NaN'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('Infinity'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMinorUnits('90000000000001'), isNull);
    });
  });

  group('minorUnitsToString', () {
    test('formats without floating point drift', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(0), '0.00');
      expect(MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(5), '0.05');
      expect(MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(100), '1.00');
      expect(MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(123456), '1234.56');
      expect(MoneyUtils.minorUnitsToString(-12345), '-123.45');
    });
  });

  group('formatBr', () {
    test('formats amounts as birr with thousands grouping', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.formatBr(0), '0.00 Br');
      expect(MoneyUtils.formatBr(5), '0.05 Br');
      expect(MoneyUtils.formatBr(125050), '1,250.50 Br');
      expect(MoneyUtils.formatBr(123456789), '1,234,567.89 Br');
      expect(MoneyUtils.formatBr(-125050), '-1,250.50 Br');
    });
  });

  group('stringToMilliunits', () {
    test('parses plain decimal quantities with up to three places', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('2'), 2000);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('1.5'), 1500);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('0.25'), 250);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('1.250'), 1250);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('1,000'), 1000000);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits(' 2 '), 2000);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('0002'), 2000);
    });

    test('rejects descriptive or invalid text instead of guessing', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits(''), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('2 boxes and 1 piece'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('1 kunital'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('1.2345'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('-1'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('.5'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('9000001'), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.stringToMilliunits('NaN'), isNull);
    });
  });

  group('milliunitsToString', () {
    test('drops trailing zeros', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.milliunitsToString(2000), '2');
      expect(MoneyUtils.milliunitsToString(1500), '1.5');
      expect(MoneyUtils.milliunitsToString(250), '0.25');
      expect(MoneyUtils.milliunitsToString(1230), '1.23');
      expect(MoneyUtils.milliunitsToString(-1500), '-1.5');
    });
  });

  group('tryLineTotalMinorUnits', () {
    test('multiplies exactly with round-half-up integer math', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(500, 1500), 750);
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(5000, 1000), 5000);
      // 333 x 0.111 = 36.963 -> 37.
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(333, 111), 37);
      // 1005 x 0.1 = 100.5 -> 101 (ties always round up).
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(1005, 100), 101);
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(500, 0), 0);
    });

    test('returns null for negative inputs or overflow', () {
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(-1, 1000), isNull);
      expect(MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(100, -1000), isNull);
      // 9e14 x 9e6 overflows a 64-bit integer.
      expect(
        MoneyUtils.tryLineTotalMinorUnits(900000000000000, 9000000),
        isNull,
      );
    });
  });
}

FILE: app\test\features\sales\cart_test.dart
==================================================

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/cart.dart';
import 'package:wholesale_pos_app/features/sales/cart_item.dart';

void main() {
  CartItem numericLine({
    required String id,
    required String name,
    required int unitPriceMinorUnits,
    required int quantityMilliunits,
    String quantityText = '',
  }) {
    return CartItem(
      id: id,
      name: name,
      unitPriceMinorUnits: unitPriceMinorUnits,
      quantityText: quantityText.isEmpty ? '$quantityMilliunits' : quantityText,
      billingQuantityMilliunits: quantityMilliunits,
    );
  }

  test('Empty cart totals to zero', () {
    final cart = Cart();
    expect(cart.subtotalMinorUnits, 0);
    expect(cart.totalMinorUnits, 0);
  });

  test('Numeric quantity lines calculate exact integer totals', () {
    final cart = Cart();
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Flour',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 450000,
        quantityMilliunits: 2000,
        quantityText: '2',
      ),
    ); // 9000.00
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Sugar',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 12000,
        quantityMilliunits: 5000,
        quantityText: '5',
      ),
    ); // 600.00

    expect(cart.subtotalMinorUnits, 960000); // 9600.00 Br
    expect(cart.totalMinorUnits, 960000);
  });

  test('Fractional billing quantities round half-up exactly', () {
    // 500 minor units x 1.5 = 750 exactly.
    expect(
      numericLine(
        id: 'a',
        name: 'Oil',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 500,
        quantityMilliunits: 1500,
        quantityText: '1.5',
      ).lineTotalMinorUnits,
      750,
    );
    // 333 x 0.111 = 36.963 -> 37 (round half up via integer math).
    expect(
      numericLine(
        id: 'b',
        name: 'Salt',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 333,
        quantityMilliunits: 111,
        quantityText: '0.111',
      ).lineTotalMinorUnits,
      37,
    );
    // 1005 x 0.1 = 100.5 -> 101 (ties round up, never banker's rounding).
    expect(
      numericLine(
        id: 'c',
        name: 'Rice',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 1005,
        quantityMilliunits: 100,
        quantityText: '0.1',
      ).lineTotalMinorUnits,
      101,
    );
  });

  test('Manual line totals keep descriptive quantities as text', () {
    final cart = Cart();
    // "2 boxes and 1 piece" is a description, never a number. The cashier
    // entered the line amount manually.
    cart.addItem(
      CartItem(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Berbere',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 25000,
        quantityText: '2 boxes and 1 piece',
        manualLineTotalMinorUnits: 137500,
      ),
    );

    expect(cart.items.first.usesManualLineTotal, isTrue);
    expect(cart.items.first.lineTotalMinorUnits, 137500);
    expect(cart.subtotalMinorUnits, 137500);
  });

  test('Identical descriptions stay as separate lines (no merging)', () {
    final cart = Cart();
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Flour',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 450000,
        quantityMilliunits: 1000,
        quantityText: '1',
      ),
    );
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Flour',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 460000,
        quantityMilliunits: 1000,
        quantityText: '1',
      ),
    );

    // Each entered line is kept exactly as the cashier described it.
    expect(cart.items.length, 2);
    expect(cart.items.first.unitPriceMinorUnits, 450000);
    expect(cart.items.last.unitPriceMinorUnits, 460000);
    expect(cart.subtotalMinorUnits, 910000);
  });

  test('replaceItem edits a line in place', () {
    final cart = Cart();
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Flour',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 450000,
        quantityMilliunits: 1000,
        quantityText: '1',
      ),
    );
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Sugar',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 12000,
        quantityMilliunits: 2000,
        quantityText: '2',
      ),
    );

    cart.replaceItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Sugar',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 13000,
        quantityMilliunits: 3000,
        quantityText: '3',
      ),
    );

    expect(cart.items.length, 2);
    expect(cart.items.last.quantityText, '3');
    expect(cart.items.last.lineTotalMinorUnits, 39000);
  });

  test('removeItem deletes only the targeted line', () {
    final cart = Cart();
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Flour',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 450000,
        quantityMilliunits: 1000,
        quantityText: '1',
      ),
    );
    cart.addItem(
      numericLine(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Sugar',
        unitPriceMinorUnits: 12000,
        quantityMilliunits: 2000,
        quantityText: '2',
      ),
    );

    cart.removeItem('1');

    expect(cart.items.length, 1);
    expect(cart.items.first.name, 'Sugar');
  });

  test('A line without quantity or manual total is a programming error', () {
    final item = CartItem(
      id: '1',
      name: 'Flour',
      unitPriceMinorUnits: 450000,
      quantityText: 'later',
    );

    expect(() => item.lineTotalMinorUnits, throwsStateError);
  });
}

FILE: app\tools\generate_license.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:cryptography/cryptography.dart';

Future<void> main(List<String> args) async {
  if (args.length != 3) {
    stderr.writeln(
      'Usage: dart run tools/generate_license.dart <hardware-id-or-> <private-key-path> <output-license-path>',
    );
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }

  final hardwareId = args[0];
  final privateKeyFile = File(args[1]).absolute;
  final outputFile = File(args[2]).absolute;

  if (!await privateKeyFile.exists()) {
    stderr.writeln('Private signing key does not exist.');
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }
  if (await outputFile.exists()) {
    stderr.writeln('Output license already exists; refusing to overwrite.');
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }

  try {
    final seed = base64Url.decode(
      base64Url.normalize((await privateKeyFile.readAsString()).trim()),
    );
    if (seed.length != 32) throw const FormatException();
    final keyPair = await Ed25519().newKeyPairFromSeed(seed);

    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    final expiresAt = DateTime.utc(
      now.year + 1,
      now.month,
      now.day,
      now.hour,
      now.minute,
      now.second,
    );

    final payload = <String, Object?>{
      'format': 'wholesale-pos-license',
      'version': 1,
      'product': 'wholesale_pos',
      'entitlements': ['pos'],
      if (hardwareId != '-') 'hardware_id': hardwareId,
      'issued_at': now.toIso8601String(),
      'expires_at': expiresAt.toIso8601String(),
    };

    final payloadBytes = utf8.encode(jsonEncode(_canonicalize(payload)));
    final signature = await Ed25519().sign(payloadBytes, keyPair: keyPair);
    final contents =
        '${base64Url.encode(payloadBytes)}.${base64Url.encode(signature.bytes)}';

    await outputFile.parent.create(recursive: true);
    final handle = await outputFile.open(mode: FileMode.write);
    try {
      await handle.writeString(contents);
      await handle.flush();
    } finally {
      await handle.close();
    }
    stdout.writeln(
      'License written to ${outputFile.path}. Expires at ${expiresAt.toIso8601String()}.',
    );
  } catch (error) {
    stderr.writeln('License generation failed: ${error.runtimeType}.');
    exitCode = 1;
  }
}

Object? _canonicalize(Object? value) {
  if (value is Map) {
    final keys = value.keys.cast<String>().toList()..sort();
    return {for (final key in keys) key: _canonicalize(value[key])};
  }
  if (value is List) return value.map(_canonicalize).toList();
  return value;
}

==================================================
FILE: app\tools\print_public_key.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:cryptography/cryptography.dart';

Future<void> main(List<String> args) async {
  if (args.isEmpty) {
    stderr.writeln(
      'Usage: dart run tools/print_public_key.dart <seed-path> [license-path]',
    );
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }
  final seed = base64Url.decode(
    base64Url.normalize((await File(args[0]).readAsString()).trim()),
  );
  if (seed.length != 32) {
    stderr.writeln('Seed must be 32 bytes.');
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }
  final keyPair = await Ed25519().newKeyPairFromSeed(seed);
  final publicKey = await keyPair.extractPublicKey();
  final hex = publicKey.bytes
      .map((byte) => byte.toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0'))
      .join();
  stdout.writeln('PUBLIC_KEY=$hex');

  if (args.length > 1) {
    final parts = (await File(args[1]).readAsString()).trim().split('.');
    final payloadBytes = base64Url.decode(base64Url.normalize(parts[0]));
    final signatureBytes = base64Url.decode(base64Url.normalize(parts[1]));
    final valid = await Ed25519().verify(
      payloadBytes,
      signature: Signature(signatureBytes, publicKey: publicKey),
    );
    stdout.writeln('LICENSE_VALID=$valid');
    stdout.writeln(utf8.decode(payloadBytes));
  }
}

==================================================
FILE: app\tools\setup_keys.dart
==================================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:cryptography/cryptography.dart';

Future<void> main(List<String> args) async {
  if (args.length != 1) {
    stderr.writeln(
      'Usage: dart run tools/setup_keys.dart <private-key-path-outside-project>',
    );
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }
  final project = Directory.current.resolveSymbolicLinksSync();
  final destination = File(args.single).absolute;
  final parent = await destination.parent.create(recursive: true);
  final resolvedParent = await parent.resolveSymbolicLinks();

  if (resolvedParent == project ||
      resolvedParent.startsWith('$project${Platform.pathSeparator}')) {
    stderr.writeln('Refusing to store private key inside the project.');
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }
  if (await destination.exists()) {
    stderr.writeln(
      'Private-key destination already exists; refusing to overwrite.',
    );
    exitCode = 2;
    return;
  }

  try {
    final pair = await Ed25519().newKeyPair();
    final privateBytes = await pair.extractPrivateKeyBytes();
    final publicKey = await pair.extractPublicKey();
    final publicHex = publicKey.bytes
        .map((byte) => byte.toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0'))
        .join();

    await destination.create(exclusive: true);
    final keyHandle = await destination.open(mode: FileMode.writeOnly);
    try {
      await keyHandle.writeString(base64Url.encode(privateBytes));
      await keyHandle.flush();
    } finally {
      await keyHandle.close();
    }

    stdout.writeln('Private signing key written to ${destination.path}.');
    stdout.writeln('Back it up now; it is never written into the project.');
    stdout.writeln('');
    stdout.writeln('Public verification key (hex):');
    stdout.writeln(publicHex);
    stdout.writeln('');
    stdout.writeln('Build the licensed application with:');
    stdout.writeln(
      'flutter build windows --release '
      '--dart-define=WHOLESALE_POS_LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY=$publicHex',
    );
  } catch (error) {
    stderr.writeln('Key setup failed: ${error.runtimeType}.');
    exitCode = 1;
  }
}

FILE: docs\DATABASE.md
